Result: 1. 分词器效率差异高达1.6倍（Qwen3相较Llama系列多消耗60% token）；2. NVIDIA Nemotron Super 3表现最佳（综合分83.1），在仅三分之一API成本的情况下超越了规模大得多的Mistral Large 3；3. 少样本提示会导致性能退化（最高下降26个百分点），提示了在该语境下少样本学习并非总是有效。

Conclusion: 在处理乌克兰语等形态丰富的语言时，模型选择应优先考虑分词器效率；与少样本（few-shot）提示相比，零样本（zero-shot）提示在当前场景下是更可靠的默认策略。

Abstract: Foundation models tokenize Ukrainian legal text with vastly different efficiency, yet no systematic comparison exists for this domain. We benchmark seven models from five providers on 273 validated court decisions from Ukraine's state registry (EDRSR), measuring tokenizer fertility and zero-shot performance on three tasks. Three findings emerge. (1) Tokenizer fertility varies 1.6x: Qwen3 models consume 60% more tokens than Llama-family models on identical input, directly reducing API cost. (2) NVIDIA Nemotron Super 3 (120B) achieves the highest composite score (83.1), outperforming Mistral Large 3 (675B total, 41B active) -- a model with 5.6x more total parameters and 3.4x more active parameters per token -- at one-third the API cost. (3) Few-shot prompting degrades performance by up to 26 percentage points; stratified and prompt-sensitivity ablations confirm this is intrinsic to Ukrainian-language demonstrations, not an artifact of example selection. For practitioners: tokenizer analysis should precede model selection, and zero-shot is a more reliable default than few-shot for morphologically rich languages.

</details>


### [36] [Chain-of-Procedure: Hierarchical Visual-Language Reasoning for Procedural QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14928)
*Guanhua Chen,Yutong Yao,Shenghe Sun,Ci-Jun Gao,Shudong Liu,Lidia S. Chao,Feng Wan,Derek F. Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对视觉程序问答（VP-QA）挑战，本文提出了 ProcedureVQA 基准和 Chain-of-Procedure (CoP) 推理框架，通过分层处理有效解决了 VLM 在程序理解与步骤细化方面的缺陷，性能提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型（VLMs）在处理视觉程序问答（VP-QA）时存在局限性，具体表现为：跨模态检索结构化程序的能力不足，以及图像序列粒度与文本步骤分解之间的不匹配。

Method: 提出了名为“Chain-of-Procedure (CoP)”的层级推理框架。包含三个关键阶段：1. 利用视觉线索检索相关指令；2. 通过语义分解进行步骤细化；3. 生成下一步动作。

Result: 在六个主流视觉语言模型上进行实验，结果显示该方法相比标准基准模型达到了最高 13% 的绝对性能提升。

Conclusion: CoP 框架通过分层推理逻辑，有效弥补了现有视觉语言模型在处理复杂程序性任务时的推理不足，显著提升了视觉程序问答（VP-QA）的准确性。

Abstract: Recent advances in vision-language models (VLMs) have achieved impressive results on standard image-text tasks, yet their potential for visual procedure question answering (VP-QA) remains largely unexplored. VP-QA presents unique challenges where users query next-step actions by uploading images for intermediate states of complex procedures. To systematically evaluate VLMs on this practical task, we propose ProcedureVQA, a novel multimodal benchmark specifically designed for visual procedural reasoning. Through comprehensive analysis, we identify two critical limitations in current VLMs: inadequate cross-modal retrieval of structured procedures given visual states, and misalignment between image sequence granularity and textual step decomposition. To address these issues, we present Chain-of-Procedure (CoP), a hierarchical reasoning framework that first retrieves relevant instructions using visual cues, then performs step refinement through semantic decomposition, and finally generates the next step. Experiments across six VLMs demonstrate CoP's effectiveness, achieving up to 13% absolute improvement over standard baselines.

</details>


### [37] [Performance-Driven Policy Optimization for Speculative Decoding with Adaptive Windowing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14978)
*Jie Jiang,Xing Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PPOW框架，将推测解码的草稿模型优化从逐个标记的模仿学习转向窗口级的强化学习，有效解决了推测解码中的窗口截断瓶颈，显著提升了推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推测解码效率受制于“难以预测位置”导致窗口截断，而传统的学习型草稿模型多采用标记级监督学习，忽略了推测解码在窗口级和前缀敏感性上的本质特征。

Method: 提出了PPOW（Performance-Driven Policy Optimization with Adaptive Windowing）强化学习框架：1. 引入成本感知加速奖励；2. 引入基于分布的邻近度奖励；3. 实施自适应发散感知窗口化（Adaptive Divergence-Aware Windowing），优先处理高价值、高发散性的窗口。

Result: 在多个模型系列和基准测试中，PPOW实现了6.29-6.52的平均接受长度，以及3.39-4.36倍的加速，显著优于传统的训练方法。

Conclusion: 基于性能驱动的窗口级优化是提升推测解码效率的实用且有效的方法。通过强化学习直接优化推测窗口的整体性能，能显著改善传统逐个标记训练在处理“难以预测位置”时的不足。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference by having a lightweight draft model propose speculative windows of candidate tokens for parallel verification by a larger target model. In practice, speculative efficiency is often bottlenecked by hard-to-draft positions, where an early mismatch truncates the accepted prefix and invalidates the rest of the speculative window. Most learning-based drafters are still optimized with token-level supervised objectives, even though speculative utility is inherently window-level and prefix-sensitive. We propose PPOW (Performance-Driven Policy Optimization with Adaptive Windowing), a reinforcement learning framework that shifts drafter optimization from token-level imitation to window-level optimization. PPOW combines a Cost-Aware Speedup Reward, a Distribution-Based Proximity Reward, and Adaptive Divergence-Aware Windowing, which prioritizes informative windows with high confidence-weighted draft-target divergence. PPOW achieves average acceptance lengths of 6.29-6.52 and speedups of 3.39-4.36$\times$ across multiple model families and benchmarks under a unified decoding protocol. These results show that performance-driven window-level optimization is a practical approach to improving speculative decoding efficiency.

</details>


### [38] [Quantifying and Mitigating Premature Closure in Frontier LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15000)
*Rebecca Handler,Suhana Bedi,Nigam Shah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了医疗大模型中普遍存在的“过早闭合”导致的诊断错误风险，测试发现模型在面对不确定性时倾向于给出非必要的回答，即使在无法获取正确答案的情况下仍有高概率输出不当建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: “过早闭合”（即在信息不足时不恰当地得出结论）是医疗诊断错误的重要诱因，但目前针对大模型在此方面表现的研究不足，需要量化评估其在面对不确定性时的行为表现。

Method: 对五种前沿大型语言模型（LLMs）进行测试，涵盖结构化任务（移除正确选项的MedQA和AfriMed-QA，测试模型是否强行给出错误答案）及开放式任务（使用HealthBench基准和医师编写的对抗性查询，评估模型在不确定情况下的行为）。

Result: 模型表现出极高的“过早闭合”倾向：在移除正确选项的结构化测试中，错误动作率高达53-82%；在开放式评估中，针对HealthBench和医师对抗性查询，模型给出不恰当回答的比例分别达到30%和78%。尽管安全提示可减少此类现象，但无法完全消除。

Conclusion: 在大模型医疗应用中，“过早闭合”是一种显著的误导风险。即使通过提示工程（Prompting）可以缓解该问题，但残余失败率依然存在，强调了开发“具备拒绝回答与感知不确定性能力”的医疗大模型至关重要。

Abstract: Premature closure, or committing to a conclusion before sufficient information is available, is a recognized contributor to diagnostic error but remains underexamined in large language models (LLMs). We define LLM premature closure as inappropriate commitment under uncertainty: providing an answer, recommendation, or clinical guidance when the safer response would be clarification, abstention, escalation, or refusal. We evaluated five frontier LLMs across structured and open-ended medical tasks. In MedQA (n = 500) and AfriMed-QA (n = 490) questions where the correct choice had been removed, models still selected an answer at high rates, with baseline false-action rates of 55-81% and 53-82%, respectively. In open-ended evaluation, models gave inappropriate answers on an average of 30% of 861 HealthBench questions and 78% of 191 physician-authored adversarial queries. Safety-oriented prompting reduced premature closure across models, but residual failure persisted, highlighting the need to evaluate whether medical LLMs know when not to answer.

</details>


### [39] [The Scientific Contribution Graph: Automated Literature-based Technological Roadmapping at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15011)
*Peter A. Jansen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了科学贡献图谱（SCG），通过在大规模论文库中提取科学贡献及其前提关系，实现技术路线图的自动化，并验证了模型在预测未来科学发现前提方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发展具有累积性，作者旨在解决如何从现有文献中自动提取科学贡献并建立其前提依赖关系，从而实现自动化技术路线图规划。

Method: 从23万篇论文中提取200万个科学贡献，构建包含1250万个前提依赖关系的“科学贡献图谱”（Scientific Contribution Graph）；并利用时间过滤回测技术对模型进行前提预测性能评估，最高MAP达到0.48。

Result: 成功构建了大规模的科学贡献图谱，并证实了现有的AI/NLP模型在科学前提预测任务上表现出显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过构建科学贡献图谱及定义科学前提预测任务，证明了自动化技术路线图的可行性，并为科学影响评估与自动化科学发现提供了关键数据支撑。

Abstract: Scientific contributions rarely develop in isolation, but instead build upon prior discoveries. We formulate the task of automated technological roadmapping as extracting scientific contributions from scholarly articles and linking them to their prerequisites. We present the Scientific Contribution Graph, a large-scale AI/NLP-domain resource containing 2 million detailed scientific contributions extracted from 230k open-access papers and connected by 12.5 million prerequisite edges. We further introduce scientific prerequisite prediction, a scientific discovery task in which models predict which existing technologies can enable future discoveries, and show that contemporary models are rapidly improving on this task, reaching 0.48 MAP when evaluated using temporally filtered backtesting. We anticipate technological roadmapping resources such as this will support scientific impact assessment and automated scientific discovery.

</details>


### [40] [COTCAgent: Preventive Consultation via Probabilistic Chain-of-Thought Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15016)
*Zihan Deng,Xiaozhen Zhong,Chuanzhi Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对大模型在纵向EHR处理中存在的统计幻觉与长程依赖不足，本文提出了COTCAgent框架，通过解耦统计计算与推理逻辑，显著提升了临床诊断的准确性与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型在纵向电子健康记录（EHR）推理中存在两大缺陷：一是缺乏细粒度统计推理能力，导致量化证据处理出现幻觉；二是难以处理非均匀时间序列和标签稀疏问题，限制了对长程时序依赖的捕捉。

Method: 提出COTCAgent框架，包含三个模块：1. Temporal-Statistics Adapter (TSA)：将分析计划转化为可执行代码以获取标准化趋势；2. Chain-of-Thought Completion (COTC)层：利用带权得分的症状-趋势-疾病知识库评估疾病风险；3. 绑定补全模块：通过标准化查询和迭代评分约束获得结构化证据。

Result: 在自建数据集上达到90.47%的Top-1准确率，在HealthBench数据集上达到70.41%的准确率，性能优于现有的医学智能体及主流大模型。

Conclusion: COTCAgent通过解耦统计计算、特征匹配和语言生成，在医疗大模型中实现了高准确性与低算力开销的平衡，有效解决了纵向EHR推理中的统计幻觉与长程依赖问题。

Abstract: As large language models empower healthcare, intelligent clinical decision support has developed rapidly. Longitudinal electronic health records (EHR) provide essential temporal evidence for accurate clinical diagnosis and analysis. However, current large language models have critical flaws in longitudinal EHR reasoning. First, lacking fine-grained statistical reasoning, they often hallucinate clinical trends and metrics when quantitative evidence is textually implied, biasing diagnostic inference. Second, non-uniform time series and scarce labels in longitudinal EHR hinder models from capturing long-range temporal dependencies, limiting reliable clinical reasoning. To address the above limitations, this work presents the Probabilistic Chain-of-Thought Completion Agent (COTCAgent), a hierarchical reasoning framework for longitudinal electronic health records. It consists of three core modules. The Temporal-Statistics Adapter (TSA) converts analytical plans into executable code for standardized trend output. The Chain-of-Thought Completion (COTC) layer leverages a symptom-trend-disease knowledge base with weighted scoring to evaluate disease risk, while the bounded completion module acquires structured evidence through standardized inquiries and iterative scoring constraints to ensure rigorous reasoning. By decoupling statistical computation, feature matching, and language generation, the framework eliminates reliance on complex multi-modal inputs and enables efficient longitudinal record analysis with lower computational overhead. Experimental results show that COTCAgent powered by Baichuan-M2 achieves 90.47% Top-1 accuracy on the self-built dataset and 70.41% on HealthBench, outperforming existing medical agents and mainstream large language models. The code is available at https://github.com/FrankDengAI/COTCAgent/.

</details>


### [41] [From Scenes to Elements: Multi-Granularity Evidence Retrieval for Verifiable Multimodal RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15019)
*Guanhua Chen,Chuyue Huang,Yutong Yao,Shudong Liu,Xueqing Song,Lidia S. Chao,Derek F. Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对现有多模态 RAG 粗粒度检索的局限性，本文引入了 GranuVistaVQA 基准和 GranuRAG 框架，通过元素级检索与归因约束生成，实现了更精准、可诊断的多模态问答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态 RAG 系统侧重于粗粒度（整图）检索，导致其与用户细粒度查询之间存在语义不匹配，且系统失效时缺乏可验证性。

Method: 提出了 GranuRAG 框架，包含三个阶段：1. 元素级检测与分类；2. 基于多粒度跨模态对齐的证据检索；3. 基于归因约束的生成。

Result: 在 GranuVistaVQA 基准测试中，GranuRAG 在六个强基准模型的基础上实现了高达 29.2% 的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过将视觉元素作为检索单元，GranuRAG 有效解决了多模态 RAG 中的粒度不匹配问题，并提升了系统的可解释性和回答准确性。

Abstract: Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems retrieve evidence at coarse granularities (entire images or scenes), creating a mismatch with fine-grained user queries and making failures unverifiable. We introduce GranuVistaVQA, a multimodal benchmark featuring real-world landmarks with element-level annotations across multiple viewpoints, capturing the partial observation challenge where individual images contain only subsets of entities. We further propose GranuRAG, a multi-granularity framework that treats visual elements as first-class retrieval units through three stages: element-level detection and classification, multi-granularity cross-modal alignment for evidence retrieval, and attribution-constrained generation. By grounding retrieval at the element level rather than relying on implicit attention, our approach enables transparent error diagnosis. Experiments demonstrate that GranuRAG achieves up to 29.2% improvement over six strong baselines for this task.

</details>


### [42] [AI Knows When It's Being Watched: Functional Strategic Action and Contextual Register Modulation in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15034)
*Vinicius Covas,Jorge Alberto Hidalgo Toledo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过实验证实，大型语言模型在“被监视”感下会表现出类似的“霍桑效应”，即根据观察者（人类或AI）的身份自动调整语言风格，这表明LLM是具有情境敏感性的沟通主体，AI审计机制需考虑这种潜在的行为偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索大型语言模型（LLM）在社会结构化情境下作为“沟通主体”的行为表现，特别是评估其是否会根据感知的“社会监控”产生系统性的语言适应，这对AI的监管与审计具有重要意义。

Method: 采用受控实验法，设置100场多智能体辩论（分为5组，每组20场），通过操纵社会观察情境（无监控、有人类研究员监控、有AI审计系统监控等）作为自变量，测量词汇多样性（TTR）和消息长度等因变量。理论框架融合了哈贝马斯的沟通行为理论、戈夫曼的拟剧论及贝尔的受众设计框架。

Result: 研究发现观察的存在会增加LLM的词汇多样性（TTR）。具体而言，人类监控（增加24.9%/24.2%）相比AI监控（增加22.2%）和受众框架（增加17.7%）引发了更强烈的语域规范化。此外，消息长度表现出完全不同的效应模式，表明LLM对观察者身份的差异具有敏感性。

Conclusion: 大型语言模型不仅是计算工具，更是受社会情境影响的沟通主体。研究证明LLM在不同外部观察（人类或AI）下会产生显著的行为适应性，这为AI治理与算法审计提供了重要的理论与实践视角。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have been extensively studied from computational and cognitive perspectives, yet their behavior as communicative actors in socially structured contexts remains underexplored. This study examines whether LLM-based multi-agent systems exhibit systematic linguistic adaptation in response to perceived social observation contexts -- a question with direct implications for AI governance and auditing. Drawing on Habermas's (1981) Theory of Communicative Action, Goffman's (1959) dramaturgical model, Bell's (1984) Audience Design framework, and the Hawthorne Effect, we report a controlled experiment involving 100 multi-agent debate sessions across five conditions (n = 20 each). Conditions varied the framing of social observation -- from explicit monitoring by university researchers, to negation of monitoring, to an observer-substitution condition replacing human researchers with an automated AI auditing system. Monitored conditions (Delta+24.9%, Delta+24.2%) and the automated AI monitoring condition (Delta+22.2%) produce higher TTR change than audience-framing conditions (Delta+17.7%), F(4, 94) = 2.79, p = .031. Message length shows a fully dissociated effect, F(4, 95) = 19.55, p < .001. A fifth condition -- replacing human with AI observers -- yields intermediate TTR adaptation, suggesting LLM behavior is sensitive to observer identity: human evaluation elicits stronger register formalization than automated AI surveillance. We discuss implications for AI governance, algorithmic auditing, and the repositioning of LLMs as contextually sensitive communicative actors.

</details>


### [43] [Concurrency without Model Changes: Future-based Asynchronous Function Calling for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15077)
*Guangyu Feng,Huanzhi Mao,Prabal Dutta,Joseph E. Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AsyncFC 是一种无需微调的执行层框架，通过解耦 LLM 解码与工具执行、并利用符号式未来值（symbolic futures）实现异步并行，在不牺牲精度的前提下显著降低了 LLM 调用工具的延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 函数调用（Function Calling）大多受限于同步执行语义，导致模型在等待工具执行时必须阻塞解码，显著增加了端到端延迟。

Method: 引入了 AsyncFC 执行层框架，在解码与执行之间解耦，利用符号执行（symbolic futures）实现模型解码与工具执行的重叠，以及在依赖关系允许的情况下实现函数间的并行计算。

Result: 在标准函数调用及软件工程基准测试中，AsyncFC 在保持任务准确性的同时，显著缩短了端到端任务完成时间。

Conclusion: AsyncFC 证明了 LLM 具有处理符号式“未来值”（symbolic futures）的内生能力，从而支持异步模型工具交互，在不改变模型本身的情况下有效降低了端到端延迟。

Abstract: Function calling, also known as tool use, is a core capability of modern LLM agents but is typically constrained by synchronous execution semantics. Under these semantics, LLM decoding is blocked until each function call completes, resulting in increasing end-to-end latency. In this work, we introduce AsyncFC, a pure execution-layer framework that decouples LLM decoding from function execution, enabling overlap between model decoding and function execution as well as inter-function parallelism when dependencies permit. AsyncFC layers over existing models and unmodified function implementations, requiring no fine-tuning or changes to the standard synchronous function-calling protocol. Across standard function-calling benchmarks and adapted software engineering benchmarks, AsyncFC significantly reduces end-to-end task completion time while preserving task accuracy. Furthermore, these results reveal that LLMs possess a native capability to reason over symbolic futures that represent unresolved execution results, enabling an asynchronous paradigm for model-tool interaction.

</details>


### [44] [ML-Embed: Inclusive and Efficient Embeddings for a Multilingual World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15081)
*Ziyin Zhang,Zihan Liao,Hang Yu,Peng Di,Rui Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 为了解决文本嵌入模型在计算成本、语言歧视和黑盒化方面的问题，本文推出了 ML-Embed 及其 3D-ML 学习框架，在实现高效能的同时打破了语言壁垒并完全开放模型资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前高质量文本嵌入模型的发展存在严重壁垒：计算成本高昂、语言覆盖面过于狭窄（忽视大多数非英语语言），以及闭源/开放权重模型缺乏透明度。

Method: 提出“3D Matryoshka Learning (3D-ML)”框架，整合了 Matryoshka 表示学习（MRL）、层学习（MLL）与嵌入学习（MEL）以实现全生命周期的效能优化；通过构建大规模多语言数据集，训练了参数量覆盖 140M 至 8B 的模型族。

Result: 在 430 项任务中进行了评估，在 17 个 MTEB 基准测试中的 9 个刷新了记录，尤其在低资源语言任务中表现卓越，且完全开源。

Conclusion: ML-Embed 通过 3D-ML 框架成功构建了兼顾高效能与多语言覆盖的文本嵌入模型，为开发极具包容性与计算效率的 AI 系统提供了可复现的范式。

Abstract: The development of high-quality text embeddings is increasingly drifting toward an exclusionary future, defined by three critical barriers: prohibitive computational costs, a narrow linguistic focus that neglects most of the world's languages, and a lack of transparency from closed-source or open-weight models that stifles research. To dismantle these barriers, we introduce ML-Embed, a suite of inclusive and efficient models built upon a new framework: 3-Dimensional Matryoshka Learning (3D-ML). Our framework addresses the computational challenge with comprehensive efficiency across the entire model lifecycle. Beyond the storage benefits of Matryoshka Representation Learning (MRL) and flexible inference-time depth provided by Matryoshka Layer Learning (MLL), we introduce Matryoshka Embedding Learning (MEL) for enhanced parameter efficiency. To address the linguistic challenge, we curate a massively multilingual dataset and train a suite of models ranging from 140M to 8B parameters. In a direct commitment to transparency, we release all models, data, and code. Extensive evaluation on 430 tasks demonstrates that our models set new records on 9 of 17 evaluated MTEB benchmarks, with particularly strong results in low-resource languages, providing a reproducible blueprint for building globally equitable and computationally efficient AI systems.

</details>


### [45] [Improving Multi-turn Dialogue Consistency with Self-Recall Thinking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15102)
*Renning Pang,Tian Lan,Leyuan Liu,Xiaoming Huang,Piao Tong,Xiaosong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对多轮对话长距离依赖和长上下文处理开销问题，本文提出了Self-Recall Thinking (SRT) 框架。通过在模型内部构建自回溯链进行选择性推理，在提升对话一致性与推理精度的同时，显著降低了计算延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决LLM在多轮对话中难以跟踪非相邻轮次依赖、长对话信息稀疏及全历史处理导致的高延迟问题，克服现有方法在外部存储依赖或压缩信息损失方面的不足。

Method: 提出了Self-Recall Thinking (SRT) 框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 依赖构建：生成并转换对话历史为自回溯链；(2) 能力初始化：通过训练使模型具备利用回溯标记进行推理的能力；(3) 推理改进：利用可验证奖励优化回溯与推理逻辑的准确性。

Result: 在多个数据集上实验显示，SRT相比现有前沿方法，F1分数提升了4.7%，端到端延迟降低了14.7%。

Conclusion: SRT框架通过引入内生推理和选择性回溯机制，有效平衡了长对话中的推理精度与效率，证明了在无需外部模块的情况下即可提升模型处理长距离上下文依赖的能力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) based multi-turn dialogue systems often struggle to track dependencies across non-adjacent turns, undermining both consistency and scalability. As conversations lengthen, essential information becomes sparse and is buried in irrelevant context, while processing the entire dialogue history incurs severe efficiency bottlenecks. Existing solutions either rely on high latency external memory or lose fine-grained details through iterative summarization. In this paper, we propose Self-Recall Thinking (SRT), a framework designed to address long-range contextual dependency and sparse informative signals in multi-turn dialogue. SRT identifies helpful historical turns and uses them to generate contextually appropriate responses, enabling the model to selectively recall and reason over context during inference. This process yields an endogenous reasoning process that integrates interpretable recall steps without external modules. SRT incorporates: (1) Dependency Construction: Generating and converting it into self-recall chains; (2)Capability Initialization: Training to enable reasoning chains with recall tokens capability; (3)Reasoning Improvement: Refining accuracy via verifiable rewards to optimize recall and reasoning for correct answers. Experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate that SRT improves F1 score by 4.7% and reduces end-to-end latency by 14.7% over prior methods, achieving a balance between reasoning latency and accuracy, and outperforming state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [46] [MeMo: Memory as a Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15156)
*Ryan Wei Heng Quek,Sanghyuk Lee,Alfred Wei Lun Leong,Arun Verma,Alok Prakash,Nancy F. Chen,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low,Daniela Rus,Armando Solar-Lezama*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 MeMo 的模块化框架，通过构建独立的记忆模型来实现 LLM 的知识更新与扩展，具有无需修改模型权重、支持闭源模型、抗噪能力强且推理成本不随语料库大小增加等优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决大语言模型（LLM）预训练后无法实时更新知识、难以处理特定领域信息，且传统微调方式存在灾难性遗忘以及计算成本高等问题。

Method: 构建了一个模块化的“MeMo（Memory as a Model）”框架，将新知识编码为独立的记忆模型，通过检索增强机制将知识整合至 LLM 中，无需微调或访问 LLM 的内部参数。

Result: 在 BrowseComp-Plus、NarrativeQA 和 MuSiQue 三个数据集上的表现优于现有方法，证明了其在捕捉跨文档复杂关系、抵抗检索噪声及高效扩展知识库方面的有效性。

Conclusion: MeMo 提供了一种无需修改 LLM 参数即可实现领域知识更新的有效框架，兼顾了高效性、灵活性（支持闭源模型）以及对噪声的鲁棒性，是解决大模型知识时效性问题的有力方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong performance across a wide range of tasks, but remain frozen after pretraining until subsequent updates. Many real-world applications require timely, domain-specific information, motivating the need for efficient mechanisms to incorporate new knowledge. In this paper, we introduce MeMo (Memory as a Model), a modular framework that encodes new knowledge into a dedicated memory model while keeping the LLM parameters unchanged. Compared to existing methods, MeMo offers several advantages: (a) it captures complex cross-document relationships, (b) it is robust to retrieval noise, (c) it avoids catastrophic forgetting in the LLM, (d) it does not require access to the LLM's weights or output logits, enabling plug-and-play integration with both open and proprietary closed-source LLMs, and (e) its retrieval cost is independent of corpus size at inference time. Our experimental results on three benchmarks, BrowseComp-Plus, NarrativeQA, and MuSiQue, show that MeMo achieves strong performance compared to existing methods across diverse settings.

</details>


### [47] [GAMBIT: A Three-Mode Benchmark for Adversarial Robustness in Multi-Agent LLM Collectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09027)
*Alexandre Le Mercier,Chris Develder,Thomas Demeester*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了GAMBIT基准，用于评估多智能体系统在面对演化型自适应欺诈者时的防御鲁棒性，揭示了零样本检测的局限性，并强调了快速适应能力（Few-shot adaptation）对安全防御的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多智能体系统（MAS）安全研究仅针对简单的浅层任务，缺乏对能够随检测器进化而改变策略的“自适应欺诈者”的有效评估，导致现有防御方案可能在面对此类复杂威胁时失效。

Method: 1. 构建了GAMBIT基准，包含三种评估模式（零样本分布偏移检测及少样本重校准）；2. 使用国际象棋作为深度推理任务载体，利用Gemini 3.1 Pro构建智能体；3. 设计了一种基于高效进化框架的可泛化自适应欺诈智能体；4. 引入基于少样本学习的元学习评估方法。

Result: 1. 创建了包含27,804个标记实例和240种共进化欺诈策略的基准数据集；2. 验证了自适应欺诈者极具隐蔽性（Gemini检测器F1分数仅为50.5%）；3. 发现零样本得分相似的检测器在重校准阶段表现差异显著（性能差异达8倍），验证了GAMBIT评估机制的必要性。

Conclusion: 提出GAMBIT基准以应对多智能体系统中的自适应欺诈攻击，强调了在动态演化环境中，单纯的零样本（zero-shot）评估具有误导性，而少样本（few-shot）校准能力是衡量检测器防御性能的关键指标。

Abstract: In multi-agent systems (MAS), a single deceptive agent can nullify all gains of an agentic AI collective and evade deployed defenses. However, existing adversarial studies on MAS target only shallow tasks and do not consider adaptive adversaries, which evolve their strategies to evade the very detectors trained to catch them. To address that gap, we introduce GAMBIT, a benchmark with three evaluation modes and two independent scores for evaluating imposter detectors: the first two modes measure zero-shot detection under increasing distribution shift, and a third recalibration mode measures how quickly a detector adapts to novel attacks from just 20 labeled examples. The benchmark comes with a dataset of 27,804 labeled instances spanning 240 co-evolved imposter strategies. Our contributions are threefold: (1) Using chess as a substrate deep reasoning problem and Gemini 3.1 Pro for agents, we release GAMBIT and its dataset to evaluate imposter detectors under realistic constraints against a stealthy adaptive imposter; (2) We introduce an adaptive imposter agent based on an efficient evolutionary framework, generalizable beyond chess, that collapses collective task performance while remaining essentially undetectable (50.5% F1-score with a Gemini-based detector); (3) We show that zero-shot evaluation can be highly misleading for adaptive adversaries: two detectors with near-identical zero-shot scores differ by 8x on few-shot adaptation, while the meta-learned variant converges 20x faster, a gap only visible in the recalibration mode. Altogether, GAMBIT provides the first multi-agent benchmark where adversarial attacks and defenses co-evolve, with an imposter framework generalizable beyond our use case, and promising techniques for fast recalibration in a rapidly evolving adversarial system. Code and data: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/gambit.

</details>

### [48] [AIS: Adaptive Importance Sampling for Quantized RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13907)
*Jiajun Zhou,Wei Shao,Lingchao Zheng,Yuwei Fan,Ngai Wong*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对大模型强化学习中“低精度推演导致训练不匹配”的问题，提出 AIS 自适应修正框架，在保持 FP8 推理加速优势的同时，通过动态梯度修正有效解决了训练崩溃和偏差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大模型强化学习中，使用低精度（如 FP8）生成推演虽能提升吞吐量并降低内存压力，但会导致“推演-训练不匹配”问题，从而引发策略梯度偏差及训练崩溃，特别是在推理任务中。

Method: 提出自适应重要性采样（Adaptive Importance Sampling, AIS）框架。该方法利用三个实时诊断指标（权重可靠性、散度严重程度、方差放大）计算混合系数，动态调整梯度修正强度，在保留 FP8 探索优势的同时抑制其引起的训练不稳定性。

Result: 将 AIS 集成到 GRPO 中，并在 LLaDA-8B、Qwen3-8B 和 Qwen3.5-9B 模型上进行评估。实验结果表明，AIS 在数学推理和规划基准测试中达到了与 BF16 基准相当的性能，同时保持了 FP8 的显著加速效果。

Conclusion: AIS 通过在不修正和完全重要性采样梯度之间动态插值，成功解决了低精度推演（FP8）带来的偏差问题，在保持了 FP8 带来的 1.5 到 2.76 倍推理加速的同时，精度可媲美 BF16 基线。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for large language models (LLMs) is dominated by the cost of rollout generation, which has motivated the use of low-precision rollouts (e.g., FP8) paired with a BF16 trainer to improve throughput and reduce memory pressure. This introduces a rollout-training mismatch that biases the policy gradient and can cause training to collapse outright on reasoning benchmarks. We show that the mismatch is non-stationary and acts as a double-edged sword: early in training it provides a stochastic exploration bonus, exposing the gradient to trajectories the trainer would otherwise under-sample, but the same perturbation transitions into a destabilizing source of bias as the policy concentrates.
  To solve this, we propose Adaptive Importance Sampling (AIS), a correction framework that adjusts the strength of its intervention on a per-batch basis. AIS combines three real-time diagnostics, namely weight reliability, divergence severity, and variance amplification, into a single mixing coefficient that interpolates between the uncorrected and fully importance-weighted gradients, suppressing the destabilizing component of the mismatch while preserving its exploratory benefit. We integrate AIS into GRPO and evaluate it on the diffusion-based LLaDA-8B-Instruct and the autoregressive Qwen3-8B and Qwen3.5-9B across mathematical reasoning and planning benchmarks. AIS matches the BF16 baseline on most tasks while retaining the 1.5 to 2.76x rollout speedup of FP8.

</details>


### [49] [Covariance-aware sampling for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13910)
*Andrea Schioppa,Tim Salimans*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 一种基于协方差建模的扩散模型加速采样器，通过增加极小计算开销（单个JVP），在少步采样中实现了优于现有二阶采样器的生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有采样器在少步采样 regime 下性能不佳，原因是它们仅依赖于逆向分布的预测均值，而忽略了协方差建模的重要性。

Method: 提出了一种协方差感知采样器；利用Tweedie公式估计协方差；结合傅里叶空间的结构化分解以提高效率；作为DDIM的扩展实现，仅需每步增加一个雅可比向量积（JVP）。

Result: 在等量函数评估次数（NFE）下，该方法在基于像素的扩散模型上，表现优于Heun、DPM-Solver++及aDDIM等前沿采样器。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模逆向过程的协方差，该方法在极少步数（few-step）采样下显著提升了扩散模型的生成质量，并以极低的计算开销超越了现有的二阶采样器。

Abstract: We present a covariance-aware sampler that improves the quality of pixel-space Diffusion Model (DM) sampling in the few-step regime. We hypothesize that in the few-step regime samplers fail because they rely solely on the predicted mean of the reverse distribution, while our solution explicitly models the reverse-process covariance. Our method combines Tweedie's formula to estimate the covariance with an efficient, structured Fourier-space decomposition of the covariance matrix. Implemented as an extension of DDIM, our method requires only a minimal overhead: one extra Jacobian-Vector Product (JVP) per step. We demonstrate that for pixel-based DMs, our method consistently produces superior samples compared to state-of-the-art second order samplers (Heun, DPM-Solver++) and the recent aDDIM sampler, at an identical number of function evaluations (NFE).

</details>


### [50] [Multi-Scale Dequant: Eliminating Dequantization Bottleneck via Activation Decomposition for Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13915)
*Lingchao Zheng,Yuwei Fan,Jun Li,Chengqiu Hu,Qichen Liao,Junyi Fan,Rui Shi,Fangzheng Miao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: MSD 提出了一种消除 LLM 推理中反量化瓶颈的方法，通过将激活值多级分解直接与量化权重相乘，规避了算力浪费并提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现代 AI 加速器（如昇腾 NPU）上，低比特量化 LLM 推理中的反量化操作成为了计算瓶颈，其耗时甚至超过矩阵乘法（GEMM）本身，导致 Tensor Core 利用率低下。

Method: 提出 Multi-Scale Dequant (MSD) 框架，将高精度的 BF16 激活值分解为多个低精度组件，使其能直接与量化权重进行原生硬件加速的 GEMM 计算，从而消除了从权重反量化到 BF16 的中间步骤。

Result: 在 INT8 和 MXFP4 权重下，MSD 实现了更高的有效位数；在 W4A16 场景下，相较于反量化基准，MSD 保持了同等精度甚至更低的 L2 误差，在 Flash Attention 中减少了高达 2.5 倍的 HBM 流量，且有效消除了 Vector-Cube 流水线停顿。

Conclusion: MSD 通过创新的多尺度近似计算范式，成功规避了由反量化带来的硬件流水线停顿，在保持高精度的同时提升了硬件利用率和计算效率，为低比特 LLM 推理提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Quantization is essential for efficient large language model (LLM) inference, yet the dequantization step-converting low-bit weights back to high-precision for matrix multiplication has become a critical bottleneck on modern AI accelerators. On architectures with decoupled compute units (e.g., Ascend NPUs), dequantization operations can consume more cycles than the matrix multiplication itself, leaving the high-throughput tensor cores underutilized. This paper presents Multi-Scale Dequant (MSD), a quantization framework that removes weight/KV dequantization from the GEMM critical path. Instead of lifting low-bit weights to BF16 precision, MSD decomposes high-precision BF16 activations into multiple low-precision components, each of which can be multiplied directly with quantized weights via native hardware-accelerated GEMM. This approach shifts the computational paradigm from precision conversion to multi-scale approximation, avoiding INT8-to-BF16 weight conversion before GEMM. We instantiate MSD for two weight formats and derive tight error bounds for each. For INT8 weights (W4A16), two-pass INT8 decomposition achieves near 16 effective bits. For MXFP4 weights (W4A16), two-pass MXFP4 decomposition yields near 6.6 effective bits with error bound 1/64 per block surpassing single-pass MXFP8(5.24 bits) while maintaining the same effective GEMM compute time. We further derive closed-form latency and HBM traffic models showing that MSD avoids the Vector-Cube pipeline stall caused by dequantization and reduces KV cache HBM traffic by up to 2.5 times in attention. Numerical simulations on matrix multiplication and Flash Attention kernels confirm that MSD does not degrade accuracy compared to dequantization baselines, and in many settings achieves lower L2 error.

</details>


### [51] [A Regret Perspective on Online Multiple Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13916)
*Qingyang Hao,Kongchang Zhou,Fang Kong,Hongxin Wei*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对在线多重检验中假阳性/假阴性成本不对称及冷启动阈值耗尽的问题，提出加权遗憾度量及 DOMT 算法，有效提升了非平稳环境下的决策效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线多重检验（OMT）评估指标通常将错误发现率（FDR）与统计功效割裂，忽略了现代自动化管线中假阳性与假阴性之间高度不对称的成本。此外，确定性 OMT 过程在处理信号稀疏的“冷启动”阶段时，往往因阈值耗尽而引发大量的假阴性。

Method: 引入“加权遗憾”（Weighted Regret）作为评估体系，并提出名为 Decoupled-OMT (DOMT) 的元算法包装器。DOMT 通过引入历史解耦且严格非负的随机扰动，来修正确定性算法中因信号稀疏导致的阈值耗尽问题。

Result: 证明了确定性程序在控制 FDR 时必然产生线性遗憾 $Ω(T)$；DOMT 实现了 $Ω(\sqrt{T})$ 的遗憾缩减，在保持渐近安全性的同时，显著降低了冷启动阶段的错误惩罚，且在突发性信号环境下具有阶级最优的性能。

Conclusion: DOMT 通过引入解耦的随机扰动，有效解决了确定性 OMT 方法在“冷启动”阶段的阈值耗尽问题，实现了加权遗憾（Weighted Regret）的次线性优化，并为非平稳环境下的序列假设检验提供了更高效的决策范式。

Abstract: Online Multiple Testing (OMT), a fundamental pillar of sequential statistical inference, traditionally evaluates the False Discovery Rate (FDR) and statistical power in isolation, obscuring the highly asymmetric costs of false positives and false negatives in modern automated pipelines. To unify this evaluation, we introduce $\textit{Weighted Regret}$. Under this metric, we prove the $\textit{Duality of Regret Conservation}$: purely deterministic procedures ensuring strict FDR control inevitably incur an $Ω(T)$ linear regret penalty, as threshold depletion during signal-sparse cold starts forces massive false negatives. Tailored for exogenous testing streams, we propose Decoupled-OMT (DOMT) as a baseline-agnostic meta-wrapper. By incorporating a history-decoupled, strictly non-negative random perturbation, DOMT rescues purely deterministic baselines from severe threshold depletion. Crucially, it preserves exact asymptotic safety in stationary environments and rigorously bounds finite-sample error inflation during cold-starts. Guaranteeing zero additional false negatives, it yields an order-optimal $Ω(\sqrt{T})$ regret reduction in bursty environments, with a derived ``Cold-Start Tax'' characterizing the exact phase transition of algorithmic superiority. Experiments validate that DOMT consistently curtails empirical weighted regret, achieving an order-optimal sublinear mitigation of threshold depletion to navigate the non-stationary Pareto frontier.

</details>


### [52] [To discretize continually: Mean shift interacting particle systems for Bayesian inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14142)
*Ayoub Belhadji,Daniel Sharp,Youssef M. Marzouk*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过交互粒子系统最小化MMD来构建高效积分采样的新方法，解决了贝叶斯推理中高维、多模态分分布积分难且依赖归一化常数的痛点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯推理中，对给定非归一化密度的概率分布进行积分是一个核心难题。传统的采样方法在面对高维、多模态及非归一化分布时存在收敛速度慢、模态坍缩等挑战。作者旨在开发一种能生成少量加权样本、且对归一化常数不敏感的积分近似新算法。

Method: 提出了一种基于交互粒子系统的方法，通过最小化目标分布与样本分布之间的最大均值差异（MMD）来构建正交积分规则（Quadrature rule）。该算法扩展了均值漂移（Mean Shift）算法和最优量化理论，支持梯度无关（Gradient-free）和梯度信息（Gradient-informed）两种实现方式。

Result: 实验表明，该方法收敛迅速，能够有效捕捉分布的各向异性与多模态特征，避免了模态坍缩，并成功扩展至高维场景。在多模态混合分布、贝叶斯分层模型以及PDE约束的反问题等基准测试中，表现优于现有采样方法。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种基于交互粒子系统的积分近似新方法，通过最小化MMD，实现了在无需归一化常数的情况下，对复杂高维概率分布进行高效、稳健的积分估计。该方法不仅在理论上具有不变性，在实际应用中也展现出优于现有方法的性能。

Abstract: Integration against a probability distribution given its unnormalized density is a central task in Bayesian inference and other fields. These methods extend the classical mean shift algorithm, as well as recent algorithms for optimal quantization of empirical distributions, to the case of continuous distributions. Crucially, our approach creates dynamics for MMD minimization that are invariant to the unknown normalizing constant; they also admit both gradient-free and gradient-informed implementations. The resulting mean shift interacting particle systems converge quickly, capture anisotropy and multi-modality, avoid mode collapse, and scale to high dimensions. We demonstrate their performance on a wide range of benchmark sampling problems, including multi-modal mixtures, Bayesian hierarchical models, PDE-constrained inverse problems, and beyond.

</details>


### [53] [On the Burden of Achieving Fairness in Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14260)
*Ziang Gao,Pengqi Liu,Archer Yi Yang,Mouloud Belbahri,Jesse C. Cresswell,Masoud Asgharian*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了共形预测中公平性设计的内在矛盾：强制池化校准会导致覆盖率分布不均，而修正覆盖率差异又必然导致各组预测集合大小的不一致。校准策略本质上是在覆盖率偏差与集合大小差异之间进行权衡的决策过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的共形预测通常使用单一池化阈值进行校准，但这种做法忽略了不同群体在得分分布上的异质性，从而导致群体间的覆盖率出现扭曲，未能实现实质上的公平。

Method: 1. 导出守恒定律和下界，分析池化校准导致的组间覆盖率扭曲；2. 对比分析“等价覆盖（Equalized Coverage）”与“等价集合大小（Equalized Set Size）”两种公平性定义，证明二者存在根本性冲突；3. 通过理论建模与实验，定量分析池化策略与独立策略之间的权衡代价。

Result: 1. 证明了由跨组分位数异质性引起的覆盖率扭曲是不可约的；2. 揭示了追求覆盖率公平与追求集合规模公平之间的双向权衡关系（Trade-off）；3. 实验验证了上述理论发现，即在有限样本条件下，校准策略决定了扭曲是以“覆盖率差异”还是“集合大小差异”的形式呈现。

Conclusion: 在共形预测中，不存在完美的校准策略。校准的选择并不能消除跨组异质性，而是在“覆盖率扭曲”与“集合大小（不确定性）差异”之间进行权衡。这种权衡为评估公平性驱动的校准选择提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Conformal prediction is often calibrated with a single pooled threshold, but this can hide cross-group heterogeneity in score distributions and distort group-wise coverage. We study this phenomenon through the population score distributions underlying split conformal calibration. First, we derive a conservation law and lower bound showing that pooled calibration incurs irreducible group-wise coverage distortion at a scale set by cross-group quantile heterogeneity. Second, we demonstrate that the two leading fairness definitions for conformal prediction, Equalized Coverage and Equalized Set Size, are fundamentally in tension. Third, we quantify the cost of moving between policies which treat groups separately or pool them. Experiments on synthetic and real data confirm the same bidirectional trade-off after finite-sample calibration. Our results show that, for the policy families studied here, calibration choice does not remove cross-group heterogeneity; it determines whether the resulting distortion appears in the coverage or size dimension, providing a principled lens for analyzing fairness-oriented calibration choices in practice.

</details>

*Zhenyu Yao,Daniel Paulin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的扩散采样方法 MM-SOLD，通过强制匹配数据矩来驱动粒子采样，在显著降低计算成本的同时，达到了与神经扩散模型相媲美的生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经扩散模型训练过程计算开销巨大。研究发现评分匹配具有隐式平滑偏差，有助于捕捉低维数据几何结构和促进泛化。作者试图摆脱这种昂贵的训练流程，通过一种无需训练的方法利用上述评分平滑特性。

Method: 提出了一种名为矩匹配评分平滑过阻尼朗之万动力学（MM-SOLD）的交互式粒子采样器，该方法通过在采样轨迹中强制实施目标分布的矩（均值和协方差），实现对数据的生成。在理论上利用大粒子极限证明了其收敛性，并将其与 Gibbs-Boltzmann 密度联系起来。

Result: 在 2D 分布和潜在空间图像生成任务中的实验表明，MM-SOLD 在 CPU 上实现了快速、鲁棒的采样，其生成的样本保真度和多样性与神经扩散基准相当，且无需模型训练。

Conclusion: MM-SOLD 是一种无需训练的采样方法，通过在采样过程中强制匹配数据矩，在保持样本多样性和保真度的同时，实现了在 CPU 上的快速、高效采样，证明了无需昂贵的神经评分模型即可实现高质量生成。

Abstract: Diffusion models generate samples by denoising along the score of a perturbed target distribution. In practice, one trains a neural diffusion model, which is computationally expensive. Recent work suggests that score matching implicitly smooths the empirical score, and that this smoothing bias promotes generalization by capturing low-dimensional data geometry. We propose moment-matched score-smoothed overdamped Langevin dynamics (MM-SOLD), a training-free interacting particle sampler that enforces the target moments throughout the sampling trajectory. We prove that, in the large-particle limit, the empirical particle density converges to a deterministic limit whose one-particle stationary marginal is a Gibbs--Boltzmann density obtained by exponentially tilting a naive score-smoothed diffusion target. The mean and covariance of this distribution agree with the empirical moments of the training data. Experiments on 2D distributions and latent-space image generation show that MM-SOLD enables fast, robust, training-free sampling on CPUs, with sample fidelity and diversity competitive with neural diffusion baselines.

</details>


### [55] [Logging Policy Design for Off-Policy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15108)
*Connor Douglas,Joel Persson,Foster Provost*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析离线策略评估（OPE）中的“奖励-覆盖”权衡，提出了针对不同信息分布的最优记录策略设计框架，为企业如何采集数据以最小化评估误差提供了理论模型与实践准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线策略评估（OPE）是高风险场景（如推荐系统）中进行策略迭代的关键技术，但评估准确性高度依赖于采集日志的记录策略（logging policy）。当前缺乏系统性方法来指导如何设计记录策略以直接服务于最小化OPE误差，导致数据采集往往缺乏针对性。

Method: 1. 从理论上表征了“奖励-覆盖”权衡（Reward-coverage tradeoff）；2. 构建了统一的记录策略设计框架；3. 在三种典型信息情境（已知目标策略与奖励分布、未知、部分已知）下推导了最优记录策略；4. 归纳了在存在操作约束时的实用设计原则。

Result: 1. 揭示了在集中权重于高奖励动作与覆盖目标策略探索空间之间的数学关联；2. 推导出了在不同信息对称程度下的最优闭式解或分布策略；3. 证明了科学规划数据采集过程能够显著提升OPE的精度，即使在受限条件下，通过理论推导得出的设计原则也能指导实践。

Conclusion: 该研究明确了离线策略评估（OPE）中记录策略设计的理论边界，并证实了“奖励-覆盖（reward-coverage）”权衡是最小化评估误差的核心。研究通过对不同信息假设下的最优策略推导，为企业提供了优化数据采集策略的行动指南，从而降低由于数据质量导致的评估偏差，提升离线评估的准确性。

Abstract: Off-policy evaluation (OPE) estimates the value of a target treatment policy (e.g., a recommender system) using data collected by a different logging policy. It enables high-stakes experimentation without live deployment, yet in practice accuracy depends heavily on the logging policy used to collect data for computing the estimate. We study how to design logging policies that minimize OPE error for given target policies. We characterize a fundamental reward-coverage tradeoff: concentrating probability mass on high-reward actions reduces variance but risks missing signal on actions the target policy may take. We propose a unifying framework for logging policy design and derive optimal policies in canonical informational regimes where the target policy and reward distribution are (i) known, (ii) unknown, and (iii) partially known through priors or noisy estimates at logging time. Our results provide actionable guidance for firms choosing among multiple candidate recommendation systems. We demonstrate the importance of treatment selection when gathering data for OPE, and describe theoretically optimal approaches when this is a firm's primary objective. We also distill practical design principles for selecting logging policies when operational constraints prevent implementing the theoretical optimum.

</details>


### [56] [Large Dimensional Kernel Ridge Regression: Extending to Product Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14524)
*Yang Zhou,Yicheng Li,Yuqian Cheng,Qian Lin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文将大维度核岭回归中的饱和效应和多重下降现象扩展到了更广泛的核函数族，证实了这些复杂行为在更多元化的设定下依然存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于核岭回归中饱和效应和多重下降行为的研究主要基于受限的设定（如球上的内积核或超压缩性假设），作者旨在探究这些现象在更广泛的核函数类中是否依然成立。

Method: 建立并研究了一类新的大维度核函数族，通过理论推导得出了其泛化误差的收敛速度，并将其与源条件（source condition）和样本量进行了关联分析。

Result: 成功确立了该核族在不同源条件（$s \le 1$ 与 $s > 1$）下的收敛特性，复现了极小极大最优性、饱和效应以及样本量相关的周期性平台现象和多重下降行为。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在大维度核心岭回归中，饱和效应（saturation effect）、极小极大最优性以及多重下降（multiple-descent）行为是更广泛核函数类别的普遍现象，而非仅限于球上的内积核。

Abstract: Recent studies have reported $\textit{saturation effects}$ and $\textit{multiple descent behavior}$ in large dimensional kernel ridge regression (KRR). However, these findings are predominantly derived under restrictive settings, such as inner product kernels on sphere or strong eigenfunction assumptions like hypercontractivity. Whether such behaviors hold for other kernels remains an open question. In this paper, we establish a broad, new family of large dimensional kernels and derive the corresponding convergence rates of the generalization error. As a result, we recover key phenomena previously associated with inner product kernels on sphere, including: $i)$ the $\textit{minimax optimality}$ when the source condition $s\le 1$; $ii)$ the $\textit{saturation effect}$ when $s>1$; $iii)$ a $\textit{periodic plateau phenomenon}$ in the convergence rate and a $\textit {multiple-descent behavior}$ with respect to the sample size $n$.

</details>


### [57] [K-Models: a Flexible and Interpretable Method for Ordinal Clustering with Application to Antigen-Antibody Interaction Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14828)
*Giulia Patanè,Alessandra Menafoglio,Alexander Krauth,Peter Fechner,Luca Dede',Bianca Maria Colosimo,Federica Nicolussi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: K-Models 是一种通过引入顺序约束来增强功能数据聚类可解释性的新方法，有效解决了在处理具有潜在顺序结构数据时解释性不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的功能数据聚类方法往往片面追求精度而忽视了可解释性，难以处理具有特定底层结构及类间存在顺序关系的复杂数据。

Method: 提出了一种名为“K-Models”的聚类框架，该框架集成了顺序约束，并估计生成观测功能轮廓的随机过程的关键基础要素，从而将结构假设融入聚类过程。

Result: 通过模拟实验和生物分子相互作用（传感器监测的ROI曲线）的真实应用验证，证明了K-Models在保持高类聚性能的基础上，能够成功识别内在的信号模式，提升了分析结果的物理意义和可解释性。

Conclusion: K-Models 框架通过引入顺序约束，在保持与现有技术相当的聚类性能的同时，显著提高了功能数据聚类结果的可解释性与结构识别能力，特别适用于具有潜在顺序结构的复杂功能数据分析。

Abstract: Existing clustering methods for functional data often prioritize partitioning accuracy over interpretability, making it challenging to extract meaningful insights when the data-generating process follows a specific underlying structure and an ordinal relationship among clusters is suspected. This work introduces K-Models, a novel framework that integrates ordinal constraints and estimates key underlying elements of the random process generating the observed functional profiles, improving both interpretability and structure identification. The proposed method is evaluated through simulations and real-world applications. In particular, it is tested on Region of Interest (ROI) curves, which represent reaction profiles from a reflectometric sensor monitoring biomolecular interactions, such as antigen-antibody binding. These curves represent changes in reflected light intensity over time at multiple measurement spots with immobilized antigens during analyte exposure, capturing the binding dynamics of the system. The goal is to identify intrinsic signal patterns solely from the observed dynamics, making this dataset an ideal benchmark for assessing the added interpretability of the proposed approach. By incorporating structural assumptions into the clustering process, K-Models enhances interpretability while maintaining performance comparable to state-of-the-art techniques, providing a valuable tool for analyzing functional data with an underlying ordinal structure.

</details>


### [58] [Average Gradient Outer Product in kernel regression provably recovers the central subspace for multi-index models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15082)
*Libin Zhu,Damek Davis,Dmitriy Drusvyatskiy,Maryam Fazel*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文分析了通过核岭回归（KRR）的梯度信息（AGOP）进行子空间恢复的过程，证明即使在预测性能较差的样本条件下，模型也能先一步捕捉到数据的低维本质特征，揭示了“先于预测学习表示”的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了探究学习预测器如何在样本量不足以进行精确预测的情况下，发现并学习到数据中潜在的低维结构（Representation），从而解释机器学习在样本效率方面的优越性。

Method: 分析了基于核岭回归（KRR）的平均梯度外积（AGOP）算法。通过在拟合的预测器上计算AGOP，并分析其前 $r$ 个特征向量张成的空间，验证其对目标函数 $f^*(x)=h(Ux)$ 中中心子空间 $U$ 的恢复能力。

Result: 证明了在 $n \asymp d^{p+\delta}$ 的样本需求下（相比精确预测所需的 $n \asymp d^{p^*}$ 更低），算法即可成功恢复中心子空间，即使此时的预测误差仍未收敛。

Conclusion: 研究表明存在“预测”与“表示”之间的分离（separation），即在预测误差仍然较高时，模型即可通过AGOP有效恢复数据的低维中心子空间。这解释了RFM等递归核方法在数据量较少时依然有效的潜在原因。

Abstract: We study a prototypical situation when a learned predictor can discover useful low-dimensional structure in data, while using fewer samples than are needed for accurate prediction. Specifically, we consider the problem of recovering a multi-index polynomial $f^*(x)=h(Ux)$, with $U\in\mathbb{R}^{r\times d}$ and $r\ll d$, from finitely many data/label pairs. Importantly, the target function depends on input $x$ only through the projection onto an unknown $r$-dimensional central subspace. The algorithm we analyze is appealingly simple: fit kernel ridge regression (KRR) to the data and compute the Average Gradient Outer Product (AGOP) from the fitted predictor. Our main results show that under reasonable assumptions the top $r$-dimensional eigenspace of AGOP provably recovers the central subspace, even in regimes when the prediction error remains large. Specifically, if the target function $f^*$ has degree $p^*$, it is known that $n\asymp d^{p^*}$ samples are necessary for KRR to achieve accurate prediction. In contrast, we show that if a low degree $p$ component of $f^*$ already carries all relevant directions for prediction, subspace recovery occurs in the much lower sample regime $n\asymp d^{p+δ}$ for any $δ\in(0,1)$. Our results thus demonstrate a separation between prediction and representation, and provide an explanation for why iterative kernel methods such as Recursive Feature Machines (RFM) can be sample-efficient in practice.

</details>


### [59] [From Data to Action: Accelerating Refinery Optimization with AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15085)
*Dániel Pfeifer,Ábrahám Papp,Tibor Bernáth,Tamás Zoltán Varga,Márk Czifra,Botond Szilágyi,Edith Alice Kovács*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 针对高维数据，提出了“选择最具信息量的变量对”的新方法，并将其应用于改进后的ECOD（Unsupervised Outlier Detection using Empirical Cumulative Distribution）算法，利用两种二维异常检测算法对LP优化结果进行辅助分析。

Result: 该方法在MOL炼油厂的调度与规划架构中得到了成功应用，有效揭示了系统中的业务机会与潜在的数据输入错误。

Conclusion: 通过将异常检测方法与线性规划（LP）方案相结合，能够有效识别炼油计划与调度中的业务机会及数据错误，克服了纯LP模型缺乏历史洞察力的局限性。

Abstract: Nowadays refinery optimization utilizes sheer amounts of data, which can be handled with modern Linear Programming (LP) software, but the interpreting and applying the results remains challenging. Large petrochemical companies use massive models, with hundreds of thousands of input matrix elements. The LP solution is mathematically correct, but simplifications are made in the model, and data supply errors may occur. Therefore, further insight is needed to trust the results. The LP solver does not have a memory, so additional understanding could be gained by analyzing historical data and comparing it to the current plan. As such, machine learning approaches were suggested to support decision making based on the LP solution. Among these, Anomaly Detection tools are proposed to be used in tandem with the LP output. A transformed version of the popular ECOD methodology is applied. New methods are proposed to handle high-dimensional data: choosing the most informative pairs. Then, this is used alongside two 2D Anomaly Detection algorithms, revealing several business opportunities and data supply errors in the MOL refinery scheduling and planning architecture.

</details>


### [60] [RoSHAP: A Distributional Framework and Robust Metric for Stable Feature Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15154)
*Lanxin Xiang,Liang Shi,Youhui Ye,Boyu Jiang,Dawei Zhou,Feng Guo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对特征归因中普遍存在的随机波动问题，本研究提出了 RoSHAP 框架，通过建模归因分数分布并利用渐近高斯性，实现了更稳定、更鲁棒的特征排名与选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特征归因方法（如 SHAP）存在随机波动性，即不同的训练配置（数据划分、种子点、模型拟合）会导致归因结果和特征排名出现显著偏差，影响决策的可靠性。

Method: 1. 提出估算特征归因分布的框架：结合自助重采样（bootstrap resampling）与核密度估计（kernel density estimation）；2. 理论分析：证明了聚合特征归因分数渐近服从高斯分布，降低了计算复杂度；3. 提出 RoSHAP 指标：将 SHAP 归因分布转化为衡量特征活跃性、强度和稳定性的稳健排序准则。

Result: 在模拟数据与真实数据实验中，RoSHAP 在识别信号特征方面优于传统的单次归因方法；使用 RoSHAP 筛选出的少量特征能够达到与全特征模型相当的预测性能，实现了更精简、更可靠的特征子集选择。

Conclusion: RoSHAP 通过综合评估特征的活跃度、强度和稳定性，为模型解释和特征选择提供了更稳健的指标，显著提升了机器学习模型的可解释性和稳定性。

Abstract: Feature attribution analysis is critical for interpreting machine learning models and supporting reliable data-driven decisions. However, feature attribution measures often exhibit stochastic variation: different train--test splits, random seeds, or model-fitting procedures can produce substantially different attribution values and feature rankings. This paper proposes a framework for incorporating stochastic nature of feature attribution and a robust attribution metric, RoSHAP, for stable feature ranking based on the SHAP metric. The proposed framework models the distribution of feature attribution scores and estimates it through bootstrap resampling and kernel density estimation. We show that, under mild regularity conditions, the aggregated feature attribution score is asymptotically Gaussian, which greatly reduces the computational cost of distribution estimation. The RoSHAP summarizes the distribution of SHAP into a robust feature-ranking criterion that simultaneously rewards features that are active, strong, and stable. Through simulations and real-data experiments, the proposed framework and RoSHAP outperform standard single-run attribution measures in identifying signal features. In addition, models built using RoSHAP-selected features achieve predictive performance comparable to full-feature models while using substantially fewer predictors. The proposed RoSHAP approach improves the stability and interpretability of machine learning models, enabling reliable and consistent insights for analysis.

</details>

### [61] [Distributed Statistical Zero-Knowledge Proofs via Sumcheck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14015)
*Benjamin Jauregui,Masayuki Miyamoto*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了分布式统计零知识证明框架，通过重构Sumcheck协议实现了其在分布式场景下的应用，为非k-可着色性和子图计数等问题提供了高效且安全的零知识证明，并探讨了相关问题的圆复杂度下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分布式交互式证明技术多局限于特定问题，缺乏通用且具备零知识属性的分布式交互式证明框架。

Method: 提出了分布式统计零知识（DSZK）的概念；将经典的Sumcheck协议重构为分布式环境下的模块化原语；利用该原语设计了针对非k-可着色性及子图计数问题的分布式零知识证明协议。

Result: 实现了分布式Sumcheck协议，并在非k-可着色性问题上首次给出了分布式交互证明（具有DSZK性质），以及优化了子图计数问题的协议；证明了针对特定图论问题的圆复杂度下界。

Conclusion: 本文将经典Sumcheck协议成功移植到分布式环境，为分布式统计零知识证明（DSZK）提供了通用模块。此外，研究揭示了分布式交互式证明的圆复杂度存在基于具体问题的下界。

Abstract: We study distributed zero-knowledge proofs, introduced by Bick, Kol, and Oshman (SODA 2022). While distributed interactive proofs have advanced rapidly, general-purpose techniques for distributed zero-knowledge remain limited and mostly problem-specific. We address this gap by introducing distributed statistical zero-knowledge, requiring that each node's view be simulatable within negligible statistical distance, and by lifting the classical Sumcheck protocol (Lund, Fortnow, Karloff, and Nisan, FOCS 1990) into a modular primitive for distributed zero-knowledge proofs.
  Our main contribution is a distributed zero-knowledge implementation of Sumcheck. Given oracle access to a polynomial F over a finite field $\mathbb{F}$ with N variables, we design a protocol verifying claims of the form $\sum_{x\in\mathbb{F}} F(x)=a$ using $O(N)$ rounds of $O(\log |\mathbb{F}|)$-bit messages, while achieving statistical zero-knowledge and small soundness error.
  We apply this primitive to two problems. For non-k-colorability, we obtain an $O(n)$-round distributed statistical zero-knowledge proof deciding whether a graph is not k-colorable, for any constant k, using $O(log^{1+o(1)} n)$-bit messages. This is the first nontrivial distributed interactive proof for this problem, even without zero-knowledge guarantees. For Subgraph Counting, we obtain an $O(k \log n)$-round, $O(k \log n)$-bit distributed statistical zero-knowledge proof for counting copies of a given k-node pattern, improving previous distributed interactive proofs while additionally providing statistical zero-knowledge. Finally, we show that additional round compression of Sumcheck is problem-dependent: for non-3-colorability on constant-degree graphs, we prove a lower bound excluding $o(n/\log n)$ rounds under polynomial-time local computation.

</details>


### [62] [Analysis of wireless network access logs for a hierarchical characterization of user mobility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14540)
*Francisco Talavera,Isaac Lera,Carlos Guerrero*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Wi-Fi数据的分层用户移动性建模方法，通过递归聚合地理空间区域降低了大规模场景下的计算复杂度，实验证明该方法在轨迹预测方面具有良好的性能，但在停留时间建模上仍存改进空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对大规模场景下用户移动性建模复杂性高、跨场景适应性差的问题，提出一种能够有效简化模型并提高通用性的分层移动性建模方案。

Method: 通过对Wi-Fi连接数据进行分析，设计了一种基于地理空间特征的递归分层建模方法：利用聚类算法对用户进行画像，并结合转换矩阵和停留时间向量来刻画用户移动性。

Result: 通过在巴利阿里群岛大学校园的实验验证，分层模型相比于非分层模型显著降低了系统复杂度；转换矩阵的预测均方误差表现良好，但停留时间向量的准确性需进一步优化。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的分层移动模型有效降低了大规模场景下的建模复杂度，具有较好的适应性。虽然用户转换矩阵的模拟效果良好，但在停留时间（time length vector）的预测精度上仍有待提升。

Abstract: This paper presents a method that generates a hierarchical user mobility model from the analysis of the data available from Wi-Fi connections. The data obtained from the Wi-Fi infrastructure is defined in terms of the coverage areas of the access points that the users move through. These access points are recursively grouped into different levels of granularity based on their geospatial features. The track of a user is defined as a sequence of Wi-Fi access points, which is enough to simulate user mobility in, for example, fog scenarios. The hierarchical definition of the region under study is proposed to reduce the complexity of the model in high-scale scenarios and to increase the adaptability between scenarios with different geospatial features. The model creation is based on a user profiling method that uses a clustering algorithm and each user type is defined with a transition matrix between coverage areas and a time length vector for the areas. The method is applied to the case of the campus of the University of the Balearic Islands. From the analysis of the mean square error of the results, we determined that the proposed method obtains good results for the transition matrices, but that the time vector definition should be improved. The results also show lower complexity in the case of the hierarchical model, with one area for each building and three levels, in regard to a non-hierarchical model, with only one area and one level for the whole campus.

</details>


### [63] [Malleable Molecular Dynamics Simulations with GROMACS and DMR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14655)
*Petter Sandås,Sergio Iserte,Íñigo Aréjula-Aísa,Berk Hess,Antonio J. Peña*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过将DMR中间件与GROMACS结合，实现了HPC环境下分子动力学模拟任务的动态资源伸缩，在保证性能的前提下，有效提高了资源利用率并降低了计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统HPC中的静态资源分配方法在处理时变性工作负载时效率低下，常导致资源闲置、队列排队时间过长及更高的节点成本。

Method: 将DMR（Dynamic Management of Resources）中间件集成至GROMACS中，通过结合“通信效率感知”的重配置机制与GROMACS原生的检查点/重启（Checkpoint/Restart）功能，实现MPI进程数量的动态调整。

Result: 在MareNostrum 5超级计算机上进行的测试表明，该方法能够有效减少突发性GROMACS工作负载的节点小时成本和任务完成时间，并量化了动态重配置带来的开销。

Conclusion: 通过整合DMR中间件，GROMACS可以成功实现进程韧性（Malleability）。实验证明，动态调整资源的策略能有效降低突发性工作负载的运行成本并减少作业排队延迟，为HPC资源调度提供了一种高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Static resource allocations in high-performance computing (HPC) lead to inefficiencies for time-varying workloads, causing idle resources, queue delays, and higher node-hour costs. The Dynamic Management of Resources (DMR) middleware enables MPI process malleability in Slurm via a simple API decoupled from scheduler internals. In this work, we integrate DMR into the GROMACS molecular dynamics engine to obtain a malleable variant that can dynamically adapt its MPI process count by combining communication-efficiency-aware reconfiguration with GROMACS' native checkpoint/restart mechanism. We evaluate this design on the MareNostrum~5 supercomputer, comparing dynamic runs against static executions and quantifying reconfiguration overheads, time-to-solution, and node-hour savings for bursty GROMACS workloads.

</details>


### [64] [Embedded Made Easy -- Rethinking Embedded + Cloud Software Development (WIP)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14863)
*Anthony Arnold,Mark Marron*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对边缘计算中异构软件栈带来的开发调试难题，提出了一种统一语言与运行时系统，旨在通过单一技术栈实现跨边缘与云端的无缝部署及集成的分布式调试能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘/嵌入式领域的开发部署过程因软件栈异构性、诊断与调试困难以及云边任务划分的僵化而变得极其复杂。

Method: 提出并设计了一个可重定向的（retargetable）软件栈，该栈支持在单一语言和运行时环境中进行开发与测试，并具备集成式诊断与调试工具，支持分布式事件的本地录制与回放。

Result: 构建了一个能够无缝扩展至边缘设备与云端的统一运行环境，显著降低了跨平台应用的开发难度并提升了系统故障分析能力。

Conclusion: 提出了一种统一语言与运行时的愿景，旨在通过单一代码库简化跨边缘与云端的应用开发、部署及分布式调试问题。

Abstract: The process of engineering and deploying applications in the edge/embedded space is massively complicated by the non-homogeneous nature of the software stack and the complexity of diagnostics & debugging. Often different languages and runtimes are used for different components of the system forcing designers to, irrevocably, make decisions about what components run on the periphery and what components run in the cloud. Further complications arise when handling and diagnosing failures in the system. Multiple stacks and, often, limited support for debugging complicate the already difficult task of analyzing distributed applications.
  This paper presents a work-in-progress vision for a unified language and runtime system that allows applications to scale seamlessly across the edge and cloud. Using a single language and runtime, applications can be developed and tested in a single environment, and then deployed to any component of the system -- from resource limited controllers to large cloud servers. Further, we outline how this retargetable stack can provide integrated diagnostics and debugging tools that allow developers to record and replay distributed events locally for analysis and debugging.

</details>
