### [65] [Clustering with Locally Bounded Ignorance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13917)
*Jaroslav Garvardt,Christian Komusiewicz*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 通过利用相关聚类中“模糊边图”的结构参数（退化度或闭包），该论文证明了该问题的多项式核存在性，克服了仅使用成本 $k$ 作为参数时无法获得多项式核的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相关聚类（Correlation Clustering）在参数 $k$ 下不存在多项式核（基于与 Edge Multicut 的联系），因此作者试图通过引入模糊边图的结构参数来寻找更小的参数集合，以实现多项式核。

Method: 使用参数化复杂性分析（Parameterized Complexity），引入模糊边图（weight-0 edges）的结构参数，包括退化度（degeneracy）和闭包（closure），构建多项式核并证明对应结构下的硬度。

Result: 在参数 $k+d$（$d$ 为退化度）和 $k+c$（$c$ 为闭包）下，相关聚类拥有多项式核；同时证明了在边和非边诱导图具有特定受限结构时，该问题存在计算复杂性瓶颈（hardness）。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了相关聚类问题在模糊边图的退化度(d)或闭包(c)参数化下的多项式核存在性，并补充了在特定受限图结构下的计算复杂度下界，从而完善了该问题的参数化复杂性图景。

Abstract: In Correlation Clustering, the input is a graph $G=(V,E)$ with weight function $ω: {V \choose 2}\to Z$
  and the task is to partition the vertex set into clusters such that
  the total weight of edges between clusters and missing edges
  inside clusters is minimized. Due to close connections
  between Correlation Clustering and Edge Multicut,
  deciding whether there is a partition with total cost at most $k$ is
  FPT with respect to $k$ but a polynomial kernel is presumably
  impossible. We study the influence of the structure of the fuzzy
  edge graph, that is, the graph induced by the weight-0 edges, on the
  problem complexity. We show in particular that Correlation
  Clustering admits a polynomial problem kernel when parameterized
  by $k+d$, where $d$ is the degeneracy of the fuzzy edge graph, and when
  parameterized by $k+c$, where $c$ is the closure of the fuzzy edge
  graph. We complement these positive results by showing hardness for
  several settings where the graph induced by the edges and nonedges has very restricted structure.

</details>


### [66] [Non-Redundancy of Low-Arity Symmetric Boolean CSPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14007)
*Amatya Sharma,Santhoshini Velusamy*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文研究了对称布尔CSP中的“非冗余度”（NRD）参数，通过引入“t-平衡性”及其与多线性多项式的等价性，近乎完全分类了元数至5的对称谓词的NRD渐近增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于非冗余度（NRD）在核化（kernelization）、稀疏化及流式算法复杂度中扮演关键角色，作者旨在研究并分类布尔对称约束满足问题中NRD的渐近增长行为，以深化对该参数在复杂性理论中表现的理解。

Method: 1. 理论框架：利用结构参数“非冗余度”（NRD）分析CSP实例。2. 上界证明：引入“t-平衡性”（t-balancedness），证明其等价于存在捕捉关系R的t次多线性多项式，从而推导出NRD的上界为O(n^t)。3. 下界证明：应用Carbonnel的框架，通过从k元OR约简来建立Ω(n^k)的复杂性下界。4. 实验验证：结合计算实验与代数准则对谓词进行分类。

Result: 1. 成功对所有元数不超过4的对称布尔谓词的NRD进行了分类。2. 解决了元数为5的谓词中除两个以外的所有情形。3. 确立了t-平衡性作为获取多项式上界的核心工具，并验证了其在复杂性分析中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究近乎完全地分类了布尔对称约束满足问题（CSP）中蕴含的“非冗余度”（NRD）的渐近增长，并揭示了其与多项式表征及特定约简框架之间的深层联系。除两个特殊情形外，该研究解决了所有元数（arity）不超过5的对称谓词的NRD分类问题。

Abstract: Non-redundancy, introduced by Bessiere, Carbonnel, and Katsirelos (AAAI 2020), is a structural parameter for Constraint Satisfaction Problems ($\mathsf{CSPs}$) that governs kernelization, exact and approximate sparsification, and exact streaming complexity. It is the largest size of a $\mathsf{CSP}$ instance admitting no smaller subinstance with the same satisfying assignments.
  We study non-redundancy $\mathsf{NRD}_n(R)$ for Boolean symmetric $\mathsf{CSPs}$ defined by an $r$-ary relation $R$ whose value depends only on Hamming weight. An instance of $\mathsf{CSP}(R)$ has $n$ variables and constraints given by $r$-tuples; a constraint is satisfied exactly when the induced tuple lies in $R$. This class includes natural predicates such as cuts and $k$-SAT clauses. Our main result is a near-complete classification of the asymptotic growth of $\mathsf{NRD}_n(R)$ for symmetric Boolean predicates of arity at most $5$. Using computational experiments and algebraic upper- and lower-bound criteria, we resolve every predicate of arity at most $4$ and all but two predicates of arity $5$.
  For upper bounds, we introduce $t$-balancedness, a lifted, higher-degree version of the balancedness notion of Chen, Jansen, and Pieterse (Algorithmica 2020). We prove that $t$-balancedness is equivalent to the existence of degree-$t$ multilinear polynomials capturing $R$, and hence implies $\mathsf{NRD}_n(R)=O(n^t)$. For lower bounds, we use Carbonnel's (CP 2022) framework: predicates admitting a special reduction from $k$-ary OR inherit OR's lower bound $Ω(n^k)$. The only unresolved arity-$5$ predicates in our framework have bounds $Ω(n^2)$ and $O(n^3)$; we reduce their exact classification to natural extremal set-system questions.

</details>


### [67] [Hybrid Sketching Methods for Dynamic Connectivity on Sparse Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15173)
*Quinten De Man,Gilvir Gill,Michael A. Bender,Laxman Dhulipala,David Tench*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合草图与无损存储的混合算法 HybridSCALE，有效解决了传统草图算法在处理非均匀稀疏图中空间成本过高的问题，在保持算法高效性的同时，在多种密度图下实现了大幅空间压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于草图的动态连通性算法虽然空间复杂度优于无损结构，但其每个顶点的草图开销巨大，导致其只有在图极其稠密时才具有存储优势，无法在稀疏的真实世界图中发挥价值。

Method: 提出了一种名为“混合草图”（hybrid sketching）的方法，仅对图中的稠密核心进行草图编码，而对稀疏外围部分采用无损存储；引入了 BalloonSketch 技术以减小 $l_0$-采样器的空间开销；开发了 HybridSCALE 系统，模块化地整合了上述机制。

Result: 实现了混合空间复杂度 $O(\min\{V+E, V \log V \log(2+E/V)\})$；BalloonSketch 将单位节点草图大小减少了 8 倍；在真实数据集上，HybridSCALE 相较于最先进的无损算法，在稀疏图、中等密度图和稠密图中分别实现了最高 15%、92% 和 97% 的空间节省。

Conclusion: HybridSCALE 证明了混合草图策略在处理动态连通性问题上的有效性。该方法巧妙结合了草图（sketching）与无损存储，能够根据图的实际密度自适应分配资源，不仅在理论上达到了新的空间复杂度上界，在实际应用中也显著降低了各类密度图所需的存储空间，成功将理论进展转化为实际工程效率。

Abstract: Dynamic connectivity is a fundamental dynamic graph problem, and recent algorithmic breakthroughs on dynamic graph sketching have reshaped what is theoretically possible: by encoding the graph as per-vertex linear sketches, these algorithms solve dynamic connectivity in only $Θ(V \log^2 V)$ space, independent of the number of edges,outperforming lossless $Θ(V+E)$-space structures that grow as the graph becomes denser. Prior to this work, no practical dynamic connectivity algorithm has been able to translate these theoretical breakthroughs into space savings on real-world graphs. The main obstacle is that per-vertex sketches cost thousands of bytes per vertex, so sketching only pays off once the graph becomes extremely dense.
  We observe that sparse real-world graphs are often not uniformly sparse, these graphs can contain dense cores on a small subset of vertices that account for a large fraction of edges. We exploit this structure via hybrid sketching: sketch only the dense core, and store the sparse periphery losslessly.
  We design new hybrid algorithms for fully-dynamic and semi-streaming connectivity with space $O(\min\{V+E, V \log V \log(2+E/V)\})$ w.h.p., simultaneously matching the lossless bound on sparse graphs, the sketching bound on dense graphs, and improving on both in an intermediate regime. A key component is BalloonSketch, a new l0-sampler reducing per-vertex sketch sizes by up to 8x. We implement HybridSCALE, a modular system treating the lossless and sketch-based components as subroutines. HybridSCALE is the first sketch-based dynamic connectivity system to save space on common real-world graphs. Compared to the state-of-the-art lossless baseline, HybridSCALE saves up to 15% space on sparse graphs (average degree < 100), up to 92% on intermediate density graphs (average degree ~ 100-1000), and up to 97% on dense graphs (average degree > 1000).

</details>


### [68] [New Algorithms for Parity-SAT and Its Bounded-Occurrence Versions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14093)
*Sanjay Jain,Junqiang Peng,Frank Stephan,Haoyun Tang,Mingyu Xiao*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文探讨了Parity-SAT问题，通过限制变量出现次数$d$和利用奇偶性结构，成功设计出一系列高效算法，突破了该类问题长期存在的经典指数级计算壁垒，且其表现优于现有的#SAT算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Parity-SAT作为$\oplus$P-complete问题，在强指数时间假设（SETH）下无法在全类中突破$2^n$或$2^m$的复杂度壁垒。本文旨在探索通过结构化约束（即变量出现次数限制）和深入挖掘奇偶性（Parity）特性，突破这一理论计算界的可能性。

Method: 1. 设计了一种随机算法，实现了$O^*(2^{m(1-1/O(d))})$的时间复杂度，突破了有界变量出现次数$d$下的$2^m$壁垒；2. 对$d=2$的特殊情况，开发了精细的分支算法，复杂度达到$O^*(1.1193^n)$或$O^*(1.3248^m)$；3. 基于结构洞察，针对一般Parity-SAT提出了以公式总长度$L$为参数的$O^*(1.1052^L)$算法；所有算法均采用多项式空间。

Result: 成功突破了固定$d$情形下的$2^m$复杂度壁垒，并在$d=2$的特殊情形及一般长度$L$的情形下获得了比目前最佳精确计数（#SAT）更优的运行时间界限。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过结构限制（如变量出现次数d）和对奇偶性特性的精细利用，可以突破Parity-SAT计算中的经典指数复杂度壁垒（$2^n$或$2^m$）。研究表明，奇偶性计算在算法设计上比通用精确计数（#SAT）更具优势，且在有界出现次数下可以系统地规避指数级限制。

Abstract: Parity-SAT is the problem of determining whether a given CNF formula has an odd number of satisfying assignments. As a canonical $\oplus$P-complete problem, it represents a fundamental variant of the exact model counting problem (#SAT). Under the Strong Exponential Time Hypothesis (SETH), Parity-SAT admits no $O^*((2-\varepsilon)^n)$-time or $O^*((2-\varepsilon)^m)$-time algorithm for any constant $\varepsilon>0$, where $n$ and $m$ denote the numbers of variables and clauses, respectively. Thus, breaking the $2^n$ or $2^m$ barrier appears impossible in full generality.
  In this work, we revisit this barrier through structural restrictions and a refined exploitation of parity. We study Parity-$d$-occ-SAT, where each variable appears in at most $d$ clauses, and obtain three main results. First, we design {a randomized} $O^*(2^{m(1-1/O(d))})$-time algorithm, thereby breaking the $2^m$ barrier for every fixed $d$. Second, for the special case $d=2$, we develop a significantly sharper branching algorithm running in $O^*(1.1193^n)$ time or $O^*(1.3248^m)$ time. Third, leveraging the structural insights underlying the $d=2$ case, we obtain an $O^*(1.1052^L)$-time algorithm for general Parity-SAT, where $L$ denotes the formula length. All algorithms use only polynomial space. Notably, our running-time bounds are better than the best known bounds for the corresponding exact counting counterparts, highlighting a genuine algorithmic advantage of parity over counting. Conceptually, our results demonstrate that parity admits finer structural reductions and more efficient branching than exact model counting, and that bounded occurrence can be systematically leveraged to circumvent classical exponential barriers.

</details>


### [69] [Fast Leaf-to-Ancestor Minimum Query in the Oracle Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14112)
*Aleksey Upirvitskiy,Aleksandr Levin*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对有根加权树路径最小值查询的数据结构，通过梯子分解和二进制提升技术，将所有权重比较操作预计算化，实现了 O(n log h) 预处理下的 O(1) 无查询开销查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在研究有根加权树中的“叶子到祖先路径最小值查询”问题，特别关注在仅允许比较操作的预言机 (Oracle) 模型下，如何优化预处理代价与查询性能。

Method: 1. 权重转换：将边权重转化为节点权重并结合确定性决胜规则以建立全序；2. 梯子分解 (Ladder Decomposition)：通过最长路径分解处理树结构；3. 二进制提升 (Binary Lifting)；4. 稀疏表 (Sparse Table)：在梯子分解后的数组上构建RMQ，预处理时利用预定义的比较预估索引。

Result: 实现了 O(n log h) 的预处理时间、空间及预言机调用次数，查询时间达到确定的 O(1)，且查询阶段无需任何预言机调用；并证明了该预处理比较复杂度在确定性比较模型下是紧致的（即达到最优）。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种在比较模型下最优的静态数据结构，实现了在预处理阶段完成所有比较操作，从而在查询阶段实现无比较的 O(1) 时间复杂度查询，并证明了预处理阶段比较次数复杂度的下界。

Abstract: We study leaf-to-ancestor path-minimum queries on a rooted, weighted tree in the oracle model, where the only allowed value operation is a comparison oracle on edge (or node) weights. We give a static data structure that, after O(n log h) preprocessing time, space, and oracle calls (where $n$ is the number of nodes and $h$ is the tree height), answers any leaf-to-ancestor query in O(1) worst-case time with zero oracle calls at query time. The method combines (I) an edge-to-node weight conversion with a deterministic tie-break to obtain a total order; (II) ladder (longest-path) decomposition; (III) binary lifting; and (IV) sparse-table RMQ built over ladder arrays, storing indices selected via the oracle during preprocessing. We also show that the preprocessing oracle-comparison bound is tight in the deterministic comparison model.

</details>


### [70] [Stochastic Matching via Local Sparsification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14195)
*Sara Ahmadian,Edith Cohen,Mohammad Roghani*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 针对去中心化系统中带宽受限的问题，本文提出了一种两阶段稀疏化框架，通过在局部进行预算内修剪，在维持低通信负担的同时实现高效率的全局匹配方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实时网约车和分布式云计算等现代去中心化系统中，瓶颈通常是局部通信带宽，而非匹配的即时性，因此需要构建一种能够平衡局部信息约束与全局匹配优化效果的框架。

Method: 1. 形式化定义了两阶段局部稀疏化（two-stage local sparsification）框架；2. 提出了一种由期望实例的分数解（fractional solution）参数化的局部选择策略；3. 通过量化解的“传播度（spread）”来衡量近似比。

Result: 理论上证明了在足够的传播度下，该稀疏化方法能保留最大匹配的期望大小；实验表明，在纽约网约车数据集和对抗性合成基准测试中，即使在严格的局部限制下，该方法仍能实现近乎最优的全局匹配，且明显优于标准的在线匹配基线。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种“两阶段局部稀疏化”框架，证明了在有限的局部通信预算下，通过基于期望实例分数解所参数化的局部选择策略，可以实现近乎最优的全局匹配，弥合了信息局部限制与全局优化之间的差距。

Abstract: The classic online stochastic matching problem typically requires immediate and irrevocable matching decisions. However, in many modern decentralized systems such as real-time ride-hailing and distributed cloud computing, the primary bottleneck is often local communication bandwidth rather than the timing of the match itself. We formalize this challenge by introducing a two-stage local sparsification framework. In this setting, arriving requests must prune their realized compatibility sets to a strict budget of $k$ edges before a central coordinator optimizes the global matching. This creates a "middle ground" between local information constraints and global optimization utility.
  We propose a local selection strategy, parametrized by a fractional solution of the expected instance. Theoretically, we quantify the approximation ratio as a function of the solution's {\em spread}. We prove that under sufficient spread, our sparsifier globally preserves the expected size of the maximum matching. Empirically, we demonstrate the robustness of our approach using the New York City ride-hailing datasets and adversarial synthetic benchmarks. Our results show that near-optimal global matching is achievable even with highly constrained local budgets, significantly outperforming standard online baselines.

</details>


### [71] [Semi-Streaming Algorithms for Submodular Maximization under Random Arrival Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14296)
*Niv Buchbinder,Moran Feldman,Siyue Liu,Sherry Sarkar*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机序下的亚模函数最大化半流算法，通过引入两种新技术，在多种组合约束下取得了优于对抗序的最优近似结果，并在拟阵约束中显著降低了算法复杂度限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在半流（semi-streaming）模型下，针对多种组合约束（如拟阵、p-系统等），探索随机输入顺序相比对抗性输入顺序是否能实现算法性能的改善。

Method: 1. 提出一种将多种约束下的离线算法转化为随机序半流算法的通用框架；2. 设计了一种针对拟阵约束的离线算法的半流变体。

Result: 在多种约束下（如拟阵、$p$-系统等）取得了优于对抗序的结果；对于拟阵约束，实现了 $1 - 1/e - \varepsilon$ 近似比，且指数级减少了所需扫描（pass）次数；同时证明了 $p$-系统中的相关难度结论。

Conclusion: 随机序与对抗序在亚模函数最大化半流算法中存在本质性能差异；所提出的两项新技术能有效将离线算法转化为具备性能优势的随机序半流算法。

Abstract: We study random order semi-streaming algorithms for submodular maximization under a wide range of combinatorial constraint classes, including matroids, matroid $p$-parity, $p$-exchange systems and $p$-systems. For most of these classes of constraints, our results are the first improvement over what is known to be achievable for adversarial order. For matroids, matching and $p$-matchoids, previous random order results were known, and we improve over some of these as well. In the case of matroids, our improved results show a separation between adversarial and random order semi-streaming algorithms, and exponentially improve the number of passes necessary for getting $1 - 1/e - \varepsilon$ approximation for maximizing a monotone submodular function subject to a matroid constraint. We also prove a new hardness result showing a similar separation for $p$-systems. Our results are based on two new technical tools. One tool provides a general way to translate offline algorithms for many classes of constraints into random order semi-streaming algorithms. The other tool is a semi-streaming variant of a recently proposed offline algorithm for matroid constraints.

</details>


### [72] [zSort: Stable Distribution Sort using Z-Score Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14419)
*Hriday Jain,Ketan Sabale,Aditya Shastri,Hiren Kumar Thakkar,Ashutosh Londhe*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了 zSort，一种能够消除“稳定性税”的自适应稳定排序算法，它在保持稳定性的前提下，实现了媲美甚至超越现有多种稳定与不稳定排序算法的高吞吐量和低延迟性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前排序算法领域存在明显的“稳定性税”问题，即 practitioners 必须在保持顺序的稳定性与追求高性能（高吞吐量/速度）之间做出妥协。

Method: 提出了一种基于 z-score 的自适应分布排序算法（zSort），旨在实现稳定性的同时，避免算法复杂性随键值宽度缩放，并通过微架构分析和大规模数据集（高达 10^7 个元素）进行了实验验证。

Result: 在微架构层面，zSort 的错误推测开销降低至 19.7%，IPC 达到 1.44；在性能表现上，zSort 比传统的比较型稳定排序算法快 3-4.5 倍，在处理重复数据和部分有序数据时具有优势，且其吞吐量可与 Skasort 等高性能不稳定算法竞争。

Conclusion: zSort 通过结合适应性策略与稳定的排序机制，有效地弥合了稳定排序与不稳定排序之间的性能鸿沟，克服了传统排序算法中“稳定性税”的局限性，成为一种高效的通用稳定排序替代方案。

Abstract: Sorting is a foundational primitive in modern data processing, influencing the execution speed of high-performance data pipelines. However, the algorithmic landscape is currently bifurcated by a pervasive "Stability Tax": practitioners must sacrifice either order preservation for high throughput or execution speed for stability. To address these limitations, this paper introduces, zSort, an adaptive z-score based distribution sorting algorithm that guarantees stability while avoiding pass complexity that scales with key-width. The performance of the proposed technique is evaluated using Microarchitectural analysis and experimental results. Microarchitectural analysis shows that zSort achieves a lower bad-speculation overhead (19.7%) than both stable baselines and several high-performance unstable algorithms and sustains a competitive IPC of 1.44. Empirical evaluation across diverse input distributions and datasets of up to 10^7 elements (64 bit) demonstrates that zSort consistently outperforms widely used comparison based stable sorting algorithms, achieving up to 3x-4.5x speedups, and a relatively better performance compared to LSD Radix, with larger gains on duplicate heavy and partially ordered inputs. Despite providing stability, zSort achieves comparable throughput as compared to high-performance unstable algorithms such as Skasort. It also maintains this performance on adaptive workloads where methods like Pdqsort typically excel and doesn't exhibit any extreme worst case. These results indicate that zSort substantially narrows the traditional performance gap between stable and unstable sorting and provides an efficient, stable sorting alternative.

</details>


### [73] [Branch-width of represented matroids in matrix multiplication time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14428)
*Mujin Choi,Tuukka Korhonen,Sang-il Oum*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种计算有限域拟阵分支宽度的改进算法，将计算复杂度降至 $O_{k,\mathbb F}(n^2) + O(n^\omega)$，并在理论上证明了在现有计算假设下该 $O(n^\omega)$ 的复杂度是不可避免的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的关于拟阵分支宽度计算的算法（如 Hliněný 等人及 Jeong 等人的工作）时间复杂度至少为 $\Omega(n^3)$，作者旨在提升计算效率至近似 $O(n^\omega)$，并探讨计算该问题的理论最优边界。

Method: 提出了一个新的算法，通过矩阵表示在有限域上检测拟阵的分支宽度。利用矩阵的标准型转换和高效的计算步骤，将计算复杂度降低至 $O_{k,\mathbb F}(n^2)+O(n^\omega)$。同时利用矩阵奇异性测试证明了该复杂度的下界。

Result: 在有限域上为给定 $n$ 元素拟阵的分支宽度问题提供了时间复杂度为 $O_{k,\mathbb F}(n^2)+O(n^\omega)$ 的算法，该算法在输入矩阵为标准型时可达到 $O_{k,\mathbb F}(n^2)$。此外，该成果还显著优化了有向图秩宽（rank-width）和拟阵路径宽（path-width）的计算，并为无限域上的近似算法提供了方案。

Conclusion: 本文提出的算法在有限域上计算拟阵分支宽度（branch-width）的时间复杂度达到近似最优，其中 $O(n^\omega)$ 的基准复杂度与矩阵乘法复杂度相关。此外，作者从理论上证明了该复杂度的最优性。

Abstract: For an $n$-element matroid $M$ given by an $n \times n$ matrix representation over a finite field $\mathbb F$ and an integer $k$, we present an $(O_{k,\mathbb F}(n^2)+O(n^ω))$-time algorithm that either finds a branch-decomposition of $M$ of width at most $k$, or confirms that the branch-width of $M$ is more than $k$, where $ω< 2.3714$ is the matrix multiplication exponent, and the $O_{k,\mathbb F}(\cdot)$-notation hides factors that depend on $k$ and $\mathbb F$ in a computable manner. All previous algorithms including Hliněný and Oum [SIAM J. Comput. (2008)] and Jeong, Kim, and Oum [SIAM J. Discrete Math. (2021)] run in at least $Ω(n^3)$ time. Moreover, if the input matrix representation is given by a standard form, our algorithm runs in $O_{k,\mathbb F}(n^2)$-time, since $O(n^ω)$-time is only needed for finding a standard form of the input matrix. When $M$ is given by an $m \times n$ matrix, the overhead for finding a standard form is $O(mn \min(m,n)^{ω-2})$.
  As corollaries, we obtain faster algorithms for rank-width of directed graphs and path-width of matroids represented over a fixed finite field. Furthermore, we also present an approximation algorithm for finding branch-width that works on infinite fields provided that the input matrix is of a standard form and contains a bounded number of distinct values of entries.
  To suggest that our algorithm is optimal, we observe that for every field $\mathbb F$, deciding whether the branch-width of a matroid represented over $\mathbb F$ is $0$ is as hard as deciding whether a square matrix over $\mathbb F$ is singular. Under the assumption that singularity testing requires $Ω(n^ω)$-time, this implies that the overhead of $O(n^ω)$ is unavoidable. We also show strengthenings of this observation to rule out some approximations under this assumption.

</details>

### [74] [SemaTune: Semantic-Aware Online OS Tuning with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15026)
*Georgios Liargkovas,Mihir Nitin Joshi,Hubertus Franke,Kostis Kaffes*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: SemaTune 是一种基于大语言模型引导的在线 OS 调优框架，通过赋予系统“语义理解”能力，在约束成本与风险的前提下，显著提升了服务器的稳态运行性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线 OS 调优控制器往往将内核参数视为黑盒，缺乏对不同控制旋钮间内在联系的理解，且在缺乏应用级指标时极易失效或导致系统性能不可逆的退化。

Method: 提出了一种包含双循环架构（快速响应循环与慢速策略更新循环）的主机端框架，利用大语言模型（LLM）将旋钮模式、遥测数据、动作-响应历史及先验经验整合为决策上下文，并引入类型检查验证机制以确保调优操作的安全性和合理性。

Result: 在 13 种真实负载和 41 个 Linux 参数的调优实验中，SemaTune 相较于默认设置提升了 72.5% 的性能，相较于非 LLM 基准提升了 153.3%。即使在仅有主机级指标的情况下，也比使用应用级指标的基准方法高出 93.7 个百分点，且显著降低了系统退化的风险。

Conclusion: SemaTune 通过引入语义感知的控制框架和基于语言模型的指导，有效克服了传统黑盒 OS 调优方法的局限性（如缺乏语义理解、性能退化风险及对应用级指标的依赖），实现了高效、安全且低成本的稳态性能优化。

Abstract: Online OS tuning can improve long-running services, but existing controllers are poorly matched to live hosts. They treat scheduler, power, memory, and I/O controls as black-box variables and optimize a scalar reward. This view ignores cross-knob policy structure, breaks down when application metrics are unavailable, and can send a running service into degraded regions that persist after the bad setting is removed. We present SemaTune, a host-side framework for steady-state OS tuning with bounded language-model guidance. SemaTune turns knob schemas, telemetry, current configuration, recent action--response history, and retrieved prior runs into a compact decision context. A fast loop proposes low-latency updates, a slower loop periodically revises the search strategy, and every proposed change passes through typed validation before reaching kernel or sysctl interfaces. This lets the controller reason about OS-control meaning and indirect performance signals while keeping model cost, latency, and authority constrained. We evaluate SemaTune on 13 live workloads from five benchmark suites while tuning up to 41 Linux parameters. Across the suite, SemaTune improves stable-phase performance by 72.5\% over default settings and by 153.3\% relative to the strongest non-LLM baseline. A 30-window session costs about \$0.20 in model calls. With only host-level metrics, SemaTune still outperforms baselines given direct application objectives by 93.7 percentage points, while avoiding severe degraded regions reached by structure-blind exploration.

</details>

### [75] [Thinking Ahead: Prospection-Guided Retrieval of Memory with Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14177)
*Harshita Chopra,Krishna Kant Chintalapudi,Suman Nath,Ryen W. White,Chirag Shah*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Prospection-Guided Retrieval (PGR)，通过生成未来对话步骤作为检索探针而非依赖原始查询，有效地解决了长跨度对话中检索低语义相似度事实的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RAG与GraphRAG系统依赖基于嵌入的语义相似度，常导致在长跨度对话中无法检索到逻辑相关但语义空间距离较远的事实，难以实现真正的个性化。

Method: 提出“前瞻导向检索（PGR）”方法：通过思维树（ToT）或线性链生成未来的预期步骤作为检索探针，而非直接使用原始查询；利用检索到的事实递归地进行多轮前瞻模拟，以发现深层关联记忆。

Result: 在MemoryQuest基准测试中召回率提升近3倍；在1,625个查询的对比中，PGR生成的回复在LLM偏好评估中获得89%-98%的胜率，并得到人类评估验证。

Conclusion: 引入显式前瞻（Prospection，即预想未来步骤）机制可显著提升长跨度对话中对低语义相似度事实的检索性能与回复质量，优于传统的仅基于相似度的检索方法。

Abstract: Long-horizon personalization requires dialogue assistants to retrieve user-specific facts from extended interaction histories. In practice, many relevant facts often have low semanticsimilarity to the query under dense retrieval. Standard Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) and GraphRAG systems are still largely retrospective: they rely on embedding similarity to the query or on fixed graph traversals, so they often miss facts that matter for the user's needs but lie far from the query in embedding space. Inspired by prospection, the human ability to use imagined futures as cues for recall, we introduce Prospection-Guided Retrieval (PGR), which decouples retrieval from how memories are stored. Given a user query, PGR first expands the goal into a short Tree-of-Thought (ToT) or linear chain of plausible next steps, and uses these steps as retrieval probes rather than relying on the original query alone. The facts retrieved by these probes are then used to personalize the next round of prospection, enabling PGR to uncover additional memories that become relevant only after the simulation is grounded in the user's history. We also introduce MemoryQuest, a challenging multi-session benchmark in which each query is annotated with 3--5 dated reference facts subject to a low query-reference similarity constraint. Across 1,625 queries spanning 185 user profiles from 3 publicly available datasets, PGR-TOT substantially improves retrieval, including nearly 3x recall on MemoryQuest over the strongest baseline. In pairwise LLM-as-judge comparisons against baselines, PGR-generated responses are preferred on 89--98% of queries, with blinded human annotations on held-out subsets showing the same trend. Overall, the results demonstrate that explicit prospection yields large gains in long-horizon retrieval and response quality relative to similarity-only baselines.

</details>


### [76] [Efficient Generative Retrieval for E-commerce Search with Semantic Cluster IDs and Expert-Guided RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14434)
*Jianbo Zhu,Xing Fang,Jing Wang,Mingmin Jin,Bokang Wang,Guangxin Song,Zhenyu Xie,Junjie Bai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对传统生成式检索难以在工业级电商场景落地的问题，本文提出了兼具高效率与长链路对齐能力的CQ-SID框架与EG-GRPO优化算法，实现了生产环境下的高性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式检索在工业电商场景中面临大规模动态商品库、低延迟要求及与后端排序策略不一致的挑战；现有的端到端替代方案难以直接落地，需要更高效且可控的召回补充机制。

Method: 1. 提出CQ-SID模型：利用类目感知与查询-物品对比学习，结合残差量化变分自编码器（RQ-VAE）生成分层语义ID，降低搜索空间复杂度；2. 提出EG-GRPO算法：通过专家指导的组相对策略优化，在稀疏奖励下实现生成召回与后端排序目标的一致性对齐。

Result: 离线测试显示，CQ-SID在语义和个性化点击命中率上分别提升了26.76%和11.11%，同时减少了50%的光束搜索规模；在线A/B测试显示GMV提升1.15%，UCTCVR提升0.40%，且该召回渠道在生产中贡献了超过70%的购买量。

Conclusion: 该研究成功构建了工业级生成式检索框架，证明了生成式检索在大型电商系统作为召回辅助渠道的可行性，有效平衡了检索质量与计算效率。

Abstract: Generative retrieval offers a promising alternative by unifying the fragmented multi-stage retrieval process into a single end-to-end model. However, its practical adoption in industrial e-commerce search remains challenging, given the massive and dynamic product catalogs, strict latency requirements, and the need to align retrieval with downstream ranking goals. In this work, we propose a retrieval framework tailored for real-world recall scenarios, positioning generative retrieval as a recall-stage supplement rather than an end-to-end replacement. Our method, CQ-SID (Category-and-Query constrained Semantic ID), employs category-aware and query-item contrastive learning along with Residual Quantized VAEs to encode items into hierarchical semantic cluster identifiers, significantly reducing beam search complexity. Additionally, we develop EG-GRPO (Expert-Guided Group Relative Policy Optimization), a reinforcement learning approach that aligns generative recall with downstream ranking under sparse rewards by injecting ground-truth samples to stabilize training. Offline experiments on TmallAPP search logs show that CQ-SID achieves up to 26.76% and 11.11% relative gains in semantic and personalized click hitrate over RQ-VAE baselines, while halving beam search size. EG-GRPO further improves multi-objective performance. Online A/B tests confirm gains in GMV (+1.15%) and UCTCVR (+0.40%). The generative recall channel now contributes substantially in production, accounting for over 50.25% of exposures, 58.96% of clicks, and 72.63% of purchases, demonstrating a viable path for deploying generative retrieval in real-world e-commerce systems.

</details>


### [77] [Stop Overthinking: Unlocking Efficient Listwise Reranking with Minimal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14450)
*Danyang Liu,Kan Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究发现 LLM 在重排序任务中存在“过度思考”现象；本文通过长度正则化的自我蒸馏方法，训练模型生成简洁高效的推理逻辑，在保证排序精度的前提下显著降低了推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于思维链（CoT）的排序模型虽效果显著，但存在“过度思考（Overthinking）”现象，即产生长篇冗余推理文本会导致边际效用递减，且带来了高昂的计算成本。

Method: 提出一种“长度正则化的自我蒸馏（Length-Regularized Self-Distillation）”框架：通过从教师模型（Rank-K）采样多样化推理链路，利用帕累托启发式滤波器筛选出高精度、低 token 消耗的推理片段，并以此微调学生模型，使其学会内化高效、简洁的推理模式。

Result: 在 TREC Deep Learning 和 NeuCLIR 基准测试中，该方法实现了与原始模型相当的重排序效果，同时将推理阶段的 token 消耗降低了 34%-37%。

Conclusion: 提出了一种长度正则化的自我蒸馏框架，能够有效精简推理过程，在保持高效排序性能的同时，显著降低了大语言模型排序器的计算开销，为推理增强的重排序在延迟敏感场景下的应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Listwise reranking utilizing Large Language Models (LLMs) has achieved state-of-the-art retrieval effectiveness. Recently, reasoning-enhanced models have further pushed these boundaries by employing Chain-of-Thought (CoT) to perform deep comparative analysis of candidate documents. However, this performance gain comes at a prohibitive computational cost, as models often generate thousands of reasoning tokens before producing a final ranking. In this work, we investigate the relationship between reasoning length and ranking quality, revealing an overthinking phenomenon where extended reasoning yields diminishing returns. To address this, we propose a Length-Regularized Self-Distillation framework. We synthesize a dataset by sampling diverse reasoning traces from a teacher model (Rank-K) and applying a Pareto-inspired filter to select traces that achieve high ranking performance with minimal token usage. By fine-tuning on these concise, high-quality rationales, the student model learns to internalize efficient reasoning patterns, effectively pruning redundant deliberation. Experiments on TREC Deep Learning and NeuCLIR benchmarks demonstrate that our method maintains the teacher's effectiveness while reducing inference token consumption by 34%-37% across different retrieval settings, offering a practical solution for deploying reasoning-enhanced rerankers in latency-sensitive applications.

</details>


### [78] [Asymmetric Generative Recommendation via Multi-Expert Projection and Multi-Faceted Hierarchical Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14512)
*Bin Huang,Xin Wang,Junwei Pan,Yongqi Zhou,Yifeng Zhou,Zhixiang Feng,Shudong Huang,Haijie Gu,Wenwu Zhu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了 AsymRec，通过解耦输入（连续语义嵌入）与输出（分层离散 ID），解决了生成式推荐中长期存在的语义损失和监督精度不足的问题，显著提升了模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐模型 (GenRec) 使用对称的语义 ID 设计，存在输入端（量化损失、流行度偏差）与输出端（离散目标精度受限）的双重信息瓶颈。

Method: 提出 AsymRec 框架，包含两个核心模块：1) 多专家语义投影 (MSP)：将连续向量映射至 Transformer 隐空间以保留语义丰富度；2) 多面分层量化 (MHQ)：构建高容量、结构化的离散目标以优化监督质量。

Result: 在多项基准测试中表现优异，平均性能相较当前最先进的生成式推荐模型提升了 15.8%。

Conclusion: AsymRec 框架通过解耦输入与输出表示，有效缓解了生成式推荐中的双重信息瓶颈，显著提升了推荐系统的准确性与长尾物品的处理能力。

Abstract: Generative Recommendation (GenRec) models reformulate recommendation as a sequence generation task, representing items as discrete Semantic IDs used symmetrically as both inputs and prediction targets. We identify a critical dual-stage information bottleneck in this design: (1) the Input Bottleneck, where lossy quantization degrades fine-grained semantics, while popularity bias skews the learned representations toward frequent items, and (2) the Output Bottleneck, where imprecise discrete targets limit supervision quality. To address these issues, we propose AsymRec, an asymmetric continuous-discrete framework that decouples input and output representations. Specifically, Multi-expert Semantic Projection (MSP) maps continuous embeddings into the Transformer's hidden space via expert-specialized projections, preserving semantic richness and improving generalization to infrequent items. Multi-faceted Hierarchical Quantization (MHQ) constructs high-capacity, structured discrete targets through multi-view and multi-level quantization with semantic regularization, preventing dimensional collapse while retaining fine-grained distinctions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AsymRec consistently outperforms state-of-the-art generative recommenders by an average of 15.8 %. The code will be released.

</details>


### [79] [Discrimination Is Generation: Unifying Ranking and Retrieval from a Tokenizer Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14853)
*Shuli Wang,Junwei Yin,Changhao Li,Senjie Kou,Chi Wang,Yinqiu Huang,Yinhua Zhu,Haitao Wang,Xingxing Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对生成式推荐中 SID 构造与个性化目标脱钩的问题，本文提出了 DIG 框架，通过将判别式排序目标融入 SID 训练，实现了排序与检索引擎的一体化优化，显著提升了推荐系统的整体准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成式推荐中，语义 ID (SIDs) 的训练通常独立于具体的个性化推荐目标，导致 SID 表达能力受限，致使生成式检索在性能上落后于经典的判别式排序模型。

Method: 提出 DIG (Discrimination Is Generation) 框架，将 tokenizer 嵌入到判别式排序模型中进行端到端联合训练；利用特征分配分类法管理特征，通过 MLP_u2t 蒸馏模块实现高效推理。

Result: 在三个公开基准测试和两个工业级数据集上验证，DIG 能够同时提升排序质量、检索质量以及统一检索-排序的整体表现。

Conclusion: DIG 通过将排序任务与 SID 编码训练在端到端框架下结合，弥补了生成式推荐中语义 ID 构造与个性化需求脱钩的缺陷，实现了排序与检索性能的双重提升。

Abstract: Semantic IDs (SIDs) define the generation space of generative recommendation and directly determine its personalization ceiling. However, existing tokenizers are trained independently with retrieval objectives, leaving personalization signals fully decoupled from the SID construction process -- a fundamental gap that causes generative retrieval to persistently lag behind discriminative ranking. In this paper, we rethink the essence of SIDs: \emph{ranking seeks argmax in item space while retrieval seeks argmax in token space; both are the same problem solved at different granularities.} Based on this insight, we propose \DIG (\textbf{D}iscrimination \textbf{I}s \textbf{G}eneration), which embeds the tokenizer inside a discriminative ranking model for end-to-end training -- the ranker naturally becomes a retrieval model, yielding two models from a single training run. \DIG is organized around a \emph{feature assignment taxonomy}: item-intrinsic static features are encoded into SIDs, user-item cross features (u2i) implicitly drive codebook boundaries toward recommendation decision boundaries during training, and an MLP$_\mathrm{u2t}$ distillation module approximates u2i at the token level for inference. Experiments on three public benchmarks and two industrial datasets demonstrate that \DIG simultaneously improves ranking, retrieval, and unified retrieval-ranking quality.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [80] [A Hardware-Aware, Per-Layer Methodology for Post-Training Quantization of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14929)
*Earl Killian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SOP是一种新型LLM后训练量化方案，通过结合分块缩放、混合码本搜索及高效硬件格式，在6.5 bpw下实现了优于传统8位量化（FP8）的重构精度，有效降低了大模型的存储与计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决大语言模型（LLM）在4.5-6位低比特量化下的精度损失问题，以降低模型权重存储与推理成本，同时针对支持每层查找表（LUT）解码的硬件进行优化。

Method: 提出Scaled Outer Product (SOP)量化方法，包含：1.基于分块的混合码本搜索（包含预定义码本NF4, BOF4等与动态码本DD4）；2.引入硬件高效的查找表输出格式（HIF）；3.结合激活加权余弦相似度选择与多选背包算法对敏感层进行梯度提升，以及离群值和稀疏残差校正。

Result: 在六个开源模型系列上验证，SOP方法在6.5 bpw配置下实现了比传统每层POT FP8（8.0 bpw）更低的权重重构误差，在节省1.5 bpw存储空间的同时保持了近乎无损的保真度。

Conclusion: SOP方法通过结合分块量化、优化的码本选择及硬件友好的LUT格式，在大参数量模型中实现了比传统FP8（8.0 bpw）更低的重构误差，同时降低了存储成本（6.5 bpw），证明了块缩放小原子精度方案在量化推理中的优越性。

Abstract: Scaled Outer Product (SOP) is a post-training quantization methodology for large language model weights, designed to deliver near-lossless fidelity at 4.5--6 bits per weight on hardware with per-layer LUT decode. The methodology combines per-layer search of fixed and dynamic codebook pairs selected by a per-block selection bit, signed per-block scales, activation-weighted cosine selection, and multiple-choice knapsack promotion of sensitive layers with outlier and sparse-residual correction. Fixed codebooks include NF4, BOF4, Split87, and SH4; per-layer optimized codebooks (DD4) are hosted in LUT SRAM. A new hardware-efficient LUT output format (HIF) is proposed to improve performance, energy, and cost. Across six open model families, the recommended FP6 operating point (E2M3sUE4M4, 6.5 bpw) achieves lower weight reconstruction error than the conventional per-layer-POT FP8 baseline (E4M3, 8.0 bpw) at 1.5 bpw lower storage cost, demonstrating that block-scaled small atoms with carefully chosen scale precision can replace conventionally-deployed FP8. Full evaluation across the 4.5--6 bpw range, including layer promotion and sparse residual correction, is reported in a companion paper.

</details>


### [81] [Vision-Based Runtime Monitoring under Varying Specifications using Semantic Latent Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13923)
*Bardh Hoxha,Oliver Schön,Hideki Okamoto,Lars Lindemann,Georgios Fainekos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向视觉观测的ptSTL运行时监控框架，通过“语义基”或“滚动预测”结合共形校准，实现了无需重训即可认证多种时序公式的鲁棒性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在视觉观测的条件下，实现对部分可观测环境下的过去时信号时序逻辑（ptSTL）进行可证明的运行时监控，并解决监控器在面对不同公式时需要重复训练与校准的效率问题。

Method: 1. 引入“语义基”方法：将视觉观测映射为原子谓词的鲁棒性分数，通过确定性解码器评估任意公式。
2. 引入“滚动预测监控器”：仅预测当前谓词值并在线重构历史。
3. 利用共形预测（Conformal Prediction）进行校准，实现有限样本的安全性保证，无需进行联合边界处理。

Result: 在行人交叉路口基准测试和Waymo自动驾驶数据集上验证：滚动监控器在短时间跨度内表现更优，而语义基监控器在长跨度下精确度高出4倍；两种方法均成功满足了统计学的共形覆盖准则。

Conclusion: 研究证明了“语义基（semantic basis）”是实现可重用、单调、1-Lipschitz接口的最小预测目标。提出的两种监控器（语义基监控器与滚动预测监控器）均能提供有效的共形覆盖保证，且在不同时间跨度下具有互补的性能优势。

Abstract: We study certified runtime monitoring of past-time signal temporal logic (ptSTL) from visual observations under partial observability. The monitor must infer safety-relevant quantities from images and provide finite-sample guarantees, while being \emph{reusable}: once trained and calibrated, it should certify any formula in a target fragment without per-formula retraining. For fragments induced by a finite dictionary of temporal atoms, we prove that the \emph{semantic basis}, the vector of atom robustness scores, is the minimum prediction target within the class of monotone, 1-Lipschitz reusable interfaces: any formula is evaluated by a deterministic decoder derived from the parse tree, and a single conformal calibration pass certifies the entire fragment with no union bound. We also introduce a \emph{rolling prediction monitor} that predicts only current predicate values and reconstructs temporal history online; this is easier to learn but grows conservative at long horizons. On a pedestrian-crossroad benchmark, rolling achieves tighter certified bounds at short horizons while the semantic-basis monitor is up to 4-times tighter at long horizons. We validate the presented monitors on real-world Waymo driving data, where both monitors satisfy the conformal coverage guarantee empirically.

</details>


### [82] [An Interpretable Latency Model for Speculative Decoding in LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15051)
*Linghao Kong,Megan Flynn,Michael Peng,Nir Shavit,Mark Kurtz,Alexandre Marques*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了一套评估推测解码在生产环境中延迟的模型，揭示了系统负载、批处理大小与推测解码加速效果之间的内在联系，为生产环境中的配置决策提供了优化指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测解码在实验室环境下表现出色，但其在生产服务系统（请求负载动态变化、批处理大小非固定）中的行为缺乏深入研究和理解。

Method: 提出了一种简单且可解释的推测解码（SD）延迟模型；利用利特尔法则（Little's Law）从请求速率推导有效批处理大小；将每个请求的需求分解为预填充（prefill）、草稿（drafting）和验证（verification）各阶段中与负载相关及无关的组件。

Result: 模型在各种模型规模、请求长度和请求速率下具有高准确性；通过实验验证了负载增加如何导致推测加速比下降；量化了草稿长度、接受率和模型规模对系统延迟的影响，并将其扩展至混合专家模型（MoE）。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个结构化的框架，用于理解和评估推测解码在实际生产服务系统中的性能表现，解释了负载增加导致加速效果减弱的原因，并为生产环境下的配置优化提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Speculative decoding (SD) accelerates large language model (LLM) inference by using a smaller draft model to propose multiple tokens that are verified by a larger target model in parallel. While prior work demonstrates substantial speedups in isolated or fixed-batch settings, the behavior of SD in production serving systems remains poorly understood: request load varies over time, and effective batch size emerges from the serving system rather than being directly controlled or observed. In this work, we develop a simple and interpretable latency model for SD in LLM serving. We infer effective batch size from request rate using Little's Law and decompose per-request demand into load-independent and load-dependent components for prefill, drafting, and verification. We validate our model using extensive measurements from vLLM across verifier and drafter model sizes, prefill and decode lengths, request rates, draft lengths, and acceptance probabilities. The model accurately describes observed latency, explains why speedups often diminish as server load increases, and characterizes how draft length, acceptance rate, and verifier-drafter size shape latency across serving conditions, with implications for configuring SD in deployed systems. We further show how the framework extends to mixture of experts models, where sparse expert activation changes the effective service costs across load regimes. Together, our results provide a structured framework for understanding SD in real LLM serving systems.

</details>


### [83] [Mechanistic Interpretability of EEG Foundation Models via Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13930)
*William Lehn-Schiøler,Magnus Ruud Kjær,Rahul Thapa,Magnus Guldberg Pedersen,Anton Storgaard Mosquera,Nick Williams,Radu Gatej,Tue Lehn-Schiøler,Sándor Beniczky,Sadasivan Puthusserypady,James Zou,Lars Kai Hansen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过稀疏自编码器揭示了EEG Transformer的内部机制，并实现了临床可解释的特征控制，为解决模型“黑盒”问题和评估临床代表性提供了量化框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管EEG基础模型在临床表现上达到顶尖水平，但其内部计算机制不透明，阻碍了临床医疗中的信任与落地应用，亟需对其进行可解释性分析。

Method: 使用了TopK稀疏自编码器（SAE）对SleepFM、REVE和LaBraM三种EEG Transformer进行特征提取；采用临床分类法对特征进行归因；提出了“目标与非目标”探针区域指标以评估概念引导（Concept Steering）的有效性；并引入频谱解码器将潜空间干预映射回生理频率特征。

Result: 研究成功提取了稀疏特征字典；确立了一种跨架构通用的字典健康审计方案；识别出了三种操作机制（可选择性引导、已编码但纠缠、未编码）；揭示了部分导致模型性能崩溃的“破坏性”干预及难以解耦的临床混淆现象；实现了对特定生理频率（如病理性慢波抑制和α波恢复）的定向控制。

Conclusion: 本研究通过SAE成功解构了EEG基础模型的内部特征表示，提供了可解释性工具，揭示了临床表征中的混淆现象（如年龄与病理学纠缠），并实现了通过潜在空间操控精确调节生理信号的频谱特性。

Abstract: EEG foundation models achieve state-of-the-art clinical performance, yet the internal computations driving their predictions remain opaque: a barrier to clinical trust. We apply TopK Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) across three architecturally distinct EEG transformers: SleepFM, REVE, and LaBraM to extract sparse feature dictionaries from their embeddings. By grounding these features in a clinical taxonomy (abnormality, age, sex, and medication), we benchmark monosemanticity and entanglement across architectures. A single hyperparameter procedure, driven by an intrinsic dictionary health audit, transfers robustly across all three architectures. Via concept steering, we introduce a "target vs. off-target" probe area metric to quantify steering selectivity and reveal three operational regimes: selectively steerable, encoded but entangled, and non-encoded. This framework exposes critical representational failures: "wrecking-ball" interventions that collapse global model performance, and clinical entanglements, such as age-pathology confounding, where it is impossible to suppress one concept without corrupting the other. Finally, a spectral decoder maps these interventions back to the amplitude spectrum, translating latent manipulations into physiologically interpretable frequency signatures, such as pathological slow-wave suppression and $α$-band restoration.

</details>


### [84] [Rethinking Molecular OOD Generalization via Target-Aware Source Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13932)
*Zhuohao Lin,Kun Li,Jiameng Chen,Jiajun Yu,Duanhua Cao,Yizhen Zheng,Wenbin Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 AI 药物研发中极端 OOD 场景预测不准的问题，本文提出了更严格的基准 SCOPE-BENCH 和基于强化学习的领域自适应框架 POMA，有效提升了模型在分子结构分布偏移下的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的支架划分（scaffold-splitting）方法存在语义重叠，导致模型产生捷径学习（shortcut learning）并高估泛化能力；同时，传统领域自适应方法在面对极端结构偏移时易引入噪声和负迁移。

Method: 1. 提出 SCOPE-BENCH：基于物理化学描述符空间的聚类划分基准，用于更严苛的 OOD 评估。
2. 提出 POMA 框架：一种 Retrieve-Compose-Adapt 管道，利用强化学习进行源域数据选择，并在宏观拓扑和微观药效团双尺度上进行领域自适应。

Result: 在 SCOPE-BENCH 上，现有 SOTA 3D 分子模型预测误差激增（高达 8.0 倍）；POMA 框架在不同骨干架构上平均相对提升 6.2%，MAE 最大降低 11.2%。

Conclusion: SCOPE-BENCH 揭示了现有分子属性预测模型在极端 OOD 场景下的脆弱性。所提出的 POMA 框架通过检索、组合与适应的策略，有效缓解了负迁移问题，显著提升了模型在结构分布外数据上的预测鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Robust prediction of molecular properties under extreme out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios is a pivotal bottleneck in AI-driven drug discovery. Current scaffold-splitting protocols fail to obstruct microscopic semantic overlap, predisposing models to shortcut learning and overestimating their true extrapolation capability; meanwhile, conventional domain adaptation paradigms suffer under extreme structural shifts, as blindly aligning heterogeneous source libraries injects topological noise and triggers negative transfer. To address these two challenges, scaffold-cluster out-of-distribution performance evaluation benchmark (SCOPE-BENCH), a benchmark built on cluster-level partitioning in an explicit physicochemical descriptor space, is proposed alongside policy optimization for multi-source adaptation (POMA), a framework that formulates knowledge transfer as a retrieve-compose-adapt pipeline: labeled source scaffolds structurally close to the unlabeled target are first identified as proxy targets; a reinforcement learning policy then adaptively selects the optimal source subset from an exponentially large candidate pool; and dual-scale domain adaptation is finally performed at macroscopic topological and microscopic pharmacophore scales. Evaluations show that prediction errors of state-of-the-art 3D molecular models surge by up to 8.0x on SCOPE-BENCH with a mean of 5.9x, while POMA achieves up to an 11.2% reduction in mean absolute error with an average relative improvement of 6.2% across diverse backbone architectures. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Molecular-OOD-Code-73F6.

</details>


### [85] [Unsupervised learning of acquisition variability in structural connectomes via hybrid latent space modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13933)
*Gaurav Rudravaram,Lianrui Zuo,Karthik Ramadass,Elyssa McMaster,Jongyeon Yoon,Aravind R. Krishnan,Adam M. Saunders,Chenyu Gao,Nancy R. Newlin,Praitayini Kanakaraj,Lori L. Beason Held,Murat Bilgel,Laura A. Barquero,Micah DArchangel,Tin Q. Nguyen,Laurie B. Cutting,Derek Archer,Timothy J. Hohman,Daniel C. Moyer,Bennett A. Landman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多中心dMRI数据的连接组分析，本文提出了一种无需手动调参的混合连续-离散潜在空间架构，利用架构退火机制有效解耦了采集误差与生物特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dMRI数据在不同站点、扫描仪和协议间存在显著的获取差异，这干扰了结构连接组分析；现有方法往往将获取效应错误地混入连续潜在空间，或依赖于需要手动调节的混合模型。

Method: 提出了一种无监督的混合潜在空间框架，通过在编码器输出端引入“架构退火”机制，无需手动调整容量即可自适应地平衡离散部分（获取效应）和连续部分（生物学变异）的权重。

Result: 在包含7,416个结构连接组的大规模数据集上，该模型在获取效应学习方面表现更优（ARI=0.53），成功解耦了站点及协议特征与生物学特征，且其聚类结果与扫描仪差异高度吻合。

Conclusion: 通过架构退火（architectural annealing）实现的混合连续-离散潜在空间模型，能够有效且自动地分离弥散磁共振成像（dMRI）中的获取变异与生物学变异，优于传统的损失函数退火方法。

Abstract: Acquisition differences across sites, scanners, and protocols in dMRI introduce variability that complicates structural connectome analysis. This motivates deep learning models that can represent high-dimensional connectomes in a low-dimensional space while explicitly separating acquisition-related effects from biological variation. Conventional dimensionality reduction methods model all variance as continuous, so acquisition effects often get absorbed into a continuous latent space. Recent hybrid latent-space models combine discrete and continuous components to address this, but typically require manual capacity tuning to ensure the discrete component captures the intended variability. We introduce an unsupervised framework that removes this manual tuning by architecturally annealing encoder outputs before decoding, allowing the model to adaptively balance discrete and continuous latent variables during training. To evaluate it, we curated a dataset of N=7,416 structural connectomes derived from dMRI, spanning ages 2 to 102 and 13 studies with 25 unique acquisition-parameter combinations. Of these, 5,900 are cognitively unimpaired, 877 have mild cognitive impairment (MCI), and 639 have Alzheimer's disease (AD). We compare against a standard VAE, PCA with k-means clustering, and hybrid models that anneal only through the loss function. Our architectural annealing produces stronger site learning (ARI=0.53, p<0.05) than these baselines. Results show that a hybrid continuous-discrete latent space, with architectural rather than loss-based annealing, provides a useful unsupervised mechanism for capturing acquisition variability in dMRI: by jointly modeling smooth and categorical structure, the Joint-VAE recovers clusters aligned with scanner and protocol differences.

</details>


### [86] [Towards the Next Frontier of LLMs, Training on Private Data: A Cross-Domain Benchmark for Federated Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13936)
*Daniel M. Jimenez-Gutierrez,Enrique Zuazua,Georgios Kellaris,Joaquin del Rio,Oleksii Sliusarenko,Xabi Uribe-Etxebarria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种利用联邦学习与 PEFT 技术在数据隐私受限场景下联合微调大模型的方法，证明了在不共享原始数据的前提下，通过联邦 PEFT 即可有效利用分散的异构私有数据提升 LLM 的领域表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型的发展受限于公共数据。许多具有高价值的数据（医疗、金融）因隐私、合规与数据孤岛问题无法共享，且这些数据本质上是非独立同分布的，现有技术难以有效利用这些私有数据提升模型专业能力。

Method: 利用 Sherpa.ai 联邦学习平台，在不交换私有数据的前提下，对共享 LLM 进行联合参数高效微调（PEFT，包括 LoRA, QLoRA, IA3）。通过 MedQA, MedMCQA, FPB, FiQA-SA 等数据集评估在非独立同分布（non-IID）环境下的模型性能。

Result: 联邦微调的效果接近集中式训练，且显著优于单机构孤立学习；QLoRA 和 IA3 在保持较高准确率的同时，从 Green AI（绿色 AI）的角度大幅提高了计算和资源效率。

Conclusion: 联邦化参数高效微调（Federated PEFT）是一种在保护隐私、跨机构非独立同分布（non-IID）数据的限制下，利用分布式私有数据实现大语言模型（LLM）定制化和领域知识增强的可行性策略。

Abstract: The recent success of large language models (LLMs) has been largely driven by vast public datasets. However, the next frontier for LLM development lies beyond public data. Much of the world's most valuable information is private, especially in highly regulated sectors such as healthcare and finance, where data include patient histories or customer communications. Unlocking this data could represent a major leap forward, enabling LLMs with deeper domain expertise and stronger real-world utility. Yet, these data cannot be shared because they are distributed across institutions and constrained by privacy, regulatory, and organizational barriers. Moreover, institutional datasets are typically non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID), differing across sites in population characteristics, data modalities, documentation patterns, and task-specific label distributions.
  In this paper, we demonstrate a practical approach to unlocking private and distributed institutional data for LLM adaptation through federated collaboration across data silos. Built on the Sherpa.ai Federated Learning platform, our framework enables nodes to jointly fine-tune a shared LLM without exchanging private data. We evaluate this approach through a cross-domain benchmark in healthcare and finance, using four closed-ended question answering and classification datasets: MedQA, MedMCQA, FPB, and FiQA-SA. We compare three parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) strategies-LoRA, QLoRA, and IA3-across pretrained backbones under non-IID settings reflecting institutional data heterogeneity. Our results show that federated fine-tuning performs close to centralized training and outperforms isolated single-institution learning. From a Green AI perspective, QLoRA and IA3 improve efficiency with limited accuracy degradation, supporting federated PEFT as a viable approach for adapting LLMs where data cannot be shared.

</details>


### [87] [EvolveMem:Self-Evolving Memory Architecture via AutoResearch for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13941)
*Jiaqi Liu,Xinyu Ye,Peng Xia,Zeyu Zheng,Cihang Xie,Mingyu Ding,Huaxiu Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EvolveMem 是一种自动演进的 LLM 记忆架构，通过 LLM 自我诊断和动态调整检索配置，实现了检索系统与存储内容的共同进化，显著提升了记忆检索的准确性和适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的长短期记忆系统在部署后通常是静态的，缺乏针对存储内容变化而调整检索策略（如评分函数、融合策略）的能力，导致系统难以适应动态增长的记忆。

Method: 提出了 EvolveMem 架构，将检索配置视为结构化动作空间，利用 LLM 驱动的诊断模块根据失败日志分析并调整检索策略，配套带有自动回滚和探索机制的防护性元分析器。

Result: 在 LoCoMo 基准测试中，较最强基准提升了 25.7%；在 MemBench 中提升了 18.9%。系统能够从最小参数集实现自主收敛，且进化策略在不同任务间表现出正向迁移。

Conclusion: EvolveMem 通过闭环自进化机制证明了检索系统可以自动实现适应性优化，且进化出的策略具有良好的跨任务泛化能力，能够捕获通用的检索原则。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for LLM agents that operate across multiple sessions, yet existing memory systems treat retrieval infrastructure as fixed: stored content evolves while scoring functions, fusion strategies, and answer-generation policies remain frozen at deployment. We argue that truly adaptive memory requires co-evolution at two levels: the stored knowledge and the retrieval mechanism that queries it. We present EvolveMem, a self-evolving memory architecture that exposes its full retrieval configuration as a structured action space optimized by an LLM-powered diagnosis module. In each evolution round, the module reads per-question failure logs, identifies root causes, and proposes targeted configuration adjustments; a guarded meta-analyzer applies them with automatic revert-on-regression and explore-on-stagnation safeguards. This closed-loop self-evolution realizes an AutoResearch process: the system autonomously conducts iterative research cycles on its own architecture, replacing manual configuration tuning. Starting from a minimal baseline, the process converges autonomously, discovering effective retrieval strategies including entirely new configuration dimensions not present in the original action space. On LoCoMo, EvolveMem outperforms the strongest baseline by 25.7% relative and achieves a 78.0% relative improvement over the minimal baseline. On MemBench, EvolveMem exceeds the strongest baseline by 18.9% relative. Evolved configurations transfer across benchmarks with positive rather than catastrophic transfer, indicating that the self-evolution process captures universal retrieval principles rather than benchmark-specific heuristics. Code is available at https://github.com/aiming-lab/SimpleMem.

</details>


### [88] [A Unified Geometric Framework for Weighted Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13943)
*Raphael Vock,Edouard Duchesnay,Benoit Dufumier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过距离几何视角揭示了对比学习加权方案对嵌入空间结构的决定性影响，解释了SupCon和Soft SupCon在类别不平衡下的几何差异，并为设计几何鲁棒的对比学习目标函数提供了理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对比学习（CL）领域对所得嵌入表征的几何形态缺乏深刻理解，导致目标函数设计缺乏原则性，且在特殊设置（如类别不平衡或连续标签）下容易出现优化失效或退化。

Method: 将加权InfoNCE目标函数解释为“距离几何问题”（Distance Geometry Problems），通过分析加权方案如何约束样本在潜在空间的几何布局，推导出不同监督及弱监督目标函数下最优嵌入的解析解。

Result: 1. 证明了SupCon在类别不平衡下会导致非均衡的类间相似度，而Soft SupCon在任何分布下均能保持正则单纯形（regular simplex）几何结构；2. 指出了y-Aware CL在标签未分布于超球面上时的几何失配问题；3. 提出了实现几何一致性的加权设计原则，例如Euclidean-Euclidean加权可获得确定性最优嵌入。

Conclusion: 对比学习中的加权方案决定了表征空间几何结构的实现性与性质。研究揭示了加权方案与目标几何结构之间的映射关系，为设计具有良好几何属性（如有序、对称、规整）的对比学习目标函数提供了理论指导，指出Soft SupCon在处理类别不平衡时的鲁棒性优于SupCon。

Abstract: Contrastive learning (CL) aims to preserve relational structure between samples by learning representations that reflect a similarity graph. Yet, the geometry of the resulting embeddings remains poorly understood. Here we show that weighted InfoNCE objectives can be interpreted as Distance Geometry Problems, where the weighting scheme specifies the target geometry to be realized by the representation. This viewpoint yields exact characterizations of the optimal embeddings for several supervised and weakly supervised objectives. In supervised classification, both SupCon and Soft SupCon (a dense relaxation of it where pairs from distinct classes have small non-zero similarity) collapse samples within each class to a single prototype. However, while balanced SupCon recovers the classical regular simplex geometry, class imbalance breaks this symmetry: SupCon induces non-uniform inter-class similarities depending on class sizes, whereas Soft SupCon preserves a regular simplex geometry regardless of class imbalance. In continuous-label settings, our framework reveals a different failure mode: y-Aware CL generally cannot attain its entropic optimum unless the labels lie on a hypersphere, exposing a mismatch between Euclidean label weights and spherical latent similarity. By contrast, geometrically consistent choices such as Euclidean-Euclidean weighting or X-CLR admit unique optimal embeddings. Our results show that the choice of weighting scheme determines whether contrastive learning is geometrically realizable, degenerate, or inconsistent, providing a principled framework for designing contrastive objectives.

</details>


### [89] [Collider-Bench: Benchmarking AI Agents with Particle Physics Analysis Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13950)
*Darius A. Faroughy,Sofia Palacios Schweitzer,Ian Pang,Siddharth Mishra-Sharma,David Shih*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Collider-Bench，一个旨在挑战LLM智能体复现大型强子对撞机（LHC）实验分析的科学基准，测试结果显示当前智能体在科学推理和处理遗漏实验细节方面表现有限，尚无法替代人工物理学家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估大型语言模型（LLM）智能体的基准测试大多忽略了真实科学工作中的复杂性和微妙之处（如缺乏实现指导、工具链差异等），亟需一套能评估智能体在开放环境下进行科学复现能力的基准。

Method: 提出了Collider-Bench基准测试，要求智能体将已发表的论文转化为可执行的模拟与选择流水线，并使用标准直方图统计指标评估预测产额，同时通过LLM裁判模型分析完整操作痕迹以检测幻觉和制造虚假证据等问题。

Result: 在Collider-Bench的测试中，平均而言，没有任何已有的通用编程智能体能够稳定超越“物理学家参与（physicist-in-the-loop）”的方案。

Conclusion: 目前的AI智能体尚无法可靠地替代物理学家完成复杂的LHC实验分析任务，现有模型在处理缺乏详细实现细节的科学任务时表现不足。

Abstract: Autonomous language-model agents are increasingly evaluated on long-horizon tool-use tasks, but existing benchmarks rarely capture the complexity and nuance of real scientific work. To address this gap, we introduce Collider-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating whether LLM agents can reproduce experimental analyses from the Large Hadron Collider (LHC) using only public papers and open scientific software. Such analyses are often difficult to reproduce because the public toolchain only approximates the software used internally by the experimental collaborations, while the published papers inevitably omit implementation details needed for a faithful reconstruction. Agents must therefore rely on physical reasoning, domain knowledge, and trial-and-error to fill these gaps. Each task requires the agent to turn a published analysis into an executable simulation-and-selection pipeline and submit predicted collision event yields in specified signal regions. These predictions are evaluated with standard histogram metrics that provide continuous fidelity scores without a hand-written rubric. We also report the computational cost incurred by each agent per task. Finally, we evaluate the codebase and full session trace using an LLM judge to catch qualitative failure modes such as fabrications, hallucinations and duplications. We release an initial set of tasks drawn from LHC searches, together with a containerized sandbox and event simulation tools. We evaluate across a capability ladder of general purpose coding agents. Our results show that on average no agent reliably beats the physicist-in-the-loop solution.

</details>


### [90] [WarmPrior: Straightening Flow-Matching Policies with Temporal Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13959)
*Sinjae Kang,Chanyoung Kim,Kaixin Wang,Li Zhao,Kimin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究提出WarmPrior，通过在生成式机器人控制中引入动作历史先验，替代高斯分布，从而提升了任务成功率、优化了模型路径效率，并增强了强化学习的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散/流匹配机器人控制策略多采用标准高斯分布作为先验，忽略了动作空间的时间相关性，限制了模型学习路径的效率和最终任务成功率。

Method: 提出WarmPrior，一种利用近期动作历史信息构建的、具有时间依赖性的先验分布，并将其应用于基准行为克隆及基于先验空间的强化学习中。

Result: 在机器人操作任务中，WarmPrior显著提升了成功率，通过引导生成模型实现更平直的概率路径（类似于矩流中的最优传输耦合），并有效改善了强化学习的采样效率与最终性能。

Conclusion: 将扩散/流匹配模型中的高斯先验替换为基于动作历史的WarmPrior，是提升生成式机器人控制性能的关键且被忽视的设计维度。

Abstract: Generative policies based on diffusion and flow matching have become a dominant paradigm for visuomotor robotic control. We show that replacing the standard Gaussian source distribution with WarmPrior, a simple temporally grounded prior constructed from readily available recent action history, consistently improves success rates on robotic manipulation tasks. We trace this gain to markedly straighter probability paths, echoing the effect of optimal-transport couplings in Rectified Flow. Beyond standard behavior cloning, WarmPrior also reshapes the exploration distribution in prior-space reinforcement learning, improving both sample efficiency and final performance. Collectively, these results identify the source distribution as an important and underexplored design axis in generative robot control.

</details>


### [91] [The Geometry of LLM Quantization: GPTQ as Babai's Nearest Plane Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.18553)
*Jiale Chen,Yalda Shabanzadeh,Elvir Crnčević,Torsten Hoefler,Dan Alistarh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过数学证明揭示了 GPTQ 本质上是格论中 Babai 最邻近平面算法的一种，通过理论溯源，不仅为 GPTQ 提供了几何解释和误差保证，还借此改进了量化算法并实现了性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPTQ 作为大模型量化的标准方法，其内部机制长期以来仅被视为代数更新序列，缺乏几何解释和严谨的误差边界，限制了对其潜力的进一步挖掘。

Method: 将 GPTQ 的权重更新过程与格论中的“最近向量问题（CVP）”建立数学联系，证明当 GPTQ 反向执行时，其过程与 Babai 最邻近平面算法一致；并基于此发现设计了无权重剪切的量化改进算法及高效 GPU 推理内核。

Result: 成功将 GPTQ 映射至 Babai 算法，为其误差传播提供了几何解释；推导出了新的误差上界；基于不剪切权重策略的改进方法在性能上优于原始 GPTQ；提供了配套的高效 GPU 推理内核。

Conclusion: 该研究为 GPTQ 提供了坚实的理论基础，证明其等同于格密码学中的 Babai 最邻近平面算法，并利用该视角改进了量化方法，不仅提升了性能，还为利用格论研究成果优化大模型量化开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Quantizing the weights of large language models (LLMs) from 16-bit to lower bitwidth is the de facto approach to deploy massive transformers onto more affordable accelerators. While GPTQ emerged as one of the standard methods for one-shot post-training quantization at LLM scale, its inner workings are described as a sequence of algebraic updates that obscure geometric meaning or worst-case guarantees. In this work, we show that, when executed back-to-front (from the last to first dimension) for a linear layer, GPTQ is mathematically identical to Babai's nearest plane algorithm for the classical closest vector problem (CVP) on a lattice defined by the Hessian matrix of the layer's inputs. This equivalence is based on a sophisticated mathematical argument, and has two analytical consequences: first, the GPTQ error propagation step gains an intuitive geometric interpretation; second, GPTQ inherits the error upper bound of Babai's algorithm under the assumption that no weights are clipped. Leveraging this bound, we design post-training quantization methods that avoid clipping, and outperform the original GPTQ. In addition, we provide efficient GPU inference kernels for the resulting representation. Taken together, these results place GPTQ on a firm theoretical footing and open the door to importing decades of progress in lattice algorithms towards the design of future quantization algorithms for billion-parameter models. Source code is available at https://github.com/IST-DASLab/GPTQ-Babai.

</details>


### [92] [Towards Resource-Efficient LLMs: End-to-End Energy Accounting of Distillation Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13981)
*Katherine Lambert,Sasha Luccioni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个针对大模型蒸馏的全流程能源核算框架，指出传统评估忽略了教师侧成本；通过对两种常见蒸馏方法的实证分析，构建了能效-性能帕累托前沿，旨在推动蒸馏研究的能源透明化与标准化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前关于大模型蒸馏的研究往往忽略了“教师侧”工作负载（如数据生成、日志缓存、评估）带来的隐藏能源成本，导致模型效率的评估存在偏差。需要一套标准化的全流程能源核算方案。

Method: 构建了全面的能源核算框架，通过分阶段追踪GPU功耗，系统测量了基于Logit的知识蒸馏（KD）和基于合成数据的监督微调（SFT）的能源消耗与碳排放，并绘制了能效-质量帕累托前沿。

Result: 揭示了此前被忽视的蒸馏过程中的能源成本，并量化了不同方法在该成本下的性能表现，为在特定预算和能耗约束下选择蒸馏策略提供了科学模型。

Conclusion: 蒸馏不仅是模型压缩手段，还涉及显著的能源支出，研究强调了在评估时必须纳入教师模型推理、数据生成和评估等全生命周期成本。该研究提供了实用的设计规则和评估工具，以在能源约束下实现最佳的“能效-性能”权衡。

Abstract: The rise in deployment of large language models has driven a surge in GPU demand and datacenter scaling, raising concerns about electricity use, grid stress, and the impacts of modern AI workloads. Distillation is often promoted as one of the most effective paths to obtain cheaper, more efficient models, yet these claims rarely account for the full end-to-end energy and resource costs, including crucial teacher-side workloads such as data generation, logit caching, and evaluation. We present a comprehensive energy accounting framework that measures the complete computational cost of distillation pipelines via detailed stage-wise tracking of GPU device power consumption. In our experiments, we separate and log empirical energy use across distinct phases and systematically measure the energy and emissions of two common distillation methods: the classic logit-based knowledge distillation and synthetic-data supervised fine-tuning, constructing energy-quality Pareto frontiers that expose the previously ignored costs. From these measurements and analyses, we derive practical design rules for selecting distillation methods and hyperparameters under energy and budget constraints, and release an open-source measurement harness and accounting protocol to provide a standardized foundation for comparable, reproducible distillation research, explicitly accountable for complete pipeline energy impact.

</details>


### [93] [Finite Sample Bounds for Learning with Score Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14168)
*Devin Smedira,Abhijith Jayakumar,Sidhant Misra,Marc Vuffray,Andrey Y. Lokhov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次为多项式指数族模型的分数匹配学习方法提供了严谨的非渐近样本复杂度分析，填补了该领域仅有渐近理论的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分数匹配方法虽然在计算上比极大似然估计更简便，但缺乏关于其统计性质的严格理论保证，且以往的研究大多仅局限于渐近分析。

Method: 采用非渐近分析技术（Non-asymptotic analysis）对分数匹配（Score matching）估计器进行理论推导。

Result: 推导出了多项式指数族模型在分数匹配下的非渐近样本复杂度边界，显示了其与模型维度之间存在多项式依赖关系。

Conclusion: 本文首次为基于分数匹配学习多项式指数族模型提供了非渐近样本复杂度边界，证明了该方法在学习高维模型结构时具有多项式级别的复杂度。

Abstract: Learning of continuous exponential family distributions with unbounded support remains an important area of research for both theory and applications in high-dimensional statistics. In recent years, score matching has become a widely used method for learning exponential families with continuous variables due to its computational ease when compared against maximum likelihood estimation. However, theoretical understanding of the statistical properties of score matching is still lacking. In this work, we provide a non-asymptotic sample complexity analysis for learning the structure of exponential families of polynomials with score matching. The derived sample bounds show a polynomial dependence on the model dimension. These bounds are the first of its kind, as all prior work has shown only asymptotic bounds on the sample complexity.

</details>


### [94] [TabPFN-3: Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13986)
*Léo Grinsztajn,Klemens Flöge,Oscar Key,Felix Birkel,Philipp Jund,Brendan Roof,Mihir Manium,Shi Bin,Hoo,Magnus Bühler,Anurag Garg,Dominik Safaric,Jake Robertson,Benjamin Jäger,Simone Alessi,Adrian Hayler,Vladyslav Moroshan,Lennart Purucker,Philipp Singer,Alan Arazi,Julien Siems,Jan Hendrik Metzen,Georg Grab,Nick Erickson,Siyuan Guo,Eliott Kalfon,Simon Bing,David Salinas,Clara Cornu,Lilly Charlotte Wehrhahn,Diana Kriuchkova,Kursat Kaya,Lydia Sidhoum,Marie Salmon,Jerry Chen,Madelon Hulsebos,Yann LeCun,Samuel Müller,Bernhard Schölkopf,Sauraj Gambhir,Noah Hollmann,Frank Hutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabPFN-3是一款高效的表格预训练基础模型，通过架构创新与测试时计算扩展，在仅使用合成数据训练的情况下，实现了百万级样本下的SOTA性能，且推理速度与计算效率远超主流集成学习工具（如AutoGluon）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格模型在处理超大规模数据集（百万行级）时存在训练时间长、推理速度慢且性能瓶颈等问题，旨在通过提升可扩展性与效率，解决高价值表格预测场景中的实际需求。

Method: 1. 延续纯合成数据预训练范式；2. 架构优化：采用行分块（row-chunking）技术与减小的KV缓存以支持1M行数据扩展；3. 引入测试时计算扩展（TabPFN-3-Plus），通过推理环节的计算投入进一步提升性能；4. 针对特定任务优化，提供时间序列（TabPFN-TS-3）及跨模态（关系型、表格-文本）专用指标。

Result: 1. 性能：在TabArena基准上显著超越 tuned/ensembled 基准；在多分类、大样本（1M行/200特征）任务上超越8小时调优的GBDT；在RelBenchV1和TabSTAR上达到SOTA。2. 效率：相比TabPFN-2.5提速达20倍，SHAP值计算提速120倍，在单个H100上实现百万行数据的快速推理。

Conclusion: TabPFN-3实现了TabPFN在处理更大规模数据集上的突破，通过引入测试时计算扩展（test-time compute scaling）和架构优化，在不依赖LLM或真实预训练数据的情况下，在处理百万级行数据时展现出优于AutoGluon等强基准的性能与速度表现，确立了其在 tabular 基础模型领域的领先地位。

Abstract: Tabular data underpins most high-value prediction problems in science and industry, and TabPFN has driven the foundation model revolution for this modality. Designed with feedback from our users, TabPFN-3 builds on this foundation to scale state-of-the-art performance to datasets with 1M training rows and substantially reduce training and inference time. Pretrained exclusively on synthetic data from our prior, TabPFN-3 dramatically pushes the frontier of tabular prediction and brings substantial gains on time series, relational, and tabular-text data. On the standard tabular benchmark TabArena, a forward pass of TabPFN-3 outperforms all other models, including tuned and ensembled baselines, by a significant margin, and pareto-dominates the speed/performance frontier. On more diverse datasets, TabPFN-3 ranks first on datasets with many classes, and beats 8-hour-tuned gradient-boosted-tree baselines on datasets up to 1M training rows and 200 features. TabPFN-3 introduces test-time compute scaling to tabular foundation models. Our API offering TabPFN-3-Plus (Thinking) exploits this to beat all non-TabPFN models by over 200 Elo on TabArena, rising to 420 Elo on the largest data subset, and outperforms AutoGluon 1.5 extreme while being 10x faster, without using LLMs, real data, internet search or any other model besides TabPFN. TabPFN-3 extends the capabilities of our models, enabling SOTA prediction on relational data (new SOTA foundation model on RelBenchV1) and tabular-text data (SOTA on TabSTAR via TabPFN-3-Plus); and improves existing integrations: a specialized checkpoint, TabPFN-TS-3, ranks 2nd on the time-series benchmark fev-bench, and SHAP-value computation is up to 120x faster. TabPFN-3 achieves this performance while being up to 20x faster than TabPFN-2.5. In addition, a reduced KV cache and row-chunking scale to 1M rows on one H100 with fast inference speed.

</details>


### [95] [HodgeCover: Higher-Order Topological Coverage Drives Compression of Sparse Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13997)
*Tao Zhong,Dongzhe Zheng,Christine Allen-Blanchette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 Sparse MoE 模型免训练压缩中存在的“三专家循环合并阻塞”问题，本文提出了基于霍奇分解（Hodge decomposition）的 HodgeCover 方法，通过精准识别并处理高阶依赖关系，提升了模型极端缩减后的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的免训练 MoE 压缩方法大多基于两两专家间信号的排名，无法处理三个及以上专家在合并时形成的“不可约循环（irreducible cycle）”问题，导致结构性盲区，限制了极端压缩场景下的性能。

Method: 提出名为 HodgeCover 的方法，利用霍奇分解（Hodge decomposition）分析专家合并路径上的 KL 散度信号。它将专家合并问题建模为 2-复形（2-complex），识别出导致合并失败的“循环障碍（harmonic kernel）”，并以此作为选择策略，结合权重剪枝（weight pruning）实现混合压缩。

Result: 在三种主流开源 Sparse MoE 模型上验证，HodgeCover 在专家缩减任务中达到了免训练基线的领先水平，并在极端压缩场景下优于现有方法，同时有效平衡了模型中保留的质量信息。

Conclusion: HodgeCover 通过识别和处理专家合并中的“循环障碍（irreducible cycle）”，揭示了显式建模专家间高阶依赖关系对于高效压缩 MoE 模型的必要性，在同等压缩比下表现优于现有免训练方法。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) layers route tokens through a handful of experts, and learning-free compression of these layers reduces inference cost without retraining. A subtle obstruction blocks every existing compressor in this family: three experts can each be pairwise compatible yet form an irreducible cycle when merged together, so any score that ranks experts on pairwise signals is structurally blind to which triples are jointly mergeable. We show the obstruction is a precise mathematical object, the harmonic kernel of the simplicial Laplacian on a 2-complex whose vertices are experts, whose edges carry KL merge barriers, and whose faces carry triplet barriers; Hodge-decomposing the edge-barrier signal isolates the kernel exactly. We turn the diagnostic into a selection objective: HodgeCover greedily covers the harmonic-critical edges and triplet-critical triangles, and a hybrid variant of HodgeCover pairs it with off-the-shelf weight pruning on survivors. On three open-weight Sparse MoE backbones under aggressive expert reduction, HodgeCover matches state-of-the-art learning-free baselines on the expert-reduction axis, leads on the aggressive-compression frontier of the hybrid axis, and uniquely balances retained mass across all four Hodge components. These results show that exposing the harmonic kernel of a learned MoE structure changes which compressor wins at the regime that matters most.

</details>


### [96] [Support Before Frequency in Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13999)
*Adrian Müller,Antoine Gonon,Zebang Shen,Ya-Ping Hsieh,Niao He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了离散扩散模型学习语言的“分层机制”：模型倾向于先捕捉数据的语法结构/支撑集（Validity），而后才学习细粒度的频率信息（Frequency），且该过程受扩散类型（均匀 vs. 掩码）影响明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离散扩散模型在语言建模领域表现出色，但其去噪目标如何具体组织学习过程、模型如何捕捉数据结构与频率信息之间的关系尚不清晰。

Method: 通过理论分析离散扩散模型（均匀扩散与掩码扩散）在小噪声体制下的逆向去噪过程，证明了反向编辑操作可以分解为领先的“支撑位”和精细的“频率系数”；并通过掩码语言扩散模型及合成语言任务进行实验验证。

Result: 理论证明并实验证实：模型在学习时先确定数据的支撑集（Validity），随后才细化支撑集内部的概率分布；且掩码扩散（Absorbing Diffusion）在向有效结构（Valid tokens）收敛方面表现出比均匀扩散更优越的机制。

Conclusion: 离散扩散模型在学习过程中表现出显著的分层特性，即优先学习数据支撑（结构的有效性）而后学习数据频率（分布细节），且这种学习机制高度依赖于扩散过程的具体实现形式（如均匀扩散与掩码扩散）。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models are increasingly competitive for language modeling, yet it remains unclear how their denoising objectives organize learning. Although these objectives target the full data distribution, we show that the exact reverse process induces a hierarchy between coarse support information and finer frequency information. For uniform and absorbing (a.k.a. masking) diffusion, we prove that, in the small-noise regime of the final denoising steps, each single-token reverse edit decomposes into a leading scale, determined by whether it moves toward the data support (e.g., grammatically valid sentences), and a finer coefficient, determining relative probabilities within the same scale. Thus, recovering validity structure only requires learning the correct order of magnitude of reverse probabilities, whereas recovering data frequencies requires coefficient-level estimation. The separation is mechanism-dependent: uniform diffusion exhibits a trichotomy into validity-improving, validity-preserving, and validity-worsening edits, while absorbing diffusion places its leading-order mass on validity-improving moves. Experiments on a masked language diffusion model and synthetic regular-language tasks support these predictions: support-localization emerges earlier than within-support frequency ranking, and the contrast between uniform and absorbing diffusion matches the predicted rate separation. Together, our results suggest that discrete diffusion models learn data support before data frequencies.

</details>


### [97] [Dywave: Event-Aligned Dynamic Tokenization for Heterogeneous IoT Sensing Signal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14014)
*Tomoyoshi Kimura,Denizhan Kara,Jinyang Li,Hongjue Zhao,Yigong Hu,Yizhuo Chen,Xiaomin Ouyang,Shengzhong Liu,Tarek Abdelzaher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Dywave 是一种针对 IoT 感知信号的动态 Token 化框架，通过小波分解和自适应事件边界识别，在大幅减少输入长度（最高 75%）的同时，显著提升了模型在各类感知任务中的精度（最高 12%）与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT 感知信号具有固有非平稳、多尺度的特征，现有的标准分词技术难以有效处理这些复杂信号，导致处理效率低下且难以捕捉潜在的物理事件语义。

Method: 提出 Dywave 框架，核心步骤包括：1. 利用基于小波的层次分解提取多尺度特征；2. 识别与语义事件对应的关键时间边界；3. 自适应压缩冗余区间，同时保持时间连贯性。

Result: 在五个真实 IoT 数据集上的实验表明，Dywave 将准确率提升了最多 12%，并将输入 Token 长度减少了最多 75%；同时在跨域适应性和序列长度变化方面表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Dywave 是一种高效、鲁棒且具有领域泛化能力的动态分词框架，通过引入小波分解和基于事件的自适应压缩，解决了 IoT 感知信号非平稳和多尺度带来的挑战，为 IoT 领域序列模型提供了更优的输入表示。

Abstract: Internet of Things (IoT) systems continuously collect heterogeneous sensing signals from ubiquitous sensors to support intelligent applications such as human activity analysis, emotion monitoring, and environmental perception. These signals are inherently non-stationary and multi-scale, posing unique challenges for standard tokenization techniques. This paper proposes Dywave, a dynamic tokenization framework for IoT sensing signals that constructs compact input representations aligned with intrinsic temporal structures and underlying physical events. Dywave leverages wavelet-based hierarchical decomposition, identifies meaningful temporal boundaries corresponding to underlying semantic events, and adaptively compresses redundant intervals while preserving temporal coherence. Extensive evaluations on five real-world IoT sensing datasets across activity recognition, stress assessment, and nearby object detection demonstrate that Dywave outperforms state-of-the-art methods by up to 12% in accuracy, while improving computational efficiency by reducing input token lengths by up to 75% across mainstream sequence models. Moreover, Dywave exhibits improved robustness to domain shifts and varying sequence lengths.

</details>


### [98] [R2R2: Robust Representation for Intensive Experience Reuse via Redundancy Reduction in Self-Predictive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14026)
*Sanghyeob Song,Donghyeok Lee,Jinsik Kim,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为了解决高UTD强化学习中的过拟合问题，本文提出了R2R2正则化方法，通过修正SPL的目标函数以实现更稳健的表示学习，显著提升了算法在机器人控制任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数据匮乏领域（如机器人），高更新数据比（UTD）虽然增强了数据重用效率，但会导致严重的过拟合现象。现有研究多关注Critic偏差，而忽视了SPL在高效UTD机制下表示层面的不稳定问题。

Method: 提出了一种名为“通过冗余减少实现稳健表示”（R2R2）的正则化方法。该方法识别出标准零中心化技术与SPL谱属性之间的冲突，并设计了非中心化目标函数来优化表示学习。此外，将SPL模块集成至SimbaV2，构建了SimbaV2-SPL。

Result: 在11项连续控制任务中，R2R2使TD7的性能提升了约22%（在UTD=20时）；同时，R2R2在SimbaV2-SPL上进一步提升了性能，使该算法设定了新的SOTA水平。

Conclusion: R2R2 方法通过引入非中心化目标函数，有效解决了自预测学习（SPL）在高数据更新比例（UTD）下的表示不稳定问题，显著提升了强化学习算法在数据匮乏环境下的泛化性能。

Abstract: For reinforcement learning in data-scarce domains like real-world robotics, intensive data reuse enhances efficiency but induces overfitting. While prior works focus on critic bias, representation-level instability in Self-Predictive Learning (SPL) under high Update-to-Data (UTD) regimes remains underexplored. To bridge this gap, we propose Robust Representation via Redundancy Reduction (R2R2), a regularization method within SPL. We theoretically identify that standard zero-centering conflicts with SPL's spectral properties and design a non-centered objective accordingly. We verify R2R2 on SPL-native algorithms like TD7. Furthermore, to demonstrate its orthogonality to prior advancements, we extend the state-of-the-art SimbaV2, which originally lacks SPL, by integrating a tailored SPL module, termed SimbaV2-SPL. Experiments across 11 continuous control tasks confirm that R2R2 effectively mitigates overfitting; specifically, at a UTD ratio of 20, it improves TD7 by ~22% and provides additional gains on top of SimbaV2-SPL, which itself establishes a new state-of-the-art. The code can be found at: https://github.com/songsang7/R2R2

</details>


### [99] [Self-Pruned Key-Value Attention: Learning When to Write by Predicting Future Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14037)
*Gergely Szilvasy,Manuel Faysse,Maria Lomeli,Matthijs Douze,Pierre-Emmanuel Mazaré,Loïc Cabannes,Wen-tau Yih,Hervé Jégou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为 SP-KV 的动态 KV Cache 修剪机制，通过联合训练的轻量级预测器动态剔除低价值缓存项，在大幅降低内存和提升推理速度的同时保持了模型原有的性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型处理超长序列及智能体任务时，Transformer 的 KV Cache 内存占用和带宽限制了生成效率和扩展性。

Method: 引入 Self-Pruned Key-Value Attention (SP-KV) 机制。通过轻量级效用预测器评估每个 KV 对的价值，结合局部窗口机制，仅在效用超过阈值时将旧 KV 对保留在全局注意力缓存中；模型与预测器通过下文预测损失进行联合端到端训练。

Result: 实现了 3 到 10 倍的 KV Cache 压缩，显著改善了内存占用和推理速度，同时在验证集损失及下游任务性能上几乎没有损失，并发现了模型中层级和头部的特定稀疏性结构。

Conclusion: SP-KV 是一种有效的 KV Cache 压缩机制，能够实现 3 到 10 倍的缓存缩减，且不牺牲模型性能。此外，该方法揭示的稀疏性模式有助于未来混合局部-全局注意力架构的设计。

Abstract: Under modern test-time compute and agentic paradigms, language models process ever-longer sequences. Efficient text generation with transformer architectures is increasingly constrained by the Key-Value cache memory footprint and bandwidth. To address this limitation, we introduce Self-Pruned Key-Value Attention (SP-KV), a mechanism designed to predict future KV utility in order to reduce the size of the long-term KV cache.
