GeoViSTA utilizes bilateral cross-attention to exchange spatial and semantic information across modalities, guided by a geography-aware attention mechanism that aligns continuous image patches with irregular census-tract tokens. We train GeoViSTA with a self-supervised joint masked-autoencoding objective, forcing it to recover missing image patches and tabular rows using local spatial context and cross-modal cues. Empirically, GeoViSTA's unified embeddings improve linear probing performance on high-impact downstream tasks, outperforming baselines in predicting disease-specific mortality and fire hazard frequency across held-out regions. These results demonstrate that jointly modeling the physical environment alongside structured socioeconomic context yields highly transferable representations for holistic geospatial inference.

</details>


### [137] [What if Tomorrow is the World Cup Final? Counterfactual Time Series Forecasting with Textual Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14422)
*Shuqi Gu,Yongxiang Zhao,Baoyu Jing,Kan Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过引入文本条件来指导时间序列的反事实预测，利用创新的文本归因机制区分关键因素，从而提升了模型在复杂场景下的泛化能力和预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测模型局限于历史模式，缺乏对复杂、随机未来事件的动态适应能力，且往往忽视了“反事实”场景，无法应对现实中更加灵活和多变的非结构化条件。

Method: 提出“带文本条件的反事实时间序列预测”任务；构建了一套涵盖事实与反事实环境的评估框架（即使在缺乏真值时也适用）；设计了一种新颖的文本归因机制，通过区分可变与不可变因素，增强了模型在复杂文本条件下的预测准确性。

Result: 模型能够实现更加灵活、感知条件的预测，并在处理复杂且具有随机性的文本条件时，展现出比传统方法更高的准确性。

Conclusion: 通过引入文本条件计数器事实预测任务和基于文本归因的机制（TADiff），该研究有效提升了预测模型处理复杂、随机且非结构化未来场景的能力，并提供了一套完善的评估框架，填补了现有模型在反事实推理和条件泛化方面的空白。

Abstract: Time series forecasting has become increasingly critical in real-world scenarios, where future sequences are influenced not only by historical patterns but also by forthcoming events. In this context, forecasting must dynamically adapt to complex and stochastic future conditions, which introduces fundamental challenges in both forecasting and evaluation. Traditional methods typically rely on historical data or factual future conditions, while overlooking counterfactual scenarios. Furthermore, many existing approaches are restricted to simple structured conditions, limiting their ability to generalize to the real-world complexities. To address these gaps, we introduce the task of counterfactual time series forecasting with textual conditions, enabling more flexible and condition-aware forecasting. We propose a comprehensive evaluation framework that encompasses both factual and counterfactual settings, even in the absence of ground truth time series. Additionally, we present a novel text-attribution mechanism that distinguishes mutable from immutable factors, thereby improving forecast accuracy under sophisticated and stochastic textual conditions. The project page is at https://seqml.github.io/TADiff/

</details>


### [138] [Collaborative Yet Personalized Policy Training: Single-Timescale Federated Actor-Critic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14423)
*Leo Muxing Wang,Pengkun Yang,Lili Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对协作学习中环境异构性问题，本文提出了一种基于共享线性子空间的联邦Actor-Critic框架，在理论上实现了有限时间收敛与线性加速，并证实了该方法能有效提升模型性能与迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Actor-Critic方法在协同训练中忽略了环境的异构性，或者通过训练单一共享策略牺牲了策略的个性化，无法有效适应多智能体协作需求。

Method: 提出了一种新型的联邦Actor-Critic框架，采用联合线性近似方法，结合投影子空间更新的微扰分析、QR分解，以及针对异构马尔可夫噪声的条件混合论证。通过单一时间尺度更新共同估计公共子空间、本地Critic头和Actor策略。

Result: 理论上证明了在给定条件下，Critic误差以$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/((1-γ)^4\sqrt{TK}))$收敛，策略梯度范数以$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/((1-γ)^6\sqrt{TK}))$收敛；实验表明该方法在Hopper-v5任务中优于传统的Single PPO和FedAvg PPO。

Conclusion: 该论文成功构建了一个联邦Actor-Critic框架，通过共享线性子空间并保持个性化组件，实现了在异构环境下的协同训练。理论分析证明了算法在Markovian采样下的有限时间收敛性，并展示了相对于智能体数量$K$的线性加速效果，实验结果验证了其在复杂任务中的优势。

Abstract: Despite the popularity of the actor-critic method and the practical needs of collaborative policy training, existing works typically either overlook environmental heterogeneity or give up personalization altogether by training a single shared policy across all agents. We consider a federated actor-critic framework in which agents share a common linear subspace representation while maintaining personalized local policy components, and agents iteratively estimate the common subspace, local critic heads, and local policies (i.e., actors). Under canonical single-timescale updates with Markovian sampling, we establish finite-time convergence via a novel joint linear approximation framework. Specifically, we show that the critic error converges to zero at the rate of $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/((1-γ)^4\sqrt{TK}))$, and the policy gradient norm converges to zero at the rate of $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/((1-γ)^6\sqrt{TK}))$, where $T$ is the number of rounds, $K$ is the number of agents, and $γ\in (0,1)$ is the discount factor. These results demonstrate linear speedup with respect to the number of agents $K$, despite heterogeneous Markovian trajectories under distinct transition kernels and coupled learning dynamics. To address these challenges, we develop a new perturbation analysis for the projected subspace updates and QR decomposition steps, together with conditional mixing arguments for heterogeneous Markovian noise. Furthermore, to handle the additional complications induced by policy updates and temporal dependence, we establish fine-grained characterizations of the discrepancies between function evaluations under Markovian sampling and under temporally frozen policies. Experiments instantiate the framework within PPO on federated \texttt{Hopper-v5} action-map heterogeneity, showing gains over Single PPO and FedAvg PPO and downstream transfer from the learned shared trunk.

</details>


### [139] [FrontierSmith: Synthesizing Open-Ended Coding Problems at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14445)
*Runyuan He,Qiuyang Mang,Shang Zhou,Kaiyuan Liu,Hanchen Li,Huanzhi Mao,Qizheng Zhang,Zerui Li,Bo Peng,Lufeng Cheng,Tianfu Fu,Yichuan Wang,Wenhao Chai,Jingbo Shang,Alex Dimakis,Joseph E. Gonzalez,Alvin Cheung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对开放式编程数据稀缺问题，本文提出FrontierSmith系统，将封闭式竞赛题自动演化为高质量开放式任务，显著增强了大模型的复杂编程与长期规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型主要针对定义明确的编码任务（如纠错、竞赛题）进行训练，缺乏处理开放式、无标准答案任务的能力，且高质量的开放式编程训练数据稀缺。

Method: 提出了FrontierSmith系统：1.通过修改目标、限制输出和泛化输入，将封闭式竞赛编程题演化为开放式问题；2.利用“思路发散度指标（idea divergence metric）”筛选出能产生多样化解法的题目；3.自动化生成测试用例与验证器。

Result: 基于FrontierSmith合成数据训练的模型性能显著提升：Qwen3.5-9B在FrontierCS和ALE-bench上分别提升+8.82分和+306.36 Elo；Qwen3.5-27B提升+12.12分和+309.12 Elo。且模型在推理时表现出更倾向于人类开发者的多轮交互和长序列规划行为。

Conclusion: 通过利用现有的封闭式编码任务，通过自动化手段生成多样化的开放式编程问题，能够有效弥补训练数据匮乏的问题，显著提升大模型处理长期、复杂编程任务的能力。

Abstract: Many real-world coding challenges are open-ended and admit no known optimal solution. Yet, recent progress in LLM coding has focused on well-defined tasks such as feature implementation, bug fixing, and competitive programming. Open-ended coding remains a weak spot for LLMs, largely because open-ended training problems are scarce and expensive to construct. Our goal is to synthesize open-ended coding problems at scale to train stronger LLM coders. We introduce FrontierSmith, an automated system for iteratively evolving open-ended problems from existing closed-ended coding tasks. Starting from competitive programming problems, FrontierSmith generates candidate open-ended variants by changing the problems'goals, restricting outputs, and generalizing inputs. It then uses a quantitative idea divergence metric to select problems that elicit genuinely diverse approaches from different solvers. Agents then generate test cases and verifiers for the surviving candidates. On two open-ended coding benchmarks, training on our synthesized data yields substantial gains over the base models: Qwen3.5-9B improves by +8.82 score on FrontierCS and +306.36 (Elo-rating-based performance) on ALE-bench; Qwen3.5-27B improves by +12.12 and +309.12, respectively. The synthesized problems also make agents take more turns and use more tokens, similar to human-curated ones, suggesting that closed-ended seeds can be a practical starting point for long-horizon coding data.

</details>


### [140] [When Answers Stray from Questions: Hallucination Detection via Question-Answer Orthogonal Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14449)
*Siyang Yao,Erhu Feng,Yubin Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QAOD框架，通过正交分解去除问题偏差，并结合Fisher重要性分析与双重探测策略，在降低计算开销的同时，显著提升了LLM幻觉检测在跨领域任务中的性能与稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM幻觉检测方法存在局限：黑盒一致性方法效率低下，单次白盒探测方法在领域偏移（domain shift）下性能急剧下降。旨在解决准确性、效率与跨领域鲁棒性之间的平衡问题。

Method: 提出QAOD（问题-答案正交分解）框架：1.利用正交投影去除回答表示中与问题对齐的部分；2.通过多样性惩罚的Fisher评分选择层，并利用Fisher重要性选择判别性神经元；3.设计双重探测策略：联合探测（正交分量+问题上下文）用于领域内判别，孤立探测（仅正交分量）用于跨领域（OOD）迁移。

Result: QAOD在领域内检测中实现最优AUROC；在跨领域（OOD）迁移表现上，于BioASQ数据集超越白盒基准线最高达21%，且计算成本低于生成成本的25%。

Conclusion: QAOD通过解耦问题无关的真实性信号，在保持极低计算成本的同时，显著提升了LLM幻觉检测在领域内和跨领域场景下的准确率。

Abstract: Hallucination detection in large language models (LLMs) requires balancing accu racy, efficiency, and robustness to distribution shift. Black-box consistency methods are effective but demand repeated inference; single-pass white-box probes are effi cient yet treat answer representations in isolation, often degrading sharply under domain shift. We propose QAOD (Question-Answer Orthogonal Decomposition), a single-pass framework that projects away the question-aligned direction from the answer representation to obtain a question-orthogonal component that suppresses domain-conditioned variation. To identify informative signals, QAOD further selects layers via diversity-penalized Fisher scoring and discriminative neurons via Fisher importance. To address both in-domain detection and cross-domain generalization, we design two complementary probing strategies: pairing the or thogonal component with question context yields a joint probe that maximizes in-domain discriminability, while using the orthogonal component alone preserves domain-agnostic factuality signals for robust transfer. QAOD's joint probe achieves the best in-domain AUROC across all evaluated model-dataset pairs, while the orthogonal-only probe delivers the strongest OOD transfer, surpassing the best white-box baseline by up to 21% on BioASQ at under 25% of generation cost.

</details>


### [141] [LiSA: Lifelong Safety Adaptation via Conservative Policy Induction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14454)
*Minbeom Kim,Lesly Miculicich,Bhavana Dalvi Mishra,Mihir Parmar,Phillip Wallis,Bharath Chandrasekhar,Kyomin Jung,Tomas Pfister,Long T. Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 LiSA 框架，通过引入结构化记忆、冲突感知规则和证据感知门控机制，使 AI 智能体防护栏能在稀疏、含噪的真实运行反馈中实现持续性、自适应的安全性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 AI 智能体处理敏感数据和执行复杂任务，传统的预部署防护栏（guardrails）难以应对复杂、依赖上下文且难以预定义的各种边缘风险，而实时的部署反馈通常稀疏且存在噪声，频繁微调又不切实际。

Method: 提出了 LiSA（Lifelong Safety Adaptation）框架，通过结构化内存实现保守的策略归纳：1. 将个别失败转换为可重用的策略抽象；2. 加入冲突感知（conflict-aware）的局部规则防止过度泛化；3. 采用基于后验下界的证据感知置信度门控（evidence-aware confidence gating），确保内存复用基于积累的证据而非单纯的经验准确度。

Result: 在 PrivacyLens+、ConFaide+ 和 AgentHarm 等数据集上，LiSA 在稀疏反馈环境下显著优于现有的记忆基准模型，即使在 20% 标签错误率的噪声反馈下仍具稳健性，且在时延-性能权衡指标上优于单纯的模型规模扩展。

Conclusion: LiSA 为在实际部署中通过有限反馈持续提升 AI 智能体安全性提供了一种切实可行的方法，能够有效应对复杂、动态的边缘风险场景。

Abstract: As AI agents move from chat interfaces to systems that read private data, call tools, and execute multi-step workflows, guardrails become a last line of defense against concrete deployment harms. In these settings, guardrail failures are no longer merely answer-quality errors: they can leak secrets, authorize unsafe actions, or block legitimate work. The hardest failures are often contextual: whether an action is acceptable depends on local privacy norms, organizational policies, and user expectations that resist pre-deployment specification. This creates a practical gap: guardrails must adapt to their own operating environments, yet deployment feedback is typically limited to sparse, noisy user-reported failures, and repeated fine-tuning is often impractical. To address this gap, we propose LiSA (Lifelong Safety Adaptation), a conservative policy induction framework that improves a fixed base guardrail through structured memory. LiSA converts occasional failures into reusable policy abstractions so that sparse reports can generalize beyond individual cases, adds conflict-aware local rules to prevent overgeneralization in mixed-label contexts, and applies evidence-aware confidence gating via a posterior lower bound, so that memory reuse scales with accumulated evidence rather than empirical accuracy alone. Across PrivacyLens+, ConFaide+, and AgentHarm, LiSA consistently outperforms strong memory-based baselines under sparse feedback, remains robust under noisy user feedback even at 20% label-flip rates, and pushes the latency--performance frontier beyond backbone model scaling. Ultimately, LiSA offers a practical path to secure AI agents against the unpredictable long tail of real-world edge risks.

</details>


### [142] [Focused PU learning from imbalanced data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14467)
*Elias Zavitsanos,Georgios Paliouras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对高度不平衡数据集的PU学习难题，作者提出了一种新的聚焦经验风险估计器，能够有效从包含误标记的未标记样本中提取正例特征，并在实际财务造假检测中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于现实世界中（如疾病基因识别、欺诈检测等）标记数据稀缺，常规机器学习方法难以处理高度不平衡的PU数据分布，且现有PU学习方法在处理“难以区分的阳性样本”和“高度不平衡场景”时能力有限。

Method: 提出了一种针对高度不平衡数据集的PU学习方法，通过一种聚焦式的经验风险估计器（Focused Empirical Risk Estimator），结合正例和未标记样本进行二分类器的训练。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在SCAR（完全随机选择）和SAR（随机选择）两种标记机制下，均在不平衡数据集上达到了最先进（State-of-the-Art）的性能。

Conclusion: 提出的聚焦经验风险估计器在处理高度不平衡的PU学习任务中表现优异，并在财务造假检测等实际应用中展现了其实用价值。

Abstract: We propose a new method of learning from positive and unlabeled (PU) examples in highly imbalanced datasets. Many real-world problems, such as disease gene identification, targeted marketing, fraud detection, and recommender systems, are hard to address with machine learning methods, due to limited labeled data. Often, training data comprises positive and unlabeled instances, the latter typically being dominated by negative, but including also several positive instances. While PU learning is well-studied, few methods address imbalanced settings or hard-to-detect positive examples that resemble negative ones. Our approach uses a focused empirical risk estimator, incorporating both positive and unlabeled examples to train binary classifiers. Empirical evaluations demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on imbalanced datasets under two labeling mechanisms - selecting positives completely at random (SCAR) and selecting at random (SAR). Beyond these controlled experiments, we demonstrate the value of the proposed method in the real-world application of financial misstatement detection.

</details>


### [143] [Test-Time Learning with an Evolving Library](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14477)
*Weijia Xu,Alessandro Sordoni,Chandan Singh,Zelalem Gero,Michel Galley,Xingdi Yuan,Jianfeng Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EvoLib 是一种无需微调即可让大模型在推理过程中自动积累、演化并复用知识的学习框架，通过自我学习实现了跨任务性能的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大型语言模型在测试时无法持续累积、复用和演化知识，且现有方法通常依赖参数更新或外部监督的问题。

Method: 提出 EvoLib 框架：维护一个共享的知识抽象库（包含模块化技能和反思性见解），通过推理轨迹自动提取知识，并利用加权与整合机制（weighting and consolidation mechanism）实现知识由具体到通用的演化。

Result: 在数学推理、代码生成和多轮智能体任务中，EvoLib 在没有地面真实值（ground-truth）反馈的情况下，显著超越了现有的测试时扩展（test-time scaling）和学习方法。

Conclusion: EvoLib 通过在推理过程中积累可复用的知识库，成功实现了语言模型在无需参数更新或外部监督下的持续学习与性能提升，证明了模型可以通过自我迭代优化推理策略。

Abstract: We introduce EvoLib, a test-time learning framework that enables large language models to accumulate, reuse, and evolve knowledge across problem instances without parameter updates or external supervision. Instead of adapting model parameters, our approach maintains a shared library of knowledge abstractions, including modular skills and reflective insights, automatically extracted from the model's own inference trajectories. To support continual improvement, we introduce a principled weighting and consolidation mechanism that jointly optimizes for immediate utility and long-term value. This allows simple, instance-specific abstractions to evolve into more general and reusable ones over time. Across challenging benchmarks in mathematical reasoning, code generation, and multi-turn agentic environments, EvoLib improves substantially over the top test-time scaling and learning methods without ground-truth feedback.

</details>


### [144] [Lang2MLIP: End-to-End Language-to-Machine Learning Interatomic Potential Development with Autonomous Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14527)
*Wenwen Li,Yuki Orimo,Nontawat Charoenphakdee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Lang2MLIP是一个基于大语言模型的多智能体框架，通过将MLIP开发转变为序列决策问题，实现了无需专家干预的自动化、自适应原子间势函数开发，降低了材料模拟的门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发机器学习原子间势函数（MLIP）涉及高门槛的跨学科知识（原子模拟、机器学习、工作流设计），且现有的自动化管线往往缺乏灵活性，难以适应复杂的材料系统，迫切需要一种能自适应、低门槛的开发工具。

Method: 提出Lang2MLIP框架，将MLIP开发建模为由LLM驱动的序列决策问题。采用多智能体架构，通过观察当前数据集、模型、评估结果和日志，自动选择行动以改进模型，支持动态调整和自我修正，而非依赖预定义的固定流水线。

Result: 在包含多个组分和界面的固体电解质界面（SEI）系统中进行了验证，证明该框架能够有效执行开发任务并实现自我优化。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的多智能体系统为MLIP开发自动化提供了一条极具前景的路径，并显著降低了非专家进行复杂材料建模的门槛。

Abstract: Developing machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) for complex materials systems remains challenging because it requires expertise in atomistic simulations, machine learning, and workflow design, as well as iterative active learning procedures. Existing automated pipelines typically assume a fixed sequence of stages or depend on domain experts, which limits their adaptability to heterogeneous materials systems where the optimal curriculum is not known in advance. To lower the barrier to developing MLIPs for non-experts, we propose Lang2MLIP, a multi-agent framework that takes natural-language input and formulates end-to-end MLIP development as a sequential decision-making problem solved by large language models (LLMs). At each step, a decision-making agent observes the current dataset, model, evaluation results, and execution log, and then automatically selects an appropriate action to improve the model. This removes the need for a predefined pipeline and enables the agent to self-correct by revisiting earlier subsystems when new failures arise. We evaluate this approach on a solid electrolyte interphase (SEI) system with multiple components and interfaces. These results suggest that LLM-based multi-agent systems are a promising direction for automating MLIP development and making it more accessible to non-experts.

</details>


### [145] [Resolving Action Bottleneck: Agentic Reinforcement Learning Informed by Token-Level Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14558)
*Langzhou He,Junyou Zhu,Yue Zhou,Zhengyao Gu,Junhua Liu,Wei-Chieh Huang,Henry Peng Zou,David Wipf,Philip S. Yu,Qitian Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 Agentic RL 中推理轨迹标记分配训练权重不均的问题，提出了 ActFocus 方法，通过重加权机制聚焦动作标记，显著提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RL 算法（如 PPO/GRPO）在处理具备推理轨迹的大模型时，对所有标记赋予相等的权重，导致信用分配不均，忽略了训练信号在动作标记上的集中效应。

Method: 提出 ActFocus 算法，通过能量模型分析识别出“动作瓶颈”现象，并对训练过程中的推理标记进行梯度加权（下调权重），同时对不确定性较高的动作标记进行能量导向的权重重分配。

Result: 在四个实验环境中，ActFocus 相比 PPO 和 GRPO 表现出显著优势，最终步骤性能分别提升高达 65.2 和 63.7 个百分点，且无额外运行时或内存负担。

Conclusion: 提出 ActFocus 策略，通过抑制推理标记的梯度和增强动作标记权重，有效解决了强化学习中的“动作瓶颈”问题，在不增加计算开销的前提下实现了性能显著提升。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning trains large language models using multi-turn trajectories that interleave long reasoning traces with short environment-facing actions. Common policy-gradient methods, such as PPO and GRPO, treat each token in a trajectory equally, leading to uniform credit assignment. In this paper, we critically demonstrate that such uniform credit assignment largely misallocates token-level training signals. From an energy-based modeling perspective, we show that token-level training signals, quantified by their correlations with reward variance of different rollouts sampled from a given prompt, concentrate sharply on action tokens rather than reasoning tokens, even though action tokens account for only a small fraction of the trajectory. We refer to this phenomenon as the Action Bottleneck. Motivated by this observation, we propose an embarrassingly simple token reweighting approach, ActFocus, that downweights gradients on reasoning tokens, along with an additional energy-based redistribution mechanism that further increases the weights on action tokens with higher uncertainty. Across four environments and different model sizes, ActFocus consistently outperforms PPO and GRPO, yielding final-step gains of up to 65.2 and 63.7 percentage points, respectively, without any additional runtime or memory cost.

</details>


### [146] [Exploring Geographic Relative Space in Large Language Models through Activation Patching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14535)
*Stef De Sabbata,Rahul Baiju,Stefano Mizzaro,Kevin Roitero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用机制可解释性中的“激活修补”技术，探究大语言模型在地理空间分析中的逻辑运作机制，以解决AI集成在地理学应用中的潜在安全隐患。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在地理学领域的使用日益广泛，但由于缺乏对其内部运作机制的深入理解，其在整合进地理过程与分析时的安全性存在重大隐患，研究旨在通过可解释性手段揭示其逻辑。

Method: 采用“激活修补”（Activation Patching）这一机制可解释性（Mechanistic Interpretability）工具，对大语言模型处理相对地理空间的内部运作方式进行探测与分析。

Result: 该研究提出了一种评估框架，能够通过技术手段观测LLM在处理相对地理空间时的内部信息流向与特征。

Conclusion: 通过使用激活修补（Activation Patching）技术，研究人员可以深入剖析LLM处理地理空间逻辑的内部机制，从而为提高地理空间分析中AI应用的安全性与可解释性提供了路径。

Abstract: The increased use of Large Language Models (LLMs) in geography raises substantial questions about the safety of integrating these tools across a wide range of processes and analyses, given our very limited understanding of their inner workings. In this extended abstract, we examine how LLMs process relative geographic space using activation patching, an emerging tool for mechanistic interpretability.

</details>


### [147] [RxEval: A Prescription-Level Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Medication Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14543)
*Shuhao Chen,Weisen Jiang,Changmiao Wang,Xiaoqing Wu,Xuanren Shi,Yu Zhang,James T. Kwok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对住院用药推荐任务，本文提出了 RxEval 基准测试，旨在评估大模型处理动态临床处方数据的推理与决策能力，发现当前模型在准确提取信息和临床逻辑推演方面存在显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有住院用药推荐基准测试主要依赖入院级别的粗粒度编码，无法模拟临床实际用药中随时间动态变化的复杂用药过程（药物、剂量、途径）。

Method: 构建了 RxEval 基准测试，包含 1,547 道基于真实处方和推理链扰动生成干扰项的多选临床决策问题，并对 16 个大模型进行了评估。

Result: RxEval 具有高挑战性和区分度，模型 F1 分数在 45.18% 到 77.10% 之间，最佳准确匹配率（Exact Match）仅为 46.10%。

Conclusion: RxEval 揭示了现有大模型在处理动态、细粒度住院用药决策时的局限性，即使是前沿模型也常忽略关键患者信息并缺乏严谨的临床推理能力。

Abstract: Inpatient medication recommendation requires clinicians to repeatedly select specific medications, doses, and routes as a patient's condition evolves. Existing benchmarks formulate this task as admission-level prediction over coarse drug codes with multi-hot diagnostic and procedure code inputs, failing to capture the per-timepoint, information-rich nature of real prescribing. We propose RxEval, a prescription-level benchmark that evaluates LLM prescribing capability by multiple-choice questions: each question presents a detailed patient profile and time-ordered clinical trajectory, requiring selection of specific medication-dose-route triples from real prescriptions and patient-specific distractors generated via reasoning-chain perturbation. RxEval comprises 1,547 questions spanning 584 patients, 18 diagnostic categories, and 969 unique medications. Evaluation of 16 LLMs shows that RxEval is both challenging and discriminative: F1 ranges from 45.18 to 77.10 across models, and the best Exact Match is only 46.10%. Error analysis reveals that even frontier models may overlook stated patient information and fail to derive clinical conclusions.

</details>


### [148] [Multi-Dimensional Model Integrity and Responsibility Assessment Index and Scoring Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14550)
*Phuc Truong Loc Nguyen,Thanh Hung Do,Truong Thanh Hung Nguyen,Hung Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为了解决高风险表格领域缺乏统一评估标准的问题，本文提出了MIRAI框架，通过整合可解释性、公平性等五大维度，为AI模型的综合绩效和责任水平提供了一个科学的评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医疗、金融等高风险表格领域，当前对人工智能的各种非性能指标（如可解释性、公平性等）的评估往往是孤立进行的，缺乏一个统一的衡量标准来综合评估模型的整体完整性与责任感。

Method: 提出了一种名为MIRAI（模型完整性与责任评估指数）的统一评估框架。该方法将可解释性、公平性、鲁棒性、隐私性和可持续性这五个维度进行标准化和方向对齐，并将其整合为一个综合分数，实现了在受控对照设置下对不同架构和计算特性的模型进行直接比较。

Result: 通过在医疗、金融和社会经济数据集上的实验，结果显示预测性能优异的模型并不一定具备更好的整体完整性与责任感。对比发现，简单的模型在某些情况下反而能实现比复杂深度学习架构更好的跨维度平衡。

Conclusion: 本文提出的MIRAI框架证明了高风险表格任务中，预测性能并非衡量模型质量的唯一标准。通过MIRAI评估，可以发现更简单的模型往往能在复杂维度平衡中优于深度学习架构，从而为监管环境下的模型选择提供了更科学、全面的依据。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence in high-stakes tabular domains cannot be evaluated by predictive performance alone, yet current practice still assesses explainability, fairness, robustness, privacy, and sustainability mostly in isolation. We propose the Model Integrity and Responsibility Assessment Index (MIRAI), a unified evaluation framework that measures tabular models across these five dimensions under a controlled comparison setting and aggregates them into a single score. MIRAI combines established metrics through normalized and direction-aligned dimension scores, which enables direct comparison across models with different architectural and computational profiles. Experiments on healthcare, financial, and socioeconomic datasets show that higher predictive performance does not necessarily imply better overall integrity and responsibility. In several cases, simpler models achieve a stronger cross-dimensional balance than more complex deep tabular architectures. MIRAI provides a compact and practical basis for responsible model selection in regulated settings.

</details>


### [149] [SeesawNet: Towards Non-stationary Time Series Forecasting with Balanced Modeling of Common and Specific Dependencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14551)
*Hao Li,Lu Zhang,Liu Chong,Yankai Chen,Pengyang Wang,Yingjie Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对非平稳时间序列预测中因归一化导致的过度平滑问题，SeesawNet 引入了 ASNA 模块，通过动态平衡公共特征与样本特有特征的建模，实现了更精确的跨维度依赖捕获。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的实例归一化（IN）方法在处理非平稳时间序列时，虽能消除分布偏移，但过度平滑了捕捉时序与跨通道异质性所需的关键结构信息。现有的改进方法未能有效解决在不同非平稳结构下如何自适应平衡公共与特有依赖的矛盾。

Method: 提出 SeesawNet 框架，核心是自适应平稳-非平稳注意力机制（ASNA）。该机制通过同时处理归一化序列（提取公共依赖）与原始序列（提取特有依赖），并根据实例的非平稳性进行自适应融合，交替进行时间和通道维度的关系建模。

Result: 在多个真实世界的时间序列预测基准测试中，SeesawNet 的效果始终优于现有的最先进（SOTA）预测模型。

Conclusion: SeesawNet 通过动态平衡公共依赖与样本特有依赖，有效解决了非平稳时间序列预测中因过度平滑导致的结构信息丢失问题，在多项基准测试中表现优于当前主流方法。

Abstract: Instance normalization (IN) is widely used in non-stationary multivariate time series forecasting to reduce distribution shifts and highlight common patterns across samples. However, IN can over-smooth instance-specific structural information that is essential for modeling temporal and cross-channel heterogeneity. While prior methods further suppress distribution discrepancies or attempt to recover temporal specific dependencies, they often ignore a central tension: how to adaptively model common and instance-specific dependency based on each instance's non-stationary structures. To address this dilemma, we propose SeesawNet, a unified architecture that dynamically balances common and instance-specific dependency modeling in both temporal and channel dimensions. At its core is Adaptive Stationary-Nonstationary Attention (ASNA), which captures common dependencies from normalized sequences and specific dependencies from raw sequences, and adaptively fuses them according to instance-level non-stationarity. Built upon ASNA, SeesawNet alternates dedicated temporal and channel relationship modeling to jointly capture long-range and cross-variable dependencies. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world benchmarks demonstrate that SeesawNet consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [150] [Efficient Multi-objective Prompt Optimization via Pure-exploration Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14553)
*Donghao Li,Chengshuai Shi,Weijuan Ou,Cong Shen,Jing Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多目标大语言模型提示词选择问题，引入多目标纯探索赌博机框架以实现有效的Pareto最优提示词集恢复及可行性最优提示词识别，并通过理论与实验验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的提示词优化通常依赖单一指标，忽略了提示词性能的多维度（Multi-faceted）特性，缺乏针对多目标权衡的系统性研究。

Method: 将提示词选择转化为多目标纯探索赌博机（Multi-objective Pure Exploration Bandits）问题；适配并改进了多目标赌博机算法；为线性情况下的可行性最优提示词识别设计了新算法并提供理论保证。

Result: 在多个大语言模型上的实验表明，所提出的基于赌博机的方法较现有基准有显著性能提升，证明了该框架在多目标提示词优化中的高效性和原则性。

Conclusion: 将多目标提示词选择问题建模为多目标单纯探索赌博机问题是有效且具有理论支撑的，该方法在处理多维度性能权衡时显著优于现有基准。

Abstract: Prompt engineering has become central to eliciting the capabilities of large language models (LLMs). At its core lies prompt selection -- efficiently identifying the most effective prompts. However, most prior investigations overlook a key challenge: the inherently multi-faceted nature of prompt performance, which cannot be captured by a single metric. To fill this gap, we study the multi-objective prompt selection problem under two practical settings: Pareto prompt set recovery and best feasible prompt identification. Casting the problem into the pure-exploration bandits framework, we adapt provably efficient algorithms from multi-objective bandits and further introduce a novel design for best feasible arm identification in structured bandits, with theoretical guarantees on the identification error in the linear case. Extensive experiments across multiple LLMs show that the bandit-based approaches yield significant improvements over baselines, establishing a principled and efficient framework for multi-objective prompt optimization.

</details>


### [151] [Boosting Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards via Randomly Selected Few-Shot Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15012)
*Kai Yan,Alexander G. Schwing,Yu-Xiong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FEST 是一种用于 LLM 的少样本演示引导强化学习算法，通过结合监督和策略内学习并引入动态权重衰减，解决了 RLVR 在复杂任务中的采样效率问题，仅用极少量数据即可实现高效训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习验证奖励机制（RLVR）在解决复杂任务时采样效率低下；而传统的监督微调（SFT）路径虽可辅助，但对标注数据的需求量大且获取成本高。

Method: 提出 FEST (Few-Shot demonstration-guided RLVR) 算法。核心组件包括：1. 监督信号引导；2. 策略内信号优化；3. 针对少样本数据集的权重衰减机制（Decaying Weights），以防止在多次训练周期中发生过拟合。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，FEST 仅需 128 条演示数据即可胜过基准方法，并在部分任务中达到与全量数据训练相当的性能。

Conclusion: FEST 通过仅利用 128 条演示数据，证明了在 RLVR 框架中结合监督信号、策略内信息与权重衰减机制，能够在大幅减少监督微调（SFT）数据依赖的同时，达到甚至超越全量数据训练的基准模型性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has achieved great success in developing Large Language Models (LLMs) with chain-of-thought rollouts for many tasks such as math and coding. Nevertheless, RLVR struggles with sample efficiency on difficult problems where correct rollouts are hard to generate. Prior works propose to address this issue via demonstration-guided RLVR, i.e., to conduct Supervised FineTuning (SFT) when RL fails; however, SFT often requires a lot of data, which can be expensive to acquire. In this paper, we propose FEST, a FEw-ShoT demonstration-guided RLVR algorithm. It attains compelling results with only 128 demonstrations randomly selected from an SFT dataset. We find that three components are vital for the success: supervised signal, on-policy signal, and decaying weights on the few-shot SFT dataset to prevent overfitting from multiple-epoch training. On several benchmarks, FEST outperforms baselines with magnitudes less SFT data, even matching their performance with full dataset.

</details>


### [152] [Woodelf++: A Fast and Unified Partial Dependence Plot Algorithm for Decision Tree Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14578)
*Ron Wettenstein,Alexander Nadel,Udi Boker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Woodelf++ 为决策树系综模型提供了一种极速的解释性工具框架，将 PDP、Joint-PDP 和高阶特征交互计算的效率提升了数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 PDP 及相关解释性工具在处理大规模数据集和高阶特征交互时计算复杂度极高，难以满足分析需求。原有的算法在计算效率和功能覆盖上存在局限。

Method: 基于伪布尔函数推导度量，构建了一个统一的高效计算框架（Woodelf++），并引入了“全 PDP”（Full PDP）以通过利用决策树的分割阈值来精确捕捉模型行为。此外，该算法支持 GPU 加速。

Result: 在 40 万行数据集的测试中，PDP 和 Joint-PDP 的计算速度比现有先进方法快 6 倍，比 scikit-learn 快 5 个数量级；在 Any-Order-PDIVs 任务上，计算时间从估计的百万年缩短至 5 分钟。

Conclusion: Woodelf++ 通过将 PDP、Joint-PDP 和 Any-Order-PDIVs 统一在一个高效框架内，在复杂度和计算效率上取得了巨大突破，特别是对于高阶特征交互的计算，实现了从几乎不可行到数分钟内完成的跨越。

Abstract: Partial Dependence Plots (PDPs) visualize how changes in a single feature affect the average model prediction. They are widely used in practice to interpret decision tree ensembles and other machine learning models. Joint-PDPs extend this idea to pairs of features, revealing their combined effect. Partial Dependence Interaction Values (PDIVs) measure feature interactions. The Any-Order-PDIVs task computes these interactions for every feature subset across all rows of the dataset.
  We introduce Woodelf++, a unified and efficient approach for computing all these useful explainability tools on decision tree ensembles, building on Woodelf, an algorithm for efficient SHAP computation. By deriving suitable metrics over pseudo-Boolean functions, Woodelf++ can compute PDPs (exact and approximate), Joint-PDPs, and Any-Order-PDIVs in a unified framework. Our method delivers substantial complexity improvements over the state of the art, including an exponential gain for Any-Order-PDIVs. Additionally, we introduce and efficiently compute Full PDPs, which leverage the model's split thresholds to faithfully capture its behavior across all possible feature values.
  Woodelf++ is implemented in pure Python and supports GPU acceleration. On a dataset with 400,000 rows, Woodelf++ computes PDP and Joint-PDP up to 6x faster than the state of the art and up to five orders of magnitude faster than scikit-learn. For Any-Order-PDIVs, the gap is even larger: Woodelf++ computes all interaction values in 5 minutes, while the state of the art is estimated to require over 1,000,000 years.

</details>


### [153] [Angel or Demon: Investigating the Plasticity Interventions' Impact on Backdoor Threats in Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14587)
*Oubo Ma,Ruixiao Lin,Yang Dai,Jiahao Chen,Chunyi Zhou,Linkang Du,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了塑性干预对DRL后门漏洞的影响，发现大多数干预能缓解后门，但SAM会加剧威胁，并据此提出了新的攻击框架与检测机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DRL后门研究多关注基础场景，忽略了现代DRL Agent中必备的“塑性干预”组件对后门漏洞稳定性的实际影响。

Method: 通过实证研究分析了涵盖14,664种干预与攻击组合的DRL场景，并结合病理分析（梯度放大、激活路径破坏、表示空间压缩）探究其机制。

Result: 发现仅SAM算法会加剧后门威胁，其余干预措施具有缓解作用；提出了鲁棒性后门注入框架SCC，并发现异常损失景观锐度可作为检测指标。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了塑性干预措施（如SAM）可能加剧DRL后门攻击的威胁，并提出通过分析损失景观锐度可有效检测后门。

Abstract: Extensive research has highlighted the severe threats posed by backdoor attacks to deep reinforcement learning (DRL). However, prior studies primarily focus on vanilla scenarios, while plasticity interventions have emerged as indispensable built-in components of modern DRL agents. Despite their effectiveness in mitigating plasticity loss, the impact of these interventions on DRL backdoor vulnerabilities remains underexplored, and this lack of systematic investigation poses risks in practical DRL deployments. To bridge this gap, we empirically study 14,664 cases integrating representative interventions and attack scenarios. We find that only one intervention (i.e., SAM) exacerbates backdoor threats, while other interventions mitigate them. Pathological analysis identifies that the exacerbation is attributed to backdoor gradient amplification, while the mitigation stems from activation pathway disruption and representation space compression. From these findings, we derive two novel insights: (1) a conceptual framework SCC for robust backdoor injection that deconstructs the mechanistic interplay between interventions and backdoors in DRL, and (2) abnormal loss landscape sharpness as a key indicator for DRL backdoor detection.

</details>


### [154] [Silent Collapse in Recursive Learning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14588)
*Zhipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 递归学习模型常出现传统指标稳定但内在表现日益退化的“沉默式崩溃”。本文识别了该现象的三个先行预警信号，并提出了MTR框架，通过元认知调节成功预防了模型的崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型等递归学习范式中，传统评估指标（如Loss、准确率）无法在崩溃发生前及时发现“沉默式崩溃”（Silent Collapse），即模型内在分布的逐步萎缩。

Method: 本文提出了MTR（监控-信任-调节）框架，通过监控预测熵、表征漂移和尾部覆盖率这三类轨迹级先兆，对模型的学习强度进行自适应调节。

Result: 研究证实了沉默式崩溃在传统指标下降前存在明确的先行指标；MTR框架能有效识别这些先兆，并在不接触原始数据的前提下防止模型内部表征的退化。

Conclusion: 针对递归学习导致的“沉默式崩溃”，提出的MTR框架能够在不依赖原始数据的情况下，通过指标先行预警和自适应调节有效防止模型性能坍塌。

Abstract: Recursive learning -- where models are trained on data generated by previous versions of themselves -- is increasingly common in large language models, autonomous agents, and self-supervised systems. However, standard performance metrics (loss, perplexity, accuracy) often fail to detect internal degradation before it becomes irreversible. Here we identify a phenomenon we call silent collapse: under broad recursive conditions, model internal distributions -- predictive entropy, representational diversity, and tail coverage -- progressively contract even as conventional metrics appear stable or improving.
  We discover that silent collapse is not abrupt. Its onset is reliably preceded by three trajectory-level precursors: (1) contraction of anchor entropy, (2) freezing of representation drift, and (3) erosion of tail coverage. These signals manifest multiple generations before any degradation in standard validation metrics, enabling early warning.
  Based on these precursors, we propose the MTR (Monitor--Trust--Regulator) framework, a lightweight metacognitive loop that monitors trajectory statistics, estimates a slow-timescale trust variable, and adaptively modulates the effective learning intensity. MTR provides early warning and actively prevents silent collapse without requiring access to pristine real data -- a critical advantage when original data is unavailable, contaminated, or private.

</details>


### [155] [Proposal and study of statistical features for string similarity computation and classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15110)
*E. O. Rodrigues,D. Casanova,M. Teixeira,V. Pegorini,F. Favarim,E. Clua,A. Conci,Panos Liatsis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将计算机视觉中的纹理统计特征（COM 和 RLM）引入字符串相似度分析，证实了其在处理多语言及复杂文本结构时，比传统的编辑距离和 LCS 等方法具有更好的鲁棒性和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在寻找一种不依赖于特定语言、语法结构或语义信息，仅基于统计学特性的通用字符串相似度度量方法，以解决跨语言或跨结构的字符序列比对需求。

Method: 将视觉计算领域常用的“共生矩阵（COM）”和“游程长度矩阵（RLM）”适配并应用于字符串相似度计算，并通过实验将其与最长公共子序列（LCS）、最大连续最长公共子序列、互信息和编辑距离等统计指标进行比较。

Result: 在合成数据集实验中，COM 和 RLM 的性能优于现有的其他统计特征（P值 < 0.001）；在真实文本抄袭数据集检测任务中，RLM 取得了最优表现。

Conclusion: COM 和 RLM 特征在字符串相似度计算中表现卓越，特别是在处理文本抄袭检测时，RLM 能够达到最佳效果，证明了视觉计算特征应用于通用字符串文本分析的有效性。

Abstract: Adaptations of features commonly applied in the field of visual computing, co-occurrence matrix (COM) and run-length matrix (RLM), are proposed for the similarity computation of strings in general (words, phrases, codes and texts). The proposed features are not sensitive to language related information. These are purely statistical and can be used in any context with any language or grammatical structure. Other statistical measures that are commonly employed in the field such as longest common subsequence, maximal consecutive longest common subsequence, mutual information and edit distances are evaluated and compared. In the first synthetic set of experiments, the COM and RLM features outperform the remaining state-of-the-art statistical features. In 3 out of 4 cases, the RLM and COM features were statistically more significant than the second best group based on distances (P-value < 0.001). When it comes to a real text plagiarism dataset, the RLM features obtained the best results.

</details>


### [156] [Forgetting That Sticks: Quantization-Permanent Unlearning via Circuit Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15138)
*Saisab Sadhu,Pratinav Seth,Vinay Kumar Sankarapu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了机器遗忘在 4-bit 量化下失效的系统性原因——由参数更新幅度过小导致的“稀疏性-永久性权衡”，并提出了 MANSU 方法，通过因果机制归因和精确的零空间投影确保遗忘在压缩后依然稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器遗忘（Machine Unlearning）评估忽略了部署时的量化过程，研究发现 4-bit 后训练量化（PTQ）会导致遗忘失效；根本原因是遗忘过程中的参数更新幅度过小，远低于量化分箱宽度，导致“稀疏性-永久性权衡”冲突。

Method: 提出 MANSU (Mechanistic-Aligned Null-Space Unlearning)，核心技术包括：1. 利用因果机制归因定位最小遗忘子图；2. 基于因果回路受限的零空间投影并结合对角 Fisher 保留约束；3. 实施逐参数幅度下限（Floor），确保更新幅度超过量化分箱宽度，从而保证遗忘不被量化抵消。此外，引入了 CAD 指标来衡量结构性擦除。

Result: 在多模型家族和危险基准测试中，MANSU 在遗忘彻底性、性能保持、抗量化复原及结构擦除四个维度上均表现最优；相比之下，现有的梯度基方法在压缩后会出现明显的遗忘恢复（准确率重升高达 +0.05）。

Conclusion: MANSU 是首个能够同时实现彻底遗忘、保留有用信息、抵御量化复原（无正向 PTQ 间隙）以及实现结构性擦除的方法，克服了现有梯度基遗忘方法在面对 4-bit 量化时的失效问题。

Abstract: Standard unlearning evaluations measure behavioral suppression in full precision, immediately after training, despite every deployed language model being quantized first. Recent work has shown that 4-bit post-training quantization can reverse machine unlearning; we show this is not a tuning artefact but a systematic dual failure: gradient-based methods that achieve meaningful forgetting lose it under compression, while methods that survive quantization barely change the model. Both failures trace to the same root cause: across all baselines, per-parameter updates lie 47-828x below the NF4 quantization bin width; updates diffused across billions of parameters cannot clear quantization bin boundaries, a consequence we formalize as a sparsity-permanence tradeoff. We present MANSU (Mechanistic-Aligned Null-Space Unlearning), which resolves both modes by combining causal circuit attribution to isolate the minimal forget-set subgraph, circuit-restricted null-space projection with a diagonal-Fisher retain bound, and a per-parameter magnitude floor guaranteeing quantization survival by construction. We additionally introduce Circuit Attribution Divergence (CAD), a mechanistic verification metric distinguishing structural erasure from behavioral suppression, a distinction existing metrics cannot make. Across multiple model families and hazard benchmarks, MANSU is the first method to jointly satisfy all four properties with margin on each (meaningful forgetting, retain preservation, non-positive PTQ gap, and structural erasure), while gradient-based baselines recover up to +0.05 accuracy under compression.

</details>


### [157] [An Amortized Efficiency Threshold for Comparing Neural and Heuristic Solvers in Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14624)
*Sohaib Afifi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究提出了一个评估神经组合优化求解器能源效率的通用框架（AET），指出神经求解器的高训练成本可在足够大的部署量下被摊销，从而实现比传统启发式算法更节能的长期运营。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对学术界对神经组合优化求解器（Neural Combinatorial Optimization）训练过程高耗能的批评，质疑“训练昂贵”是否直接等同于“整体部署不节能”，旨在通过量化分析评估神经求解器的真实能源效率。

Method: 1. 定义“摊销效率阈值（AET）”：确定神经求解器在达到多大部署量时，其总能源消耗能低于基准启发式算法；2. 建立能源/碳足迹模型：引入包含训练成本（固定）与推理成本（动态）的数学框架；3. 实验验证：以 Multi-Task VRP (MTVRP) 环境及 HGS (PyVRP) 为基准进行实证分析。

Result: 在 MTVRP (n=20) 实验中，神经求解器的盈亏平衡点（AET）约为 1.58 × 10^5 次实例部署。当部署量超过此阈值，神经求解器的累计能源消耗会低于启发式基准（以达到同等解质量为前提），且其单次推理的平均能耗比为 0.41。

Conclusion: 通过定义“摊销效率阈值（AET）”，本研究证明了神经组合优化求解器在特定部署规模下不仅可行，且在能源成本上具备优于传统启发式算法的潜力，打破了“神经求解器不节能”的固有偏见。

Abstract: A common critique of neural combinatorial-optimization solvers is that they are less energy-efficient than CPU metaheuristics, given the operational energy cost of training them on GPUs. This paper examines the inferential step from "training is expensive" to "neural solvers are net-inefficient", which is where the critique actually goes wrong. Training the network costs a large fixed amount of GPU energy; running the metaheuristic costs a small amount of CPU energy on every instance, repeated as long as the solver is deployed. The two are not commensurable until a deployment volume is fixed. We define the Amortized Efficiency Threshold (AET) as the deployment volume above which a neural solver breaks even with a heuristic baseline in total energy or carbon, under an explicit constraint on solution quality. We show that the cumulative-energy ratio between the two solvers tends to a constant strictly below one whenever the network wins per-instance, and that this limit does not depend on how the training cost was measured. An embodied-carbon term amortizes hardware fabrication symmetrically on both sides. We instantiate the framework on the Multi-Task VRP (MTVRP) environment at n=20 customers across 19 problem variants and five training seeds, with HGS via PyVRP as the heuristic baseline. The measured crossover sits near $1.58 \times 10^5$ deployed instances; the per-instance ratio is 0.41, reflecting the moderate size of the instances tested. The contribution is the framework, the open instrumentation, and the measurement protocol; structural convergence of the ratio at larger problem sizes is left to future empirical work.

</details>


### [158] [Self-Distilled Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15155)
*Zhengxi Lu,Zhiyuan Yao,Zhuowen Han,Zi-Han Wang,Jinyang Wu,Qi Gu,Xunliang Cai,Weiming Lu,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SDAR提供了一种在多轮Agent训练中平衡策略梯度和自蒸馏的新方法，通过引入门控机制有效利用细粒度教师信号，显著提升了Agent的交互性能与训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的强化学习（RL）在处理长序列任务时监督信号过于稀疏。虽然在线自蒸馏（OPSD）提供了Token级监督，但在多轮Agent场景中存在稳定性不足以及处理不完美技能检索时的不对称性问题。

Method: 引入SDAR（Self-Distilled Agentic Reinforcement Learning），将OPSD视为门控辅助目标。使用Sigmoid门控机制处理Token级信号，对教师模型认可的正向偏差Token加强蒸馏，同时对负向拒绝进行软衰减，保持强化学习（RL）作为主要优化骨干。

Result: 在Qwen2.5和Qwen3系列模型上，与GRPO相比，在ALFWorld、WebShop和Search-QA基准测试中分别取得了+9.4%、+10.2%和+7.0%的性能提升。

Conclusion: SDAR通过将自蒸馏作为可选的辅助目标，成功解决了多轮Agent训练中的不稳定性问题，在多个具有挑战性的基准测试中优于现有的RL和RL-OPSD方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a central paradigm for post-training LLM agents, yet its trajectory-level reward signal provides only coarse supervision for long-horizon interaction. On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) complements RL by introducing dense token-level guidance from a teacher branch augmented with privileged context. However, transferring OPSD to multi-turn agents proves problematic: compounding multi-turn instability destabilizes supervision, while skill-conditioned privileged guidance requires asymmetric treatment for negative teacher rejections may arise from imperfect skills retrieval or utilization. We introduce SDAR (Self-Distilled Agentic Reinforcement Learning), which treats OPSD as a gated auxiliary objective while keeping RL as the primary optimization backbone. SDAR maps detached token-level signals into a sigmoid gate, strengthening distillation on teacher-endorsed positive-gap tokens and softly attenuating negative teacher rejections. Across the Qwen2.5 and Qwen3 families on ALFWorld, WebShop, and Search-QA, SDAR substantially improves over GRPO (+9.4% on ALFWorld, +7.0% on Search-QA, +10.2% on WebShop-Acc), avoids the instability of naive GRPO+OPSD, and consistently outperforms hybrid RL--OPSD baselines across model scales.

</details>


### [159] [Unbiased and Second-Order-Free Training for High-Dimensional PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14643)
*Jaemin Seo,Surin Lee,Jae Yong Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对BSDE求解高维PDE中Euler-Maruyama离散化带来的损失偏差，提出了一种无需计算二阶导数的无偏训练框架，兼顾了数学精度与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于BSDE的深度学习方法在求解高维PDE时面临Euler-Maruyama (EM) 时间离散化带来的固有损失偏差；而虽然高阶离散化方案（如Heun）能消除此偏差，但它们需要计算二阶空间导数，显著增加了计算负担，降低了该方法的优势。

Method: 对Euler-Maruyama (EM) 导致的损失函数偏差进行了深入的理论分析，并据此设计了一种无需二阶导数计算的改进训练框架，以替代高性能但计算昂贵的高阶方案（如Heun方法）。

Result: 该框架成功实现了无偏训练，同时避免了对二阶导数的显式求解，保留了BSDE方法在解决高维问题时避免维度灾难及计算高效的特性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种无偏且无需二阶导数的BSDE训练框架，在保持计算效率的同时消除了由Euler-Maruyama离散化引起的偏差。

Abstract: Deep learning methods based on backward stochastic differential equations (BSDEs) have emerged as competitive alternatives to physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) for solving high-dimensional partial differential equations (PDEs). By leveraging probabilistic representations, BSDE approaches can avoid the curse of dimensionality and often admit second-order-free training objectives that do not require explicit Hessian evaluations. It has recently been established that the commonly used Euler-Maruyama (EM) time discretization induces an intrinsic bias in BSDE training losses. While high-order schemes such as Heun can fully eliminate this bias, such schemes re-introduce second-order spatial derivatives and incur substantial computational overhead. In this work, we provide a principled analysis of EM-induced loss bias and propose an unbiased, second-order-free training framework that preserves the computational advantages of BSDE methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/seojaemin22/Un-EM-BSDE.

</details>


### [160] [Slower Generalization, Faster Memorization: A Sweet Spot in Algorithmic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14659)
*Shin So,Kyelim Lee,Albert No*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究揭示了在复杂结构化输出任务中，增加训练数据量并不总是能线性加速验证集收敛。存在一个“数据集规模的甜蜜点”，超过该点后，模型泛化所需的训练成本反而可能上升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在挑战关于“grokking（顿悟/突发性泛化）”现象的直觉：即通常认为在数据集足够大时，额外数据应加速验证集收敛。研究者希望探究在结构化输出任务中，这一规律是否始终成立。

Method: 通过在 Needleman-Wunsch (NW) 矩阵生成任务中对比不同数据集规模下小型 Transformer 的训练表现，并设置乘法任务作为基准进行对比分析。

Result: 研究发现存在一个“数据集大小的最佳阈值”，超过该阈值后，虽然模型仍能泛化，但达到高精度所需的梯度更新步数反而增加；而在泛化初期，较大的数据集反而能加速训练收敛到高准确率。

Conclusion: 研究表明，泛化所需的临界数据量与优化收敛速度所需的最佳数据集大小并不一致。在结构化输出任务中，学习规律和完成精确拟合之间存在差异，且这种“数据越多越好”的直觉在特定情境下会失效。

Abstract: Critical-data-size accounts of grokking suggest a natural post-threshold intuition: once training data is sufficient to identify the underlying rule, additional data should accelerate validation convergence. We show that this intuition can fail in a controlled structured-output task. In Needleman--Wunsch (NW) matrix generation, small Transformers reach high validation exact-match accuracy fastest at an intermediate dataset size, not at the largest one. Past this dataset-size sweet spot, generalization remains achievable but requires more gradient updates. Conversely, in the regime where partial validation competence first appears, larger datasets can require fewer updates to reach high training accuracy, suggesting that emerging rule structure can accelerate fitting beyond example-wise memorization. A multiplication baseline does not show the same post-threshold slowdown. These results separate the critical data size for the onset of generalization from the dataset size that optimizes update-based convergence, and identify structured-output tasks where learning the rule and completing exact-fitting can diverge.

</details>


### [161] [AQKA: Active Quantum Kernel Acquisition Under a Shot Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14672)
*Jian Xu,Chao Li,Delu Zeng,John Paisley,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对量子核学习中高昂的测量成本问题，本文提出了AQKA方法，根据下游模型的灵敏度对核矩阵条目进行非均匀的自适应shot分配，在真实量子硬件上显著降低了资源损耗并提高了分类准确度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在近场量子硬件上，估计 $N \times N$ 量子核矩阵需要大量的测量成本（$Θ(N^2 S)$）。现有的预算节省方法多采用均匀分配策略，忽略了不同核矩阵条目对下游分类器性能的影响，导致资源利用效率低下。

Method: 1. 提出了AQKA（自适应量子核分配）方法，根据下游分类器的灵敏度动态分配测量shot；2. 推导了闭式配对级获取理论（Acquisition Theory），给出shot分配公式 $s_{ij}^{\star} \propto |g_{ij}|\sqrt{K_{ij}(1-K_{ij})}$；3. 建立了新的稀疏感知Cauchy-Schwarz界限和可插拔后悔界（regret bound）。

Result: 在预算受限环境下，AQKA显著优于均匀分配及其他现有方法（如ShoFaR），在模拟实验和真实硬件（ibm_pittsburgh, ibm_aachen等）上均实现了分类精度的显著提升（最高提升约32个百分点）。同时，AQKA在不同规模和 budget 限制下展现了比现有方法更强的鲁棒性和扩展性。

Conclusion: AQKA通过实验证明了非均匀shot分配能显著提升量子核学习在预算受限环境下的分类性能。该研究通过闭式理论推导，为shot的最佳分配策略提供了理论支撑，并验证了在真实量子硬件上的有效性。

Abstract: Estimating an $N \times N$ quantum kernel from circuit fidelities requires $Θ(N^2 S)$ measurement shots, the dominant bottleneck for deployment on near-term hardware. Existing budget-saving methods (Nyström-QKE, ShoFaR, kernel-target alignment) sub-sample \emph{which} entries to measure but allocate shots \emph{uniformly} within their chosen subset, ignoring how much each entry drives the downstream classifier. We close this gap with two contributions. \textbf{First, a complete regime decomposition} for shot-budgeted quantum kernel learning: a principled menu of when each allocator wins. Our method, \emph{AQKA}, dominates the budget-limited regime ($B \lesssim 16 n_{\mathrm{pairs}}$) on sparse-sensitivity KRR, with the gap \emph{growing} from $+8$ to $+25$ pts over uniform as $N$ scales $225{\to}1000$ and reaching $+26$--$32$ pts on an \texttt{ibm\_pittsburgh} (156-qubit Heron) hardware kernel; Nyström-QKE wins at saturating budgets on planted-sparse via low-rank reconstruction; ShoFaR is competitive only at extreme low budgets. \textbf{Second, a closed-form pair-level acquisition theory}: $s_{ij}^{\star} \propto |g_{ij}|\sqrt{K_{ij}(1-K_{ij})}$ with explicit gradient $g_{ij}$ for KRR (Lemma~1, $|β_iα_j+β_jα_i|\sqrt{K_{ij}(1-K_{ij})}$) and SVM via the envelope theorem ($|η_i^*η_j^*|\sqrt{K_{ij}(1-K_{ij})}$); a \emph{corrected} sparsity-aware Cauchy--Schwarz rate $ρ\le 2m/N$ matching empirics (vs.\ the naive $m^2/N^2$); an explicit-constant plug-in regret bound (Theorem~2); and a tighter SVM ceiling $ρ^{\mathrm{SVM}} \le m_{\mathrm{sv}}^2/N^2$. We close with the first multi-seed live online adaptive shot allocation on quantum hardware: $+17.0 \pm 4.8$ pts at $N{=}20$ on \texttt{ibm\_aachen} ($3.5σ$, 5 seeds), with the advantage holding at $N{=}30$ at higher budget on \texttt{ibm\_berlin} ($+14.0 \pm 8.5$ pts, 5 seeds).

</details>


### [162] [ReMIA: a Powerful and Efficient Alternative to Membership Inference Attacks against Synthetic Data Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14686)
*Davide Scassola,Andrea Coser,Sebastiano Saccani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ReMIA的实用隐私度量方法，通过对比两组合成数据来评估成员推理风险，在低计算资源消耗下实现了与现有顶级攻击相当的敏感度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的高精度成员推理攻击（MIA）依赖大量的影子模型，计算成本高且需要大量辅助数据；而轻量级指标（如DCR）虽然快速但敏感度不足，难以准确评估合成数据的隐私风险。

Method: 提出了一种名为ReMIA（相对成员推理攻击）的指标。该方法不依赖于影子建模（Shadow Modeling），而是通过对比两个从不同源数据集生成的合成数据集，训练一个二分类器来判断样本的原始归属，仅需两次SDG训练运行及等量辅助数据。

Result: 实验验证了ReMIA在多个表格数据集和SDG模型上的敏感度与现有SOTA MIA方法相当，但显著降低了计算复杂度和辅助数据需求。

Conclusion: ReMIA是一种高效且实用的隐私度量指标，在保持与现有先进成员推理攻击（MIA）相当的敏感度的同时，极大地降低了计算开销和数据需求。研究还表明，合成数据生成器在隐私与效用平衡方面优于传统的噪声掩码匿名化方法。

Abstract: Tabular data sharing under privacy constraints is increasingly important for research and collaboration. Synthetic data generators (SDGs) are a promising solution, but synthetic data remains vulnerable to attacks, such as membership inference attacks (MIAs), which aim to determine whether a specific record was part of the training data. State-of-the-art MIAs are powerful but impractical: they rely on shadow modeling, requiring hundreds of SDG training runs, and need auxiliary data several times larger than the original training set. Fast proxy metrics like distance to closest record (DCR) are efficient but have limited sensitivity to MIA risk. We introduce ReMIA (Relative Membership Inference Attack), a practical privacy metric that requires only two SDG training runs and additional data no larger than the original training set. Rather than predicting whether a record was in the training set, ReMIA generates two synthetic datasets from two source datasets and measures whether a classifier can identify which source a record came from. Experiments across multiple tabular datasets and SDGs show that ReMIA has a sensitivity comparable to state-of-the-art MIAs while being substantially more practical. We further observe that SDGs can achieve privacy-utility trade-offs that traditional noise-based anonymization methods do not match. Code is available at https://github.com/aindo-com/remia.

</details>


### [163] [NeuroAtlas: Benchmarking Foundation Models for Clinical EEG and Brain-Computer Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14698)
*Konstantinos Kontras,Trui Osselaer,Stylianos G. Mouslech,Angeliki-Ilektra Karaiskou,Guido Gagliardi,Thomas Strypsteen,Mohammad Hossein Badiei,Anku Rani,Maarten Vanmarcke,Miguel Bhagubai,Chanakya Ekbote,Jaedong Hwang,Christos Chatzichristos,Paul Pu Liang,Maarten De Vos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeuroAtlas通过大规模基准测试揭示了当前EEG基础模型的局限性：专用模型对比通用模型并无显著性能优势，且由于缺乏临床导向的评估体系，现有模型远未达到临床应用的统一标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前EEG基础模型在不同研究间的评估标准不统一（数据集、预处理、指标各异），且缺乏对临床相关性的深入考量，导致其实际效能和通用性不明。

Method: 构建了目前规模最大的EEG基准测试平台“NeuroAtlas”，包含42个数据集、260k小时录音，覆盖临床诊断（癫痫、睡眠、脑龄）与脑机接口任务；对比了专门的EEG基础模型与通用时间序列基础模型，并采用了针对临床任务的定制化评估指标。

Result: 1. 专用EEG模型并未显著优于通用时间序列模型；2. 标准机器学习指标无法有效反映临床实用性；3. 模型性能在不同任务领域间表现不稳定，缺乏一致的优势。

Conclusion: 目前的EEG基础模型尚未实现“开箱即用”的统一模型愿景，表现仅与通用时间序列模型相当，且缺乏稳健的临床实用性。NeuroAtlas为未来通用EEG基础模型的研发提供了关键的基准测试和评估框架。

Abstract: Foundation models (FMs) promise to extract unified representations that generalize across downstream tasks. They have emerged across fields, including electroencephalography (EEG), but it is less clear how effective they are in this particular field. Published evaluations differ in datasets, in the EEG-specific preprocessing that might influence reported results, and in the reported metrics, frequently obscuring the clinical relevance in EEG. We introduce NeuroAtlas, the largest EEG benchmark to date: 42 datasets and 260k hours covering clinical EEG (epilepsy, sleep medicine, brain age estimation) and brain-computer interfaces, and include multiple datasets per task along with bespoke clinical evaluation metrics. Besides evaluating EEG-FMs with respect to supervised baselines, we present results from generic time-series FMs. We report three findings. First, EEG-specific FMs do not consistently outperform time-series FMs, which have neither EEG-focused architectures nor been pretrained on EEG. Second, standard machine learning metrics are insufficient to assess clinical utility: thus, we thoroughly evaluate more appropriate measures such as the quality of event-level decision-making, hypnogram-derived features, and the brain-age gap in the domains of epilepsy, sleep, and brain age, respectively. Third, model rankings and performance can vary substantially within domains. We conclude that pretrained models perform largely on par, with only narrow advantages for a few, and that current models do not yet deliver on the promise of an out-of-the-box unified EEG model. NeuroAtlas exposes this gap and provides the datasets and metrics for the next generation of unified EEG FMs.

</details>


### [164] [TAPIOCA: Why Task- Aware Pruning Improves OOD model Capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14738)
*Krish Sharma,Omar Naim,Soumadeep Saha,Nicholas Asher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究揭示了任务感知层剪枝的奥秘：它并非提升了通用性能，而是通过“剪除”导致OOD输入产生表示失真的层，实现了输入数据与模型任务适应几何空间的重新对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究（如TALE）表明任务感知层剪枝能提升性能，但其背后的机制尚不明确，尤其是为何在特定分布下有效。

Method: 通过多项式回归任务和大型语言模型进行实验；分析模型层级范数和成对距离分布；利用因果干预实验（如残差缩放）验证各层的效用。

Result: 1. 任务感知剪枝在分布内（ID）数据上无正向增益；2. 该方法始终能提升分布外（OOD）准确率；3. OOD输入会导致模型内部表示几何（范数和距离分布）失真，剪枝能修正这种失真。

Conclusion: 任务感知剪枝通过移除放大分布外（OOD）失真的层，使模型的表示几何形状与任务适应的几何形状重新对齐，从而提升OOD表现。该方法在ID数据上无增益，但在OOD任务中具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: Recent work has promoted task-aware layer pruning as a way to improve model performance on particular tasks, as shown by TALE. In this paper, we investigate when such improvements occur and why. We show first that, across controlled polynomial regression tasks and large language models, such pruning yields no benefit on in-distribution (ID) data but consistently improves out-of-distribution (OOD) accuracy. We further show empirically that OOD inputs induce layerwise norm and pairwise-distance profiles that deviate from the corresponding ID profiles. This leads to a geometric explanation of task-aware pruning: each task induces a task-adapted geometry, characterized empirically by the representation profiles observed on ID inputs. OOD inputs can introduce a distorted version of the task-adapted geometry. Task-aware pruning identifies layers that create or amplify this distortion; by removing them, it shifts OOD representational norms and pairwise distances toward those observed on the adapted distribution. This realigns OOD inputs with the model's task-adapted geometry and improves performance. We provide causal evidence through controlled distribution shifts and residual-scaling interventions, and demonstrate consistent behavior across model scales.

</details>


### [165] [Selective Safety Steering via Value-Filtered Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14746)
*Bat-Sheva Einbinder,Hen Davidov,Yee Whye Teh,Yarin Gal,Yaniv Romano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大语言模型安全引导中“过度干预”导致性能下降的问题，本文提出了一种基于值过滤的解码方法，通过量化控制干预概率，在提高安全性的同时最大限度地保留了模型的原始性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的解码时安全引导方法往往会进行“过度干预”，对原本安全的输出也进行不必要的修改，这不仅破坏了预训练模型原有的有用性、流利度、风格和连贯性，还损害了模型表现。

Method: 提出了一种新的测试时引导（test-time steering）方法。该方法引入了“值过滤（value-based safety criterion）”机制，通过单一阈值超参数来控制“错误干预（false intervention）”的概率上限，仅在模型生成的 token 违背安全准则时才进行干预。

Result: 在多个数据集和实验中，该方法优于现有的 baseline，在安全性提升与保持模型原始生成性能（有用性、相似度）之间达成了更好的权衡。

Conclusion: 提出了“值过滤解码（Value-Filtered Decoding）”方法，通过引入基于值的安全准则并明确干预概率的上限，在确保模型输出安全性的同时，有效避免了对原本安全的响应进行不必要的过度干预，实现了安全性、有用性与模型原始特性之间的更优平衡。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) are trained to align with human values, their generations may still violate safety constraints. A growing line of work addresses this problem by modifying the model's sampling policy at decoding time using a safety reward. However, existing decoding-time steering methods often intervene unnecessarily, modifying generations that would have been safe under the base model. Such unnecessary interventions are undesirable, as they can distort key properties of the base model such as helpfulness, fluency, style, and coherence. We propose a new test-time steering method designed to reduce such unnecessary interventions while improving the safety of unsafe responses. Our approach filters tokens using a value-based safety criterion and provides an explicit bound on the probability of false interventions. A single threshold hyperparameter controls this bound, allowing practitioners to trade off higher rates of unnecessary intervention for better output safety. Across multiple datasets and experiments, we show that our value-filtered decoding method outperforms existing baselines, achieving better trade-offs between safety, helpfulness, and similarity to the base model.

</details>


### [166] [Cognitive-Uncertainty Guided Knowledge Distillation for Accurate Classification of Student Misconceptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14752)
*Qirui Liu,Hao Chen,Weijie Shi,Jiajie Xu,Jia Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对学生误解识别问题，本文提出了一种基于认知不确定性的两阶段知识蒸馏框架，通过精确筛选少量高价值样本并结合难度自适应机制，使得4B小模型在精度上大幅超越了大规模参数模型，有效解决了低资源与部署难的痛点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决学生误解识别中面临的三大难题：数据长尾分布导致的样本稀缺、错误类别边界模糊带来的标注噪声，以及部署时大型模型（预训练偏差且难以端侧部署）与小型模型（易过拟合）之间的权衡矛盾。

Method: 提出一种两阶段知识蒸馏架构：第一阶段进行标准蒸馏以迁移任务能力；第二阶段采用基于认知不确定性的双层边际选择机制（Dual-layer Marginal Selection），根据教师模型的不确定性和置信度差异识别四类关键样本，并结合难度自适应机制（Difficulty-adaptive mechanism）来平衡硬标签与软标签的影响。

Result: 在仅利用10.30%筛选样本进行训练的情况下，MAP-Charting数据集上的MAP@3达到0.9585（提升17.8%）；在使用4B参数小模型的情况下，在初中代数误解基准测试中达到84.38%的准确率，显著超越了SOTA大语言模型（67.73%）及标准微调的72B模型（81.25%）。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种高效的知识蒸馏框架，证明了通过在少量高质量样本上进行针对性的知识迁移，不仅能解决长尾分布和标注噪声问题，还能使小参数模型在学生误解识别任务上超越大规模模型，展现了在资源受限环境下的卓越性能。

Abstract: Accurately identifying student misconceptions is crucial for personalized education but faces three challenges: (1) data scarcity with long-tail distribution, where authentic student reasoning is difficult to synthesize; (2) fuzzy boundaries between error categories with high annotation noise; (3) deployment parado-large models overlook unconventional approaches due to pretraining bias and cannot be deployed on edge, while small models overfit to noise. Unlike traditional methods that increase diversity through large-scale data synthesis, we propose a two-stage knowledge distillation framework that mines high-value samples from existing data. The first stage performs standard distillation to transfer task capabilities. The second stage introduces a dual-layer marginal selection mechanism based on cognitive uncertainty, identifying four types of critical samples based on teacher model uncertainty and confidence differences. For different data subsets, we design difficulty-adaptive mechanism to balance hard/soft label contributions, enabling student models to inherit inter-class relationships from teacher soft labels while distinguishing ambiguous error types. Experiments show that with augmented training on only 10.30% of filtered samples, we achieve MAP@3 of 0.9585 (+17.8%) on the MAP-Charting dataset, and using only a 4B parameter model, we attain 84.38% accuracy on cross-topic tests of middle school algebra misconception benchmarks, significantly outperforming sota LLM (67.73%) and standard fine-tuned 72B models (81.25%). Our code is available at https://github.com/RoschildRui/acl2026_map.

</details>


### [167] [Crys-JEPA: Accelerating Crystal Discovery via Embedding Screening and Generative Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14759)
*Nian Liu,Nikita Kazeev,Stephen Gregory Dale,Artem Maevskiy,Yuwei Zeng,Ryoji Kubo,Pengru Huang,Thomas Laurent,Yann LeCun,Kostya S. Novoselov,Xavier Bresson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为解决晶体生成过程中的稳定性与新颖性矛盾，本文提出了 Crys-JEPA 架构，该方法通过能源感知潜在空间和迭代筛选优化策略，在保持晶体稳定性的同时显著提升了生成材料的新颖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有晶体生成模型陷入了“稳定性-新颖性”权衡：模仿已知分布虽能保证稳定但缺乏新颖性，偏离已知分布则会导致稳定性迅速丧失；发现同时具备稳定且新颖的材料区域极其狭窄。

Method: 提出 Crys-JEPA（晶体联合嵌入预测架构），学习一个能够感知形成能差异的能源感知潜在空间，并将稳定性评估重构为基于嵌入的比较；同时构建了一个“筛选-细化”迭代生成流水线。

Result: 在 MP-20 和 Alex-MP-20 数据集上，该方法在 V.S.U.N（由有效性、稳定性、独特性、新颖性组成的综合指标）上分别取得了高达 81.4% 和 82.6% 的性能提升。

Conclusion: Crys-JEPA 成功克服了晶体生成中“稳定性与新颖性”的权衡困境，通过能源感知潜在空间和迭代优化流程，实现了高质量、高稳定性和高新颖性新材料的发现。

Abstract: De novo crystal generation seeks to discover materials that are not merely realistic, but also stable and novel. However, most existing generative models are trained to maximize the likelihood of observed crystals, which encourages samples to stay close to known materials yet not necessarily align with the criteria that matter in discovery. Through an empirical investigation, we show that current crystal generative models are caught in a pronounced stability--novelty trade-off: moving toward the observed distribution preserves stability but limits novelty, whereas moving away from it quickly destroys stability. This suggests that the useful region for discovering crystals that are both stable and novel is extremely narrow. To escape the trade-off, we introduce Crys-JEPA, a joint embedding predictive architecture for crystals that learns an energy-aware latent space preserving formation-energy differences. In this space, stability assessment can be reformulated as an embedding-based comparison against accessible training crystals, reducing the reliance on expensive energy evaluation and task-specific external references. Building on Crys-JEPA, we further develop a screening-and-refinement pipeline that identifies promising generated crystals and reintroduces them to refine the generative model. On MP-20 and Alex-MP-20 datasets, we achieve improvements over baselines up to 81.4% and 82.6% on V.S.U.N metric, respectively.

</details>


### [168] [Composable Crystals: Controllable Materials Discovery via Concept Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14769)
*Nian Liu,Yuwei Zeng,Ryoji Kubo,Nikita Kazeev,Stephen Gregory Dale,Artem Maevskiy,Pengru Huang,Thomas Laurent,Kostya S. Novoselov,Xavier Bresson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于概念组合的晶体生成框架，通过VQ-VAE学习可解释的“晶体构件”，实现了对晶体结构的受控生成与高效探索，显著提升了生成结构的质量与新颖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有材料发现中的晶体生成方法多依赖黑盒随机采样，缺乏对结构生成过程的控制，难以有效生成超出训练分布的新颖结构。

Method: 1. 训练向量量化变分自编码器（VQ-VAE）以发现可重用的晶体概念（局部原子环境及全局对称模式）；2. 引入概念组合生成器（composition generator），并通过迭代自我强化学习（self-refinement）提升生成效率；3. 利用这些重组后的概念作为条件，引导下游晶体生成模型。

Result: 在MP-20和Alex-MP-20数据集上的实验表明，该方法在V.S.U.N（有效性、稳定性、独特性、新颖性）指标上分别提升了53.2%和51.7%，在结构新颖性方面表现尤为突出。

Conclusion: 通过引入概念组合框架，通过解耦和重组可解释的原子/对称性概念，实现了比传统黑盒随机采样更高效、可控且新颖的晶体结构生成。

Abstract: De novo crystal generation, a central task in materials discovery, aims to generate crystals that are simultaneously valid, stable, unique, and novel. Existing methods mainly rely on black-box stochastic sampling, providing limited control over how generated structures move beyond the observed distribution. In this paper, we introduce a concept-based compositional framework for crystal generation. We train a vector-quantized variational autoencoder to automatically discover a shared set of reusable crystal concepts, which serve as building blocks for guided generation. These learned concepts naturally exhibit interpretability from both local atomic environments and global symmetry patterns, and generalize to crystals from different distributions. By recombining such concepts, our framework enables controllable exploration of novel crystals beyond the training distribution, rather than relying solely on unconstrained random sampling. To further improve composition efficiency, we introduce a composition generator and iteratively refine it using high-quality samples generated by the model itself. The resulting concept compositions are then used to condition downstream crystal generation. Numerical experiments on MP-20 and Alex-MP-20 show that compositing concepts separately increase base model up to 53.2% and 51.7% on V.S.U.N metric, with particular gains in novelty.

</details>


### [169] [Beyond What to Select: A Plug-and-play Oscillatory Data-Volume Scheduling for Efficient Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14773)
*Suorong Yang,Hanqi Zhu,Hai Gan,Fangjian Su,Guang Li,Furao Shen,Soujanya Poria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了PODS框架，通过动态调度数据筛选比例（在正则化与覆盖率间震荡），无需更改现有的样本重要性评分，即可显著加速模型训练并提升最终泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数据筛选方法大多关注“挑选哪些样本”，但忽略了“挑选多少样本”的动态性。研究发现，挑选不同的数据量会带来隐含的正则化效应，存在数据覆盖率与优化保真度之间的权衡。

Method: 提出了PODS（Plug-and-play Oscillatory Data-volume Scheduling）框架。该方法不改变样本筛选标准，而是作为轻量级模块，在训练过程中动态调度筛选比例（Selection Ratio），交替执行低比例的“正则化阶段”和高比例的“恢复阶段”。

Result: PODS能够显著提升效率-泛化权衡。实验显示，在ImageNet-1k上，PODS在减少50%训练成本的同时提升了准确率，并将大模型指令微调（Instruction Tuning）的速度提升了2倍以上且无性能损失。

Conclusion: PODS证明了动态调整数据选择比例（在正则化与高保真训练间切换）是提升数据筛选效率与模型泛化能力的有效策略，且具有广泛的模型与任务兼容性。

Abstract: Data selection accelerates training by identifying representative training data while preserving model performance. However, existing methods mainly focus on designing sample-importance criteria, i.e., deciding what to select, while typically fixing the selected data volume as the target ratio throughout training. Thus, they are often dynamic in sample identity but static in data volume. In this work, we revisit data selection from an optimization perspective and show that selected-data training induces an implicit regularization effect modulated by the instantaneous selection ratio. This reveals a key trade-off: lower ratios amplify selection-induced regularization, whereas higher ratios preserve data coverage and optimization fidelity. Motivated by this insight, we propose PODS, a Plug-and-play Oscillatory Data-volume Scheduling framework. Rather than introducing another sample-scoring metric, PODS serves as a lightweight module that dynamically schedules how much data to select over training. Under the target selection ratio, PODS alternates between low-ratio regularization phases and high-ratio recovery phases to exploit selection-induced regularization without sacrificing optimization stability. With its lightweight, ratio-level, and task-agnostic design, PODS is compatible with existing static and dynamic selection methods and broadly applicable across training paradigms. Experiments across various datasets, architectures, and tasks show that PODS consistently improves the efficiency-generalization trade-off, e.g., reducing ImageNet-1k training cost by 50% with improved accuracy and accelerating LLM instruction tuning by over 2x without performance degradation.

</details>


### [170] [Peng's Q($λ$) for Conservative Value Estimation in Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14779)
*Byeongchan Kim,Min-hwan Oh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CPQL，一种利用保守多步Peng's Q(λ)算子的离线强化学习算法，实现了对值函数的稳健估计，在离线性能及在线微调稳定性上均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有的 offline RL 方法在处理多步估计时存在的不确定性以及过度悲观倾向，旨在通过多步算子优化离线策略学习，解决固定点偏移问题并提升训练性能。

Method: 提出保守Peng's Q(λ)（CPQL）算法。该方法改进了经典的Peng's Q(λ)算子，通过在离线学习中采用保守值估计，利用离线轨迹的多步信息，实现隐含的行为正则化。

Result: 在D4RL基准测试中，CPQL显著优于现有的离线单步强化学习基准；在离线到在线的迁移学习中，能有效防止微调初期的性能骤降，实现稳健提升。

Conclusion: CPQL通过引入保守的Peng's Q(λ)算子，有效解决了离线RL中的值估计过悲观问题，优于现有的单步基准，并为离线到在线的强化学习迁移提供了稳健的初始化策略。

Abstract: We propose a model-free offline multi-step reinforcement learning (RL) algorithm, Conservative Peng's Q($λ$) (CPQL). Our algorithm adapts the Peng's Q($λ$) (PQL) operator for conservative value estimation as an alternative to the Bellman operator. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work in offline RL to theoretically and empirically demonstrate the effectiveness of conservative value estimation with a \textit{multi-step} operator by fully leveraging offline trajectories. The fixed point of the PQL operator in offline RL lies closer to the value function of the behavior policy, thereby naturally inducing implicit behavior regularization. CPQL simultaneously mitigates over-pessimistic value estimation, achieves performance greater than (or equal to) that of the behavior policy, and provides near-optimal performance guarantees -- a milestone that previous conservative approaches could not achieve. Extensive numerical experiments on the D4RL benchmark demonstrate that CPQL consistently and significantly outperforms existing offline single-step baselines. In addition to the contributions of CPQL in offline RL, our proposed method also contributes to the offline-to-online learning framework. Using the Q-function pre-trained by CPQL in offline settings enables the online PQL agent to avoid the performance drop typically observed at the start of fine-tuning and to attain robust performance improvements. Our code is available at https://github.com/oh-lab/CPQL.

</details>


### [171] [Understanding Imbalanced Forgetting in Rehearsal-Based Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14785)
*Alberto Tamajo,Srinandan Dasmahapatra,Rahman Attar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了类增量学习中遗忘不平衡的系统性成因，通过最后层梯度干扰系数成功预测和解释了为何不同类别的遗忘程度存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对类增量学习（CIL）中即便使用均衡回放策略，依然存在严重的类间遗忘不平衡现象，旨在深入探究造成这种现象的深层机制。

Method: 构建了三个基于最后层梯度的系数，用于量化和分析增量步骤中对过去各类别产生的干扰；通过实验验证这些系数对遗忘排名的预测能力及因果相关性。

Result: 实验证明提出的梯度系数能有效预测过去类别的遗忘程度；其中，自诱导干扰（self-induced interference）是导致遗忘不平衡的最主要因素，且受新类干扰系数的影响。

Conclusion: 通过识别导致类间遗忘差异的机制（特别是自诱导干扰），为解决类增量学习中的遗忘不平衡问题提供了理论依据和新的优化方向。

Abstract: Neural networks suffer from catastrophic forgetting in class-incremental learning (CIL) settings. Rehearsal$\unicode{x2013}$replaying a subset of past samples$\unicode{x2013}$is a well-established mitigation strategy. However, recent results suggest that, despite balanced rehearsal allocation, some classes are forgotten substantially more than others. Despite its relevance, this imbalanced forgetting phenomenon remains underexplored. This work shows that imbalanced forgetting arises systematically and severely in rehearsal-based CIL and investigates it extensively. Specifically, we construct, from a principled analysis, three last-layer coefficients that capture different gradient-level sources of interference affecting each past class during an incremental step. We then demonstrate that, together, they reliably predict how past classes will rank in terms of forgetting at the end of that step. While predictive performance alone does not establish causality, these results support the interpretation of the coefficients as a plausible mechanistic account linking last-layer gradient-level interactions during training to class-level forgetting outcomes. Notably, one coefficient$\unicode{x2013}$capturing self-induced interference$\unicode{x2013}$emerges as the strongest predictor, with controlled experiments providing evidence consistent with this coefficient being influenced by the new-class interference coefficient. Overall, our findings provide valuable insights and suggest promising directions for mitigating imbalanced forgetting by reducing class-wise disparities in the identified sources of interference.

</details>


### [172] [GFMate: Empowering Graph Foundation Models with Test-time Prompt Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14809)
*Yan Jiang,Ruihong Qiu,Zi Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对现有图基础模型（GFMs）提示微调过度依赖源域和预训练策略的问题，本文提出了 GFMate 框架，通过独立于预训练的测试时提示调整策略，利用有标签和无标签数据实现高效的跨域性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图提示微调方法存在强烈的域耦合和预训练策略依赖，限制了其泛化能力；此外，现有方法未充分利用测试时的无标签数据，导致在不同图基础模型（GFMs）上的适配效果有限。

Method: 提出了 GFMate 框架：1) 引入在目标域上应用的中心点提示（centroid prompts）和层提示（layer prompts），解耦了对源域和预训练模型的依赖；2) 设计了测试时补充学习目标，充分利用目标域的有标签和无标签数据进行提示微调。

Result: 在 12 个基准数据集上的实验表明，GFMate 显著提高了模型性能，最高提升幅度达 30.63%，且表现出高计算效率。

Conclusion: 提出了一种名为 GFMate 的测试时图提示微调框架，成功实现了与预训练无关的高效图模型适配，在多种基准测试中表现出色。

Abstract: Graph prompt tuning has shown great potential in graph learning by introducing trainable prompts to enhance the model performance in conventional single-domain scenarios. Recent research has extended graph prompts to improve Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) by few-shot tuning auxiliary prompts. Despite their progress, most existing methods embed source-domain information into prompts, which serve either as input to GFMs or encoded during model pre-training. Such prompt entanglement with specific source domains and GFM pre-training strategy restricts their generalisability to other domains and different GFMs. Furthermore, existing GFM prompts merely rely on few-shot tuning for adaptation, neglecting the rich information in unlabelled target domain test data. Motivated by these insights, this paper aims to empower GFMs with pre-training-agnostic test-time graph prompt tuning, named GFMate. GFMate introduces centroid and layer prompts applied after pre-training on target domains, avoiding entanglement with specific source domains and model pre-training. In addition, a test-time complementary learning objective is devised to exploit both labelled and unlabelled target domain data for effective test-time prompt tuning. Extensive experiments on 12 benchmark datasets demonstrate the superior performance and efficiency of GFMate, achieving improvements of up to 30.63%. Code is available at https://github.com/YanJiangJerry/GFMate.

</details>


### [173] [ToMAToMP: Robust and Multi-Parameter Topological Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14824)
*Ludo Andrianirina,Mathieu Carrière*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 ToMAToMP 算法，通过引入多参数持久同调工具，克服了传统 ToMATo 聚类算法在单函数处理、图参数依赖及异常值敏感方面的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 ToMATo 算法受限于需要手动调整图参数、对异常值敏感，且一次仅能处理单个函数的局限性，无法满足多变量数据分析的需求。

Method: 提出 ToMAToMP 算法，利用多参数持久同调中的 MMA 分解技术，扩展了传统 ToMATo 在多函数输入下的应用能力，并证明了其鲁棒性。

Result: 实验证明 ToMAToMP 在多种数据集上均优于现有的拓扑和非拓扑基线算法，具备更好的聚类效能与质量。

Conclusion: ToMAToMP 是首个能够处理多函数输入的拓扑聚类算法，在多变量数据分析中表现优异，并为解决传统 ToMATo 算法的图参数敏感性和异常值敏感问题提供了理论保障。

Abstract: Topological clustering, and its main algorithm ToMATo, is a clustering method from Topological Data Analysis (TDA) which has been applied successfully in several applications during the last few years. This is due to its high versatility, as clusters are detected from the persistent components in the sublevel sets of any user-defined function (gene expression, pixel values, etc), and efficiency, as topological clustering enjoys robustness guarantees. However, ToMATo is also limited in several ways. First, a graph on the data points needs to be provided as a hyper-parameter of the method (whose fine-tuning is left to the user). Second, ToMATo is known to be very sensitive to outlier values in the function range. Finally, and most importantly, ToMATo can only handle one function at a time, whereas it is critical to use several functions in various applications. In this article, we introduce ToMAToMP: the first topological clustering method able to handle several functions at the same time with theoretical guarantees. More specifically, we leverage a recent tool from multi-parameter persistent homology, called MMA decomposition, to design our clustering algorithm, and prove that it enjoys robustness properties. As corollaries, we show that it can be used to make ToMATo independent of graph tuning, and robust to outliers.
