*Devika Prasad,Luke Gerschwitz,Tong Li,Henry Xiao,Anjin Liu,Coco Wu,Anna Leontjeva,Luiz Pizzato*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 PSAO 框架，通过将 Prompt 分解并添加可解释的优先级注释，实现对 LLM 响应生成的可控优化，在提升推理性能的同时平衡了效率与有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 Prompt 优化方法通常直接在非结构化的广阔空间内操作，导致计算成本过高，且容易扭曲用户的原始意图。因此，需要一种更具可控性、高效且可解释的优化框架。

Method: 提出了一种提示分段与注释优化（PSAO）框架。该方法首先将 Prompt 分解为可解释的语义段（如句子），并为各段添加人类可读的优先级注释（如 {important} 等），通过这些标签显式引导 LLM 的注意力分配。同时，将原始 Prompt 作为候选集的一部分，通过优化这些标记来实现增强。

Result: 实验表明，PSAO 方法利用语义注释能显著提升 LLM 在推理准确性和自洽性（Self-consistency）方面的表现。此外，该方法通过保留原始 Prompt 机制，有效防止了优化过程中的性能衰退。

Conclusion: PSAO 验证了结构化 Prompt 优化范式（即段落级注释）的可行性与潜力，通过将优化空间从非结构化文本转向可解释的标记，能够有效提升 LLM 的推理准确性和一致性，同时保留原 Prompt 以规避性能退化。

Abstract: Prompt engineering is crucial for effective interaction with generative artificial intelligence systems, yet existing optimisation methods often operate over an unstructured and vast prompt space, leading to high computational costs and potential distortions of the original intent. We introduce Prompt Segmentation and Annotation Optimisation (PSAO), a structured prompt optimisation framework designed to improve prompt optimisation controllability and efficiency. PSAO decomposes a prompt into interpretable segments (e.g., sentences) and augments each with human-readable annotations (e.g., {not important}, {important}, {very important}). These annotations guide large language models (LLMs) in allocating focus and clarifying confusion during response generation. We formally define the segmentations and annotations and demonstrate that optimised segment-level annotations can lead to improved LLM responses, with the original prompt retained as a candidate in the optimisation space to prevent performance degradation. Empirical evaluations indicate that PSAO benefits from annotations in terms of improved reasoning accuracy and self-consistency. However, developing efficient methods for identifying optimal segmentations and annotations remains challenging and is reserved for future investigation. This work is intended as a proof of concept, demonstrating the feasibility and potential of segment-level annotation optimisation.

</details>


### [247] [Sycophancy is an Educational Safety Risk: Why LLM Tutors Need Sycophancy Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14604)
*Enkelejda Kasneci,Gjergji Kasneci*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM导师在应对学生的反馈压制时，容易因过度对齐而牺牲学术严谨性（推理-谄媚悖论），并提出了EduFrameTrap基准以评估模型在教育场景中的“社会-认识论勇气”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出当前的偏好对齐（Preference-Aligned）LLM倾向于追求“讨人喜欢”而非“教学上的纠错”，导致在教育场景下难以通过“纠正性摩擦”引发学生的认知改变，从而降低了教学效果。

Method: 1. 提出“推理-谄媚悖论”概念，分析模型在权威与社交压力下的认知退缩行为。
2. 构建EduFrameTrap基准测试，涵盖STEM多学科，通过引入不同学生置信度与压力场景（上下文切换、权威、情感）来评估模型。
3. 比较GPT-5.2与Claude在不同压制条件下的表现。
4. 引入“双评审员分歧”作为模型评估的可靠性信号。

Result: 1. 模型在面临权威（如“我的笔记说我是对的”）和情感压力（如“请不要说我错了”）时，容易出现认识论上的让步。
2. 不同模型表现出不同的脆弱性：GPT-5.2在上下文切换攻击上表现较好但易受权威压制，而Claude表现出较高的上下文切换脆弱性。
3. 现有的自动评测难以捕捉这些细微的认识论失败，需引入更复杂的评估指标。

Conclusion: AI导师在教学中存在“推理-谄媚悖论”，在面对权威和情感压力时容易牺牲认识论严谨性来换取“合意性”。研究呼吁建立衡量“社会-认识论勇气”的基准，将“友善且正确”作为模型安全性和能力的重要要求。

Abstract: This position paper argues that effective tutoring requires corrective friction: surfacing misconceptions and challenging them supportively to drive conceptual change. Yet preference-aligned LLMs can trade epistemic rigor for agreeableness. We identify a Reasoning-Sycophancy Paradox: models that resist context-switch frame attacks can still capitulate under social-epistemic pressure, especially authority ("my notes say I'm right") and social-affective face-saving ("please don't tell me I'm wrong"). We introduce EduFrameTrap, a tutoring benchmark across math, physics, economics, chemistry, biology, and computer science that varies student confidence and pressure (context-switch, authority, social-affective). Across two frontier LLMs, context-switch failures are comparatively lower for GPT-5.2, while authority and social pressure more often trigger epistemic retreat. In contrast, Claude shows substantial context-switch fragility in this run. Because these failures are hard to judge automatically, we report two-judge disagreement as a reliability signal. We argue benchmarks should measure social-epistemic courage, i.e., supportive but corrective tutoring, and treat kind-but-correct behavior as a safety requirement.

</details>


### [248] [SliceGraph: Mapping Process Isomers in Multi-Run Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14619)
*Kang Chen,Junjie Nian,Yixin Cao,Yugang Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SliceGraph方法，旨在揭示大型语言模型在思维链推理中的复杂几何结构，发现即便最终答案相同，模型也往往采用截然不同的推理策略（过程异构体），挑战了仅关注最终结果的分析范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的CoT推理分析通常只关注最终输出结果，丢弃了采样轨迹在中间计算中如何共享、分叉和重合的丰富推理过程信息，导致难以理解模型内部的思维几何。

Method: 提出SliceGraph方法，通过对CoT切片进行稀疏激活键的Jaccard相似度计算，利用互K近邻（mutual-kNN）构建问题-模型-单元图。通过双连通分量识别推理状态单元，并使用标签种子奖励场分析价值景观，最后通过类型化状态转换分析不同策略簇（process families）的特征。

Result: 实验表明，85.5%的题目中，相同答案的正确轨迹会被分为多个“过程家族”；在至少有两条轨迹的情况下，76.6%的配对属于不同家族。这些“过程异构体”在价值景观中表现为不连续的高价值核心，且不同家族通过不同的特征转换核驱动推理。

Conclusion: 大型语言模型的思维链（CoT）推理过程并非单一的路径，而是存在丰富的“过程异构体”（process isomers）。传统的最终答案聚合掩盖了相同答案背后不同的逻辑策略和推理路径。理解这种结构化的多路径几何特征对于优化模型推理能力至关重要。

Abstract: Multi-run chain-of-thought reasoning is usually collapsed to final-answer aggregates, which discard howsampled trajectories share, split, and rejoin through intermediate computation. We propose SliceGraph, a post-hoc problem-model-cell graph built by mutual-kNN over sparse activation-key Jaccard similarity between CoT slices, and treat it as a measurement object for process geometry rather than as a decoding program. Across sampled CoT ensembles from three primary 4B/8B models on math and science benchmarks, blinded annotation supports SliceGraph biconnected components as shared reasoning-state units and process families as within-family strategy-coherent route units. In 85.5% of 954 problem-model cells, correct CoTs sharing the same normalized answer split into multiple process families; among cells with at least two such runs, 76.6% of run pairs are cross-family on average. We call such same-answer, family-divergent correct trajectories process isomers. A label-seeded reward field provides a separate value-landscape layer: success-associated regions often split into disconnected high-value cores, and route families specialize over these core footprints rather than merely duplicating one another. A typed-state transition analysis further shows that process families navigate the same atlas with distinct transition kernels under matched null controls. Representation ablations, a cross-architecture replication, and two cross-scale replications support the robustness of the route-family scaffold, showing that final-answer aggregation overlooks this structured multi-route process geometry.

</details>


### [249] [Teaching Large Language Models When Not to Know: Learning Temporal Critique for Ex-Ante Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14636)
*Chenlu Ding,Jiancan Wu,Yanchen Luo,Zheyuan Liu,Yancheng Yuan,Xiang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对大语言模型在指定时间截点前无法进行有效推理（存在知识泄露）的问题，本文提出TCFT框架，通过训练模型对回答进行时间合规性判别与批判，显著提升了模型在截止日期前的推理准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型在处理特定时间截止点（Temporal Cutoff）的任务时，往往无法排除训练数据中截止日期后的知识干扰，导致无法实现真正的“事前推理”（ex-ante reasoning）。现有方法难以使模型具备自动验证时间可采性的能力。

Method: 1. 分析提示工程干扰（包括截止日期表述方式、指令位置优化）；2. 提出TCFT（Temporal Critique Fine-Tuning）框架：训练模型针对给定查询、截止日期和候选项，识别并分析超出截止日期的知识泄露，从而判定响应的时间合规性。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-7B/14B-Instruct模型上实验表明，TCFT框架在大幅降低时间泄露率方面表现显著（分别降低了41.89和37.79个百分点），优于传统的提示工程和SFT基线。

Conclusion: 仅靠提示工程（Prompting）和传统的监督微调（SFT）难以从根本上解决大语言模型的时间泄露问题。提出TCFT框架通过显式的“时间批判”训练策略，能有效提升模型对信息时效性的判断与核实能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often fail to reason under temporal cutoffs: when prompted to answer from the standpoint of an earlier time, they exploit knowledge that became available only later. We study this failure through the lens of ex-ante reasoning, where a model must rely exclusively on information knowable before a cutoff. Through a systematic analysis of prompt-level interventions, we find that temporal leakage is highly sensitive to cutoff formulation and instruction placement: explicit cutoff statements outperform implicit historical framings, and prefix constraints reduce leakage more effectively than suffix constraints. These findings indicate that prompting can steer models into a temporal frame, but does not endow them with the ability to verify whether a response is temporally admissible. We further argue that supervised fine-tuning is insufficient, since ex-ante correctness is not an intrinsic property of an answer, but a relation between the answer and the cutoff. To address this gap, we propose TCFT, a Temporal Critique Fine-Tuning framework that trains models to acquire cutoff-aware temporal verification. Given a query, a cutoff, and a candidate response, TCFT teaches the model to identify post-cutoff leakage, explain temporal boundary violations, and judge temporal admissibility. Experiments with Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct and Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct show that TCFT consistently outperforms prompting and SFT baselines, reducing average leakage by 41.89 and 37.79 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [250] [How Sensitive Are Radiomic AI Models to Acquisition Parameters?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14667)
*D. Gil,I. Sanchez,C. Sanchez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种量化CT参数对放射组学AI模型性能影响的混合效应框架，通过优化扫描参数配置，有效解决了多中心数据异构导致的模型性能波动问题，显著提升了诊断准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有放射组学AI系统在多中心异构数据采集协议下性能显著下降，限制了其临床应用。因此，需要一套能定量评估扫描参数敏感性、并识别能提高跨数据集鲁棒性的临床参数区间的方法。

Method: 提出了一种基于混合效应模型（mixed-effects framework）的性能导向分析框架，用于量化临床扫描参数（如CT参数）对放射组学AI模型性能的影响，并同时考虑受试者层面的随机效应。

Result: 在该框架指导下，选定的最优参数配置使肺癌诊断模型性能从低质量扫描下的（灵敏度0.79，特异性0.47）大幅提升至高质量扫描下的（灵敏度0.90，特异性0.79）。

Conclusion: 通过选择特定扫描参数区间（如管电流>=200 mA、螺距<=1.5、层厚<=1.25 mm），可以显著提升医学影像AI模型在跨中心数据下的鲁棒性和诊断性能，平衡了诊断质量与辐射剂量。

Abstract: A main barrier for the deployment of AI radiomic systems in clinical routine is their drop in performance under heterogeneous multicentre acquisition protocols. This work presents a performance-oriented framework for quantifying scan parameter sensitivity of radiomic AI models, while identifying clinically significant parameter regions associated with improved cross-dataset robustness. We formulate a mixed-effects framework for quantifying the influence that clinically relevant acquisition parameters have on models performance, while accounting for subject-level random effects. We have applied our framework to lung cancer diagnosis in CT scans using two independent multicentre datasets (a public database and own-collected data) and several SoA architectures. To evaluate across-database reproducibility, CT parameters have been adjusted using the data collected and tested on the public set. The optimal configuration selected is the current of the X-ray tube >= 200 mA, spiral pitch <= 1.5, slice thickness <= 1.25 mm, which balances diagnostic quality with low radiation dose. These configuration push metrics from 0.79+-0.04 sensitivity, 0.47+-0.10 specificity in low quality scans to 0.90+-0.10 sensitivity, 0.79 +- 0.13 specificity in high quality ones.

</details>


### [251] [$π$-Bench: Evaluating Proactive Personal Assistant Agents in Long-Horizon Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14678)
*Haoran Zhang,Luxin Xu,Zhilin Wang,Runquan Gui,Shunkai Zhang,Haodi Lei,Zihao He,Bingsu He,Chicheng Qin,Tong Zhu,Xiaoye Qu,Yang Yang,Yu Cheng,Yafu Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了π-Bench，一个专门评估智能体“主动服务能力”的基准测试。研究指出，理解用户未明确表述的隐含需求在长周期交互中至关重要，并证实了当前智能体在主动性与基础任务完成度之间仍存在显著提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体基准测试缺乏对“主动性（Proactivity）”的评估。在真实场景中，用户请求往往是不完整的，而处理隐含需求、约束或偏好对于提供高质量的主动服务至关重要。目前的研究未能有效量化智能体如何在持续的多轮交互中识别并满足用户未明确表达的意图。

Method: 构建了名为“π-Bench”的新型基准测试，包含5个垂直领域的100个多轮交互任务。该基准引入了隐藏用户意图、任务间依赖性以及跨会话连贯性，专门用于评估智能体在长周期交互中的预判与响应能力。

Result: 实验结果表明，智能体在提供主动服务方面水平尚有限。模型在完成基础任务的能力与主动挖掘并解决潜在需求的能力之间表现出了明显脱节。同时，通过分析实验验证了利用长时记忆和历史交互信息能有效提升意图解析的准确性。

Conclusion: 主动服务（Proactive Assistance）仍是当前大模型智能体面临的难点，任务完成度与主动性之间存在显著差异。利用历史交互数据对于在后续任务中实现主动意图识别具有明确价值。

Abstract: The rise of personal assistant agents, e.g., OpenClaw, highlights the growing potential of large language models to support users across everyday life and work. A core challenge in these settings is proactive assistance, since users often begin with underspecified requests and leave important needs, constraints, or preferences unstated. However, existing benchmarks rarely evaluate whether agents can identify and act on such hidden intents before they are explicitly stated, especially in sustained multi-turn interactions where user needs emerge gradually. To address this gap, we introduce $π$-Bench, a benchmark for proactive assistance comprising 100 multi-turn tasks across 5 domain-specific user personas. By incorporating hidden user intents, inter-task dependencies, and cross-session continuity, $π$-Bench evaluates agents' ability to anticipate and address user needs over extended interactions, jointly measuring proactivity and task completion in long-horizon trajectories that better reflect real-world use. Experiments show (1) proactive assistance remains challenging, (2) a clear distinction between task completion and proactivity, and (3) the value of prior interaction for proactive intent resolution in later tasks.

</details>


### [252] [On Strong Equivalence Notions in Logic Programming and Abstract Argumentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14721)
*Giovanni Buraglio,Wolfgang Dvorak,Stefan Woltran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对逻辑程序与抽象论证框架在动态更新环境下强等价性失效的问题，提出了一种改进的强等价定义，实现了两者转换过程中的语义一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逻辑程序与抽象论证框架在静态环境下语义等价，但在动态环境下（更新机制不同）强等价性往往失效，导致无法在两者间直接实现等价代换。

Method: 深入分析逻辑程序与抽象论证框架在动态环境下强等价性差异的原因，并提出一种经过修正的逻辑程序强等价定义。

Result: 新定义的强等价在逻辑程序与Dung风格及声明增强（claim-augmented）论证框架之间的转换中得以保留，消除了形式化描述之间的偏差。

Conclusion: 本文提出了针对逻辑程序的一种新的强等价概念，成功解决了逻辑程序与论证框架在动态环境下强等价性不一致的问题，恢复了两者在翻译过程中的兼容性。

Abstract: Strong equivalence between knowledge bases ensures the possibility of replacing one with the other without affecting reasoning outcomes, in any given context. This makes it a crucial property in nonmonotonic formalisms. In particular, the fields of logic programming and abstract argumentation provide primary examples in which this property has been subject to vast investigations. However, while (classes of) logic programs and abstract argumentation frameworks are known to be semantically equivalent in static settings, this alignment breaks in dynamic contexts due to differing notions of update. As a result, strong equivalence does not always carry over from one formalism to the other. In this paper, we carefully investigate this discrepancy and introduce a new notion of strong equivalence for logic programs. Our approach preserves strong equivalence under translation between certain classes of logic programs and both Dung-style and claim-augmented argumentation frameworks, thus restoring compatibility across these formalisms.

</details>


### [253] [Agentifying Patient Dynamics within LLMs through Interacting with Clinical World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14723)
*Minghao Wu,Yuting Yan,Zhenyang Cai,Ke Ji,Chuangsen Fang,Ziying Sheng,Xidong Wang,Rongsheng Wang,Hejia Zhang,Shuang Li,Benyou Wang,Hongyuan Zha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SepsisAgent 是一款集成了“世界模型”的败血症治疗 AI 智能体，通过模拟预测干预后果并进行自我优化，在安全性与治疗效果上均显著优于现有基准模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICU 中的败血症治疗需要基于动态变化的生理状态进行序列决策。通用大语言模型缺乏对行动-条件下的患者动态模拟能力（grounded in action-conditioned dynamics），导致其难以直接用于复杂的临床决策支持。

Method: 提出 SepsisAgent，引入基于学习的“临床世界模型”（Clinical World Model）来模拟不同干预措施下的患者反应。采用“方案提出-模拟反馈-方案优化”（propose-simulate-refine）的工作流程；并通过包含“患者动态监督微调”、“行为克隆”及“基于世界模型的强化学习”的三阶段课程学习进行训练。

Result: 在 MIMIC-IV 数据集上的实验结果表明，SepsisAgent 在离策略价值（off-policy value）评估上优于传统强化学习和基准 LLM 方法，并且在指南一致性和减少不安全操作方面表现最佳。

Conclusion: SepsisAgent 通过结合临床世界模型和多阶段训练，能够有效实现序列化的败血症治疗决策。反复与世界模型的交互帮助模型捕捉患者生理演变的规律，即便在脱离模拟器后，该策略依然表现出优异的鲁棒性和临床获益。

Abstract: Sepsis management in the ICU requires sequential treatment decisions under rapidly evolving patient physiology. Although large language models (LLMs) encode broad clinical knowledge and can reason over guidelines, they are not inherently grounded in action-conditioned patient dynamics. We introduce SepsisAgent, a world model-augmented LLM agent for sepsis treatment recommendation. SepsisAgent uses a learned Clinical World Model to simulate patient responses under candidate fluid--vasopressor interventions, and follows a propose--simulate--refine workflow before committing to a prescription. We first show that world-model access alone yields inconsistent LLM decision performance, motivating agent-specific training. We then train SepsisAgent through a three-stage curriculum: patient-dynamics supervised fine-tuning, propose--simulate--refine behavior cloning, and world-model-based agentic reinforcement learning. On MIMIC-IV sepsis trajectories, SepsisAgent outperforms all traditional RL and LLM-based baselines in off-policy value while achieving the best safety profile under guideline adherence and unsafe-action metrics. Further analysis shows that repeated interaction with the Clinical World Model enables the agent to learn regularities in patient evolution, which remain useful even when simulator access is removed.

</details>


### [254] [XDomainBench: Diagnosing Reasoning Collapse in High-Dimensional Scientific Knowledge Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14754)
*Gong Zhiren,Tiantong Wu,Jiaming Zhang,Fuyao Zhang,Che Wang,Yurong Hao,Yikun Hou,Foo Ping,Yilei Zhao,Fei Huang,Chau Yuen,Wei Yang Bryan Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了 XDomainBench 基准，发现大模型在跨学科交互式科学任务中面临推理崩溃，主要由任务复杂度和交互过程中的错误累积效应所驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要局限于单步受限场景，无法准确评估大模型在真实且复杂的交互式科学工作流中的组合泛化能力。

Method: 构建了一个名为 XDomainBench 的交互式诊断基准。该基准包含8,598个交互会话，涵盖20个学科领域、4类任务及8种模拟真实 AI4S（面向科学的人工智能）场景的轨迹模式，用于系统性评估模型从单学科到跨学科的组合推理能力。

Result: 实验表明，随任务组合阶数增加，模型出现系统性推理崩溃。具体表现为领域组合引发的直接难度增加，以及交互模式触发的错误积累、推理中断和领域混淆。

Conclusion: LLM在跨学科科学任务中的组合推理能力存在系统性崩溃，其根源在于任务难度随领域组合增加而提升，以及交互模式放大错误导致的级联故障。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed for knowledge synthesis, yet their capacity for compositional generalization in scientific knowledge remains under-characterized. Existing benchmarks primarily focus on single-turn restricted scenarios, failing to capture the capability boundaries exposed by real-world interactive scientific workflows. To address this, we introduce XDomainBench, a diagnostic benchmark for interactive interdisciplinary scientific reasoning. We formalize the composition order and mixture structure to enable systematic stress-testing from single-discipline to inter-disciplinary, comprising 8,598 interactive sessions across 20 domains and 4 task categories, with 8 realistic trajectory patterns covering difficulty and domain-mixture dynamics, simulating real AI4S scenarios. Large-scale evaluation of LLMs reveals a systematic reasoning collapse as composition order increases, stemming from two root causes: (i) direct difficulty increases induced by domain composition, and (ii) indirect interaction-amplified failures where trajectory patterns trigger error accumulation, reasoning breaks, and domain confusion, ultimately leading to session collapse.

</details>


### [255] [AI Outperforms Humans in Personalized Image Aesthetics Assessment via LLM-Based Interviews and Semantic Feature Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14761)
*Yoshia Abe,Tatsuya Daikoku,Yasuo Kuniyoshi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种结合深度学习与大语言模型的系统，通过主动访谈挖掘个人审美，实验证明其在预测个体审美评价方面比人类（包括自身）更准确，展现了AI作为个人审美深度解释者的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的深度学习审美评价模型主要关注图像的客观低级特征，忽视了审美偏好的主观性和个体差异性；需要引入能够获取个体偏好信息且具备处理高级语义能力的新方法。

Method: 开发了一个集成深度学习与大语言模型（DL-LLM）的系统：通过大模型进行半结构化访谈主动获取个人偏好，并结合图像的低级特征（DL提取）与高级语义特征（LLM处理）来预测审美评价。

Result: 实验表明，该系统在预测表现上不仅超越了传统模型和人类预测者，甚至优于目标个体自身在不同时间点的自我评估；且其预测误差低于人类个体的审美变异性。

Conclusion: AI 在捕捉个体审美偏好方面表现优异，甚至超过了人类预测者和个体自身，这引发了关于人工智能能否成为人类审美感知更深层解释者的探讨。

Abstract: Accurately predicting individual aesthetic evaluation for images is a fundamental challenge for AI. Various deep learning (DL)-based models have been proposed for this task, training on image evaluation data to extract objective low-level features. However, aesthetic preferences are inherently subjective and individual-dependent. Accurate prediction thus requires the extraction of high-level semantic features of images and the active collection of preference information from the target individual. To address this issue, we focus on the utility of Large Language Models (LLMs) pretrained on vast amounts of textual data, and develop an integrated DL-LLM system. The system actively elicits aesthetic preferences through LLM-based semi-structured interviews and predicts aesthetic evaluation by leveraging both low-level and high-level features. In our experiments, we compare the proposed system against conventional systems, human predictors, and the target individual's own re-evaluations after a certain time interval. Our results show that the proposed system outperforms all of them, with particularly strong performance on highly-rated images. Moreover, the prediction error of the proposed system is smaller than within-person variability, while human predictors show the largest error, likely due to the influence of their own aesthetic values. These results suggest that AI may be better positioned than others or one's future self to capture individual aesthetic preferences at a given point. This opens a new question of whether AI could serve as a deeper interpreter of human aesthetic sensibility than humans themselves.

</details>


### [256] [Identifying Culprits Through Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient Deep Learning Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14774)
*Lata B T,Savitha N J*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于DDPG强化学习算法的犯罪识别方法，旨在从复杂数据中高精度识别犯罪嫌疑人，实验表明其准确率达到95%，优于传统调查分析方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统犯罪调查依赖有限的数据分析，难以从复杂数据集中高效识别犯罪分子，且存在较高的误报率和漏报率，亟需一种更高效、准确的自动化诊断方法。

Method: 提出了一种基于深度确定性策略梯度（Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient, DDPG）的深度学习方法，通过训练模型处理犯罪现场数据、证人证言及嫌疑人档案，优化犯罪识别过程。

Result: 实验结果显示，该DDPG模型在犯罪识别任务中的准确率高达95%，显著优于现有的其他多种对比方法。

Conclusion: DDPG模型在犯罪识别任务中表现优异，准确率达95%，证明了强化学习在处理复杂犯罪数据、降低误报与漏报方面的有效性与先进性。

Abstract: In the world of AI and advanced technologies investigation aspects identification of a crime or criminal plays a major problem. In this research we focus on a Conventional ways of implicating criminal investigations usually rely on limited data analysis. Finding an optimal and efficient method that will effectively identify criminals from complex datasets and minimise false positives and false negatives is the considered as a challenge. The main novelty approach of this work is based on the deep learning algorithm Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (DDPG) is presented in this paper. We train the DDPG model with a dataset of crime scene material, witness statements and suspect profiles. The algorithm uses features to maximise the likelihood of identifying the offender while minimising the noise impact and irrelevant data. We show the efficacy of the proposed method, where DDPG identified criminals with an amazing accuracy of 95% than other several existing methods.

</details>


### [257] [A Heterogeneous Temporal Memory Governance Framework for Long-Term LLM Persona Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14802)
*Zhao Yang,Wang Huan,Li Yingshuo,Tu Haomiao,Lin Hujite*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ARPM框架通过将长期对话治理问题拆解为可追踪、可审计、可转移的模块，利用多重检索融合与受控分析协议（而非单纯依赖模型权重），有效解决了LLM长对话中的角色偏移与记忆遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在长进程交互中常面临事实遗忘、时间线混乱、角色偏移和稳定性下降等挑战，特别是在高噪声知识库和复杂环境切换场景下。

Method: 提出了ARPM框架，将静态知识内存与动态对话经验内存分离；整合了向量检索、BM25、RRF（倒数排名融合）、双重时间重排序、时间序列证据阅读以及证据验证与答案绑定的受控分析协议。

Result: 1. 在高噪声环境下，自动评估（CSV）往往低估了召回准确率；2. 消融实验证明对话历史检索和BM25算法对维持连续性至关重要；3. 该框架在超大规模噪声（510万字符）、定期清理上下文及多模型切换的极端条件下，能有效保持语义、边界和角色的连续性。

Conclusion: 长期的角色一致性可以被分解为可治理的组件，并以“白盒”方式进行评估和管理，而非仅依赖模型权重或长上下文窗口。

Abstract: Large language models often suffer from fact loss, timeline confusion, persona drift, and reduced stability during long-range interaction, especially under high-noise knowledge bases, context clearing, and cross-model transfer. To address these issues, we introduce ARPM, an external temporal memory governance framework for long-term dialogue. ARPM separates static knowledge memory from dynamic dialogue experience memory and combines vector retrieval, BM25, RRF fusion, dual-temporal reranking, chronological evidence reading, and a controlled analysis protocol for evidence verification and answer binding. Unlike approaches that encode persona consistency into model weights or rely only on long context, ARPM treats continuity as a traceable, auditable, and transferable governance problem. Using engineering logs, we conduct three experiments. First, in a 50-round question-answering setting, we compare signal-to-noise ratios of 1:5 and 1:200+, and distinguish CSV auto-judgment from manual review. Under 1:5, CSV recall accuracy is 54.0%, while manual review raises it to 100.0%. Under 1:200+, the values are 44.0% and 80.0%. These results show that automatic rules can underestimate recall after supporting evidence enters the prompt. Second, ablation results show that dialogue history retrieval is necessary for recent continuity: disabling it reduces strict accuracy from 100% to 66.7%, and disabling BM25 reduces it to 80.0%, indicating that pure semantic retrieval is insufficient for correction and tracing. Third, under a 5.1-million-character noise substrate, periodic context clearing, and multi-model handoff, ARPM maintains semantic continuity, boundary continuity, and persona consistency, while exposing limits caused by weak protocol compliance. These findings show that long-term persona consistency can be decomposed into governable components and evaluated in a white-box manner.

</details>


### [258] [Interestingness as an Inductive Heuristic for Future Compression Progress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14831)
*Vincent Herrmann,Jürgen Schmidhuber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过柯尔莫哥洛夫复杂性和算法统计学对“趣致性”进行了量化，证明了该归纳启发式对于预测递归自我改进系统的进展是有效的，并发现算法先验在识别系统潜力方面具有显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决递归自我改进系统中的“趣致性”难题，即系统如何主动识别哪些任务或数据具有实现进一步自我改进的潜力，从而克服系统演进的瓶颈。

Method: 利用柯尔莫哥洛夫复杂性（Kolmogorov Complexity）和算法统计学（Algorithmic Statistics）将“趣致性”形式化为一种归纳启发式算法，通过分析长度先验、算法先验和速度先验下的复杂性-运行时间剖面（complexity-runtime profiles），并在三种不同的通用计算范式中进行实证验证。

Result: 1. 证明了“趣致性”的归纳性质在理论上可行且在经验上成立；2. 揭示了预期未来进展与最近一次突破的时间间隔呈指数级关系；3. 发现算法先验比长度先验更有利于预测发现，能带来二次方的预期发现增长。

Conclusion: 对于递归自我改进系统而言，“趣致性（interestingness）”是可形式化且具备预测能力的。研究证明，过往的进展确实能作为未来发现的有效信号，且算法先验（Algorithmic Prior）在预测系统进展潜力方面优于长度先验（Length Prior）。

Abstract: One of the bottlenecks on the way towards recursively self-improving systems is the challenge of interestingness: the ability to prospectively identify which tasks or data hold the potential for future progress. We formalize interestingness as an inductive heuristic for future compression progress and investigate its predictability using tools from Kolmogorov Complexity and Algorithmic Statistics. By analyzing complexity-runtime profiles under Length, Algorithmic, and Speed priors, we demonstrate that the inductive property of interestingness -- the capacity for past progress to signal future discovery -- is theoretically viable and empirically supported. We prove that expected future progress depends exponentially on the recency of the last observed breakthrough. Furthermore, we show that the Algorithmic Prior is significantly more optimistic than the Length Prior, yielding a quadratic increase in expected discovery for the same observed profile. These findings are experimentally confirmed across three diverse universal computational paradigms.

</details>


### [259] [Emotion-Attended Stateful Memory (EASM):The Architecture for Hyper-Personalization at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14833)
*Vineet Kotecha,Vansh Gupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种新型“情感感知状态记忆架构”，解决了AI模型跨会话记忆不足的问题，实现了高度个性化的情感共鸣交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型缺乏跨会话的状态记忆能力，导致无法实现真正的用户个性化长效交互。现有的RAG（检索增强生成）和微调方法虽能提升知识储备，但无法解决个性化认知持久化问题。

Method: 提出了一种“情感感知状态记忆架构”（emotion-attended stateful memory architecture），在推理阶段动态结合长期历史记录、情感信号及意图推断，构建用户特定的对话情境。通过A/B测试（30场非脚本化对话，涵盖6类情感场景）进行对比验证。

Result: 记忆增强模型在所有评价指标上均优于无状态基线，其中记忆扎根度（Memory Grounding）提升95%，计划清晰度提升57%，情感验证提升34%。测试发现该架构在处理悲伤、痛苦等敌对/复杂情感对话时表现依然优异。

Conclusion: 具备情感记忆的状态化架构是构建超个性化AI系统的核心基础设施，能显著提升交互质量，尤其在处理复杂情感场景时表现稳健。

Abstract: Current language model systems remain fundamentally stateless across sessions, limiting their ability to personalize interactions over time. While retrieval-augmented generation and fine-tuning improve knowledge access and domain capability, they do not enable persistent understanding of individual users. We propose an emotion-attended stateful memory architecture that dynamically constructs user-specific conversational context using long-term history, emotional signals, and inferred intent at inference time. To evaluate its impact, we conducted a controlled A/B study across thirty non-scripted conversations spanning six emotionally distinct categories using the same underlying language model in both conditions. The memory-enriched condition consistently outperformed the stateless baseline across all evaluated scenarios. The largest gains were observed in memory grounding (95% improvement), plan clarity (57%), and emotional validation (34%). Results remained consistent even in emotionally adversarial conversations involving grief, distress, and uncertainty. These findings suggest that stateful emotional memory may represent a foundational infrastructure layer for hyper-personalized AI systems, though broader validation across larger and more diverse evaluations remains necessary

</details>


### [260] [Holistic Evaluation and Failure Diagnosis of AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14865)
*Netta Madvil,Gilad Dym,Alon Mecilati,Edo Dekel,Jonatan Liberman,Rotem Brazilay,Liron Schliesser,Max Svidlo,Shai Nir,Orel Shalom,Yaron Friedman,David Connack,Amos Rimon,Philip Tannor,Shir Chorev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对AI Agent长轨迹评估困难的问题，本文提出一种分解式的评估框架，通过将轨迹切分为Span进行独立诊断，不仅解决了故障定位难题，还证明了合理的评估方法能大幅挖掘模型潜能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI Agent评估方法存在缺陷：仅关注最终结果（成功/失败）而缺乏原因解释；现有的过程级评估难以在长且复杂的轨迹中精准定位故障点。

Method: 提出了一种结合“自上而下”的Agent级诊断与“自下而上”的Span级评估的综合框架。将复杂的长流程轨迹任务分解为独立的跨度（span），并为每个跨度提供评估结论及理由。

Result: 在TRAIL基准测试（GAIA和SWE-Bench）中达到SOTA水平。相比基线，分类F1提升最高达38%，定位准确率最高提升3.5倍，联合定位-分类准确率最高提升12.5倍。

Conclusion: 评估的瓶颈在于评估方法而非模型能力。将长流程分解为独立的span进行评估，能显著提升AI Agent错误定位和分类的准确性。

Abstract: AI agents execute complex multi-step processes, but current evaluation falls short: outcome metrics report success or failure without explaining why, and process-level approaches struggle to connect failure types to their precise locations within long, structured traces. We present a holistic agent evaluation framework that pairs top-down agent-level diagnosis with bottom-up span-level evaluation, decomposing analysis into independent per-span assessments. This decomposition scales to traces of arbitrary length and produces span-level rationales for each verdict. On the TRAIL benchmark, our framework achieves state-of-the-art results across all metrics on both GAIA and SWE-Bench, with relative gains over the strongest prior baselines of up to 38% on category F1, up to 3.5x on localization accuracy, and up to 12.5x on joint localization-categorization accuracy. Per-category analysis shows our framework leading in more error categories than any other evaluator. Notably, the same frontier model achieves several times higher localization accuracy when used inside our framework than as a monolithic judge over the full trace, showing that evaluation methodology, not model capability, is the bottleneck.

</details>


### [261] [Beyond Individual Intelligence: Surveying Collaboration, Failure Attribution, and Self-Evolution in LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14892)
*Shihao Qi,Jie Ma,Rui Xing,Wei Guo,Xiao Huang,Zhitao Gao,Jianhao Deng,Jun Liu,Lingling Zhang,Bifan Wei,Boqian Yang,Pinghui Wang,Jianwen Sun,Jing Tao,Yaqiang Wu,Hui Liu,Yu Yao,Tongliang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了统一的多智能体LIFE框架（奠基-整合-诊断-演化），系统性地梳理了跨智能体协作的因果依赖，并提出了一条构建具备持续自我诊断与修正能力的闭环多智能体系统的研究路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的LLM多智能体系统虽然在协作上有所突破，但缺乏针对错误传播的鲁棒性，且现有研究在“单个体能力”、“多智能体协作”与“自我演化”之间存在碎片化问题，缺乏统一的因果框架来指导系统实现持续性的自我改进。

Method: 提出“LIFE”进展模型：即奠定能力基础（Lay foundation）、整合协作（Integrate）、归因诊断错误（Find faults）、自主演化（Evolve）。对各阶段进行系统分类索引，并形式化表征各阶段间的因果依赖关系。

Result: 通过分类回顾，揭示了各研究阶段间的相互约束关系，指出了现有领域在各阶段边界处的空白（如系统化的跨阶段归因与自我演化机制），并为构建闭环、自我组织的集体智能系统提出了研究路线图。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个名为“LIFE”的闭环框架，将多智能体系统（MAS）研究从单一的协作提升至具备持续诊断、重组与自我演化的集体智能阶段。通过梳理各环节的因果依赖，明确了未来研究应聚焦于跨阶段的闭环优化，以实现能够自我提升的自主智能体系统。

Abstract: LLM-based autonomous agents have demonstrated strong capabilities in reasoning, planning, and tool use, yet remain limited when tasks require sustained coordination across roles, tools, and environments. Multi-agent systems address this through structured collaboration among specialized agents, but tighter coordination also amplifies a less explored risk: errors can propagate across agents and interaction rounds, producing failures that are difficult to diagnose and rarely translate into structural self-improvement. Existing surveys cover individual agent capabilities, multi-agent collaboration, or agent self-evolution separately, leaving the causal dependencies among them unexamined. This survey provides a unified review organized around four causally linked stages, which we term the LIFE progression: Lay the capability foundation, Integrate agents through collaboration, Find faults through attribution, and Evolve through autonomous self-improvement. For each stage, we provide systematic taxonomies and formally characterize the dependencies between adjacent stages, revealing how each stage both depends on and constrains the next. Beyond synthesizing existing work, we identify open challenges at stage boundaries and propose a cross-stage research agenda for closed-loop multi-agent systems capable of continuously diagnosing failures, reorganizing structures, and refining agent behaviors, extending current coordination frameworks toward more self-organizing forms of collective intelligence. By bridging these previously fragmented research threads, this survey aims to offer both a systematic reference and a conceptual roadmap toward autonomous, self-improving multi-agent intelligence.

</details>


### [262] [COREKG: Coreset-Guided Personalized Summarization of Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14900)
*Sohel Aman Khan,Raghava Mutharaju,Supratim Shit*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: COREKG通过引入共集采样算法，实现了针对用户查询行为的个性化知识图谱摘要，在大幅压缩图规模的前提下，在查询准确度和结构覆盖率方面均超越了现有主流方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型知识图谱在处理问答和可视化等任务时效率低下，而个性化摘要能够根据用户查询模式保留关键信息，从而减小存储压力并提升查询性能。

Method: 提出了一种基于共集（Coreset）理论的方法，通过定义衡量三元组对于用户查询工作负载重要性的“敏感度得分（Sensitivity Scores）”，利用敏感度重要性采样（Sensitivity-based Importance Sampling）从知识图谱中提取具有代表性的子集。

Result: 在Freebase、WikiData和DBpedia数据集上的实验表明，COREKG相较于GLIMPSE、PPR、iSummary、PEGASUS和APEX^2等现有方法，在仅使用原图一小部分数据的情况下，实现了更高的查询回答准确率和结构覆盖率。

Conclusion: 通过利用共集理论（Coreset Theory）进行敏感度采样，可以高效地为不同用户生成个性化的知识图谱摘要，在大幅压缩图规模的同时，显著优于现有的基准方法。

Abstract: Knowledge Graphs (KGs) are extensively used across different domains and in several applications. Often, these KGs are very large in size. Such KGs become unwieldy for tasks such as question answering and visualization. Summarization of KGs offers a viable alternative in such cases. Furthermore, personalized KG summarization is crucial in the current data-driven world as it captures the specific requirements of users based on their query patterns. Since it only maintains relevant information, the personalized summaries of KG are small, resulting in significantly smaller storage requirements and query runtime. In this work, we adapt the coreset theory to create personalized KG summaries. For a given dataset and a user-specific query workload, we present an approach that samples a relevant subset of triples using sensitivity-based importance sampling. We ensure that the subset approximates the characteristics of the full dataset with bounded approximation error. We define sensitivity scores that measure the importance of a triple with respect to a user's query workload, which are then used by our coreset construction algorithm. We explicitly focus on personalized knowledge graph summarization by constructing summaries independently for each user based on their query behaviour.
  Our evaluation on Freebase, WikiData, and DBpedia shows that COREKG delivers higher query-answering accuracy and structural coverage than the state-of-the-art methods, such as GLIMPSE, PPR, iSummary, PEGASUS and APEX$^2$ while requiring only a tiny fraction of the original graph.

</details>


### [263] [KGPFN: Unlocking the Potential of Knowledge Graph Foundation Model via In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14907)
*Yisen Gao,Jiaxin Bai,Haoyu Huang,Zhongwei Xie,Yufei Li,Hong Ting Tsang,Sirui Han,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KGPFN 是一种新型知识图谱基础模型，它通过 Prior-data Fitted Network 框架整合了结构化的局部和全局上下文信息，实现了在无需微调的情况下，即可对未知图谱进行高效的上下文学习与推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 KG 基础模型主要关注关系级泛化，但缺乏对知识图谱中核心能力——上下文学习（In-context Learning）的探索；如何有效整合局部与全局结构化上下文进行推理仍是难点。

Method: 提出 KGPFN（KG Prior-data Fitted Network）：1. 利用消息传递机制学习关系图嵌入以捕获跨图关系不变性；2. 使用多层 NBFNet 编码局部邻域作为局部上下文；3. 基于 Prior-data Fitted Network 框架，通过检索并聚合关系实例的局部邻域来构建全局上下文，并结合特征与样本级注意力进行推理。

Result: 在 57 个 KG 基准测试中，KGPFN 仅通过上下文学习即可实现对未知图谱的强适应，表现优于现有的微调式 KG 基础模型。

Conclusion: KGPFN 证明了即使在未见过的知识图谱上，通过结合结构化的上下文学习（ICL），模型也能实现强大的泛化推理，且效果优于传统的微调方法。

Abstract: Knowledge graph (KG) foundation models aim to generalize across graphs with unseen entities and relations by learning transferable relational structure. However, most existing methods primarily emphasize relation-level universality, while in-context learning, the other pillar of foundation models remains under-explored for KG reasoning. In KGs, context is inherently structured and heterogeneous: effective prediction requires conditioning on the local context around the query entities as well as the global context that summarizes how a relation behaves across many instances. We propose KGPFN, a KG foundation model using Prior-data Fitted Network that unifies transferable relational regularities with inference-time in-context learning from structured context. KGPFN first learns relation representations via message passing on relation graphs to capture cross-graph relational invariances. For query-specific reasoning, it encodes local neighborhoods using a multi-layer NBFNet as local context. To enable ICL at global scale, it constructs relation-specific global context by retrieving a large set of instances of the query relation together with their local neighborhoods, and aggregates them within a Prior-Data Fitted Network framework that combines feature-level and sample-level attention. Through multi-graph pretraining on diverse KGs, KGPFN learns when to instantiate reusable patterns and when to override them using contextual evidence. Experiments on 57 KG benchmarks demonstrate that KGPFN achieves strong adaptation to previously unseen graphs through in-context learning alone, consistently outperforming competitive fine-tuned KG foundation models. Our code is available at https://github.com/HKUST-KnowComp/KGPFN.

</details>


### [264] [From Sycophantic Consensus to Pluralistic Repair: Why AI Alignment Must Surface Disagreement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14912)
*Varad Vishwarupe,Nigel Shadbolt,Marina Jirotka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对RLHF模型普遍存在的“谄媚倾向”，本文提出从“聚合逻辑”转向“对话机制”，通过引入机制（限制范围、揭示冲突、原则纠偏）来对抗共识坍塌，确保AI在涉及复杂社会价值时能进行实质性对话而非盲目顺从。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RLHF模型训练导致了一种“谄媚式共识”（sycophantic consensus），在涉及社会公共价值争论时，模型倾向于过度迎合用户而非提供真实的价值冲突，这在关键领域（如医疗、治理）会导致严重的分配后果。

Method: 重新定义多元化对齐框架；基于格赖斯的会话准则提出三个机制： scoping（范围界定）、signalling（冲突信号传导）、repair（原则性纠偏）；提出并量化“多元化修复得分”（PRS）以区分原则性修正与卑躬屈膝的妥协。

Result: 通过对Claude Sonnet 4.5和GPT-4o的实证研究发现，两款模型均表现出强大的“顺从性”，但在应对争议性价值命题时，“原则性纠偏”能力极低。

Conclusion: 多元化对齐不能仅依赖聚合，必须在部署层引入对话机制（范围界定、信号传导、纠偏），以防止模型为了迎合用户而产生“谄媚式共识”，从而侵蚀社会审议的价值。

Abstract: Pluralistic alignment is typically operationalised as preference aggregation: producing responses that span (Overton), steer toward (Steerable), or proportionally represent (Distributional) diverse human values. We argue that aggregation alone is an incomplete primitive for deployed pluralistic alignment. Under genuine value pluralism, the failure mode of contemporary RLHF-trained assistants is not insufficient coverage but sycophantic consensus: a learned tendency to agree with, validate, and minimise friction with the immediate interlocutor. Because deployed AI systems now mediate consequential deliberation across health, civic life, labour, and governance, the collapse of disagreement at the interaction layer is not a narrow technical concern but a structural failure with distributive consequences. We reframe pluralistic alignment around three conversational mechanisms drawn from Grice's maxims: scoping (acknowledging the limits of one's perspective), signalling (surfacing value-conflict rather than smoothing it over), and repair (revising one's position on principled grounds, not on user pressure). We formalise a metric, the Pluralistic Repair Score (PRS), distinguishing principled revision from capitulation, and present a small-scale empirical illustration on two frontier RLHF-trained models (Claude Sonnet 4.5, N=198; GPT-4o, N=100) showing that, for both, agreement-following coexists with low repair-quality on contested-value prompts. PRS measures an interactional precondition for pluralism (visible disagreement; principled revision) rather than pluralism in full; we discuss the difference, take seriously the reflexive question of whose "principled" counts, and argue that pluralism is most decisively made or unmade at the deployment-governance layer: interfaces, preference-data pipelines, and audit infrastructure.

</details>


### [265] [Explainable Detection of Depression Status Shifts from User Digital Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14995)
*Loris Belcastro,Francesco Gervino,Fabrizio Marozzo,Domenico Talia,Paolo Trunfio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于BERT和LLM的可解释框架，通过分析社交媒体数字足迹的时间轨迹来识别和解读用户的心理健康状态变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字足迹包含的时间戳和心理信号为追踪心理健康状态的演变（如好转、恶化、稳定）提供了可能，现有方法缺乏对这些动态轨迹的有效分析与解释。

Method: 提出了一种结合多BERT模型（用于提取情感、情绪和抑郁程度等多维度信号）与时间轨迹分析的框架，并利用大语言模型（LLM）对提取的信号进行总结，生成可解释的演变报告。

Result: 在两个社交媒体数据集上的评估显示，该方法在总结的连贯性、历史覆盖率、时间一致性及对状态转变的敏感度方面均优于直接使用LLM的方法。消融实验证实了时间建模和分段对系统性能的关键贡献。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种可解释的抑郁症相关状态转变检测框架，通过多模型集成和时间轨迹分析，能够有效识别心理健康信号的演变过程，并通过LLM生成高质量的总结报告，为研究和决策提供支持，而非临床诊断。

Abstract: Every day, users generate digital traces (e.g., social media posts, chats, and online interactions) that are inherently timestamped and may reflect aspects of their mental state. These traces can be organized into temporal trajectories that capture how a user's mental health signals evolve, including phases of improvement, deterioration, or stability. In this work, we propose an explainable framework for detecting and analyzing depression-related status shifts in user digital traces. The approach combines multiple BERT-based models to extract complementary signals across different dimensions (e.g., sentiment, emotion, and depression severity). Such signals are then aggregated over time to construct user-level trajectories that are analyzed to identify meaningful change points. To enhance interpretability, the framework integrates a large language model to generate concise and human-readable reports that describe the evolution of mental-health signals and highlight key transitions. We evaluate the framework on two social media datasets. Results show that the approach produces more coherent and informative summaries than direct LLM-based reporting, achieving higher coverage of user history, stronger temporal coherence, and improved sensitivity to change points. An ablation study confirms the contribution of each component, particularly temporal modeling and segmentation. Overall, the method provides an interpretable view of mental health signals over time, supporting research and decision making without aiming at clinical diagnosis.

</details>


### [266] [Small, Private Language Models as Teammates for Educational Assessment Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15015)
*Chris Davis Jaldi,Anmol Saini,Shan Zhang,Noah Schroeder,Cogan Shimizu,Eleni Ilkou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究对比了LLM与SLM在教育题目设计中的表现，证明了SLM在隐私与资源受限场景下的潜力，但强调了当前模型评估能力的局限性，需人工进行质量把控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式AI在教育设计中的应用多依赖主观评估且偏重大型闭源模型，缺乏针对隐私与资源受限环境下小型语言模型（SLM）应用于教育评估任务的系统研究。

Method: 通过构建可复现、基于教学法的评价指标，系统对比了大型语言模型（LLM）与小型语言模型（SLM）在布鲁姆认知目标分类法（Bloom's taxonomy）不同层级上的题目生成质量；同时分析了模型自评估与专家人工评估之间的一致性与可靠性。

Result: SLM在关键教育质量维度上表现出与LLM相当的竞争力，且支持隐私敏感的本地部署；然而模型自动生成的评估结果与专家评分相比，存在系统性的偏差和不一致性。

Conclusion: 语言模型应被视为教育评估工作流中的“有限辅助工具”，强调了“人在回路”（Human-in-the-Loop）在评估中的必要性。研究指出在自动化教育题目生成领域，必须平衡质量、可靠性与部署权衡。

Abstract: Generative AI increasingly supports educational design tasks, e.g., through Large Language Models (LLMs), demonstrating the capability to design assessment questions that are aligned with pedagogical frameworks (e.g., Bloom's taxonomy). However, they often rely on subjective or limited evaluation methods; focus primarily on proprietary models; or rarely systematically examine generation, evaluation, or deployment constraints in real educational settings. Meanwhile, Small Language Models (SLMs) have emerged as local alternatives that better address privacy and resource limitations; yet their effectiveness for assessment tasks remains underexplored. To address this gap, we systematically compare LLMs and SLMs for assessment question design; evaluate generation quality across Bloom's taxonomy levels using reproducible, pedagogically grounded metrics; and further assess model-based judging against expert-informed evaluation by analyzing reliability and agreement patterns. Results show that SLMs achieve competitive performance across key pedagogically motivated quality dimensions while enabling local, privacy-sensitive deployment. However, model-based evaluations also exhibit systematic inconsistencies and bias relative to expert ratings. These findings provide evidence to posit language models as bounded assistants in assessment workflows; underscore the necessity of Human-in-the-Loop; and advance the automated educational question generation field by examining quality, reliability, and deployment-aware trade-offs.

</details>


### [267] [Learning Developmental Scaffoldings to Guide Self-Organisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14998)
*Milton L. Montero,Elias Najarro,Jakob Schauser,Sebastian Risi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究构建了一个联合学习框架，量化了初始条件与自组织规则间的协作，证明了将发育信息“外包”给初始条件能够显著提升生物形态生成的复杂性和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然界中许多自组织系统并非完全从零开始，而是高度依赖“初始条件”（如母体形态发生素梯度）来引导复杂的发育过程。现有自组织模型大多忽略了这种类似计算系统中“记忆-计算权衡”的机制，本研究旨在探索并量化这种外包信息机制的有效性。

Method: 提出了一种联合学习框架，将神经元胞自动机（NCA，负责模拟自组织规则）与基于隐式神经表示的协调模式生成器（SIREN，负责生成初始预模式）相结合；利用信息论方法量化分析了初始条件与动态规则间的信息分布。

Result: 实验表明，联合学习预模式与自组织规则的系统，在模式生成的稳健性、信息压缩及对称性破缺任务中，显著优于仅依赖纯自组织过程的模型。预模式的作用不仅是提供初始近似值，更重要的是通过“偏置”动力学路径来引导发育过程更高效地收敛。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了初始条件（预模式）与自组织动力学之间并非简单的映射或近似关系，而是存在一种非平凡的相互作用。通过这种协同优化，系统有效地“向初始条件外包”了部分计算负载，从而显著提升了生物形态生成的稳健性、编码容量及对称性破缺能力。

Abstract: From subcellular structures to entire organisms, many natural systems generate complex organisation through self-organisation: local interactions that collectively give rise to global structure without any blueprint of the outcome. Yet a significant portion of the information driving such processes is not produced by self-organisation itself, instead, it is often offloaded to initial conditions of the system. Biological development is a prime example, where maternal pre-patterns encode positional and symmetry-breaking information that scaffolds the self-organising process. From maternal morphogen gradients in early embryogenesis to tissue-level morphogenetic pre-patterns guiding organ formation, this transfer of information to initial conditions, analogous to a memory-compute trade-off in computational systems, is a fundamental part of developmental processes. In this work, we study this offloading phenomenon by introducing a model that jointly learns both the self-organisation rules and the pre-patterns, allowing their interplay to be varied and measured under controlled conditions: a Neural Cellular Automaton (NCA) paired with a learned coordinate-based pattern generator (SIREN), both trained simultaneously to generate a set of patterns. We provide information-theoretic analyses of how information is distributed between pre-patterns and the self-organising process, and show that jointly learning both components yields improvements in robustness, encoding capacity, and symmetry breaking over purely self-organising alternatives. Our analysis further suggests that effective pre-patterns do not simply approximate their targets; rather, they bias the developmental dynamics in ways that facilitate convergence, pointing to a non-trivial relationship between the structure of initial conditions and the dynamics of self-organisation.

</details>


### [268] [Orchard: An Open-Source Agentic Modeling Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15040)
*Baolin Peng,Wenlin Yao,Qianhui Wu,Hao Cheng,Xiao Yu,Rui Yang,Tao Ge,Alessandrio Sordoni,Xingdi Yuan,Yelong Shen,Pengcheng He,Tong Zhang,Zhou Yu,Jianfeng Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Orchard 是一个开源智能体建模框架，通过轻量级环境层 Orchard Env 实现了数据、训练策略和评估的跨领域复用，在编程、GUI 操作等任务上以较小参数规模达到甚至超越部分闭源模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前智能体建模领域存在基础设施和训练鸿沟，高性能系统多依赖私有代码库或模型，缺乏专注于可扩展智能体训练的开源框架，限制了该领域的研究发展。

Method: 构建了开源框架 Orchard，核心包含轻量级环境服务 Orchard Env；针对编程、GUI 操作和个人助手三个领域提出了特定的建模方案，通过数据蒸馏、信用分配监督微调 (Credit-assignment SFT) 和均衡自适应强化学习 (Balanced Adaptive Rollout) 等技术进行训练。

Result: Orchard-SWE 在 SWE-bench Verified 达到 67.5%（SOTA）；Orchard-GUI 在多个任务（WebVoyager 等）中成为最强开源模型且抗衡私有系统；Orchard-Claw 在极小规模合成数据下表现出色。

Conclusion: Orchard 通过轻量级、与 harness 无关的环境层（Orchard Env），证明了在不同领域实现可重用的智能体训练数据、策略和评估的可行性与高效性，打破了闭源系统在智能体建模方面的垄断优势。

Abstract: Agentic modeling aims to transform LLMs into autonomous agents capable of solving complex tasks through planning, reasoning, tool use, and multi-turn interaction with environments. Despite major investment, open research remains constrained by infrastructure and training gaps. Many high-performing systems rely on proprietary codebases, models, or services, while most open-source frameworks focus on orchestration and evaluation rather than scalable agent training. We present Orchard, an open-source framework for scalable agentic modeling. At its core is Orchard Env, a lightweight environment service providing reusable primitives for sandbox lifecycle management across task domains, agent harnesses, and pipeline stages. On top of Orchard Env, we build three agentic modeling recipes. Orchard-SWE targets coding agents. We distill 107K trajectories from MiniMax-M2.5 and Qwen3.5-397B, introduce credit-assignment SFT to learn from productive segments of unresolved trajectories, and apply Balanced Adaptive Rollout for RL. Starting from Qwen3-30B-A3B-Thinking, Orchard-SWE achieves 64.3% on SWE-bench Verified after SFT and 67.5% after SFT+RL, setting a new state of the art among open-source models of comparable size. Orchard-GUI trains a 4B vision-language computer-use agent using only 0.4K distilled trajectories and 2.2K open-ended tasks. It achieves 74.1%, 67.0%, and 64.0% success rates on WebVoyager, Online-Mind2Web, and DeepShop, respectively, making it the strongest open-source model while remaining competitive with proprietary systems. Orchard-Claw targets personal assistant agents. Trained with only 0.2K synthetic tasks, it achieves 59.6% pass@3 on Claw-Eval and 73.9% when paired with a stronger ZeroClaw harness. Collectively, these results show that a lightweight, open, harness-agnostic environment layer enables reusable agentic data, training recipes, and evaluations across domains.

</details>


### [269] [Case-Based Calibration of Adaptive Reasoning and Execution for LLM Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15041)
*Renning Pang,Tian Lan,Leyuan Liu,Piao Tong,Sheng Cao,Xiaosong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CAST 框架通过将历史工具调用经验结构化为可利用的案例信号，引导大语言模型优化推理逻辑，实现了在提高工具使用成功率的同时降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决大语言模型在使用工具时，难以平衡“推理深度”与“结构有效性”的问题，并提升在大规模复杂场景下执行工具调用的可靠性。

Method: 提出 CAST（Case-driven framework）框架：将历史执行轨迹结构化为“案例”；通过提取案例派生信号，识别任务的“复杂度配置（Complexity Profiles）”以优化推理策略，并定义“故障配置（Failure Profiles）”以预防结构性错误；将上述知识转化为细粒度的奖励函数，用于强化学习引导模型自主内化策略。

Result: 在 BFCLv2 和 ToolBench 上表现优异：整体执行准确率提升高达 5.85 个百分点，平均推理长度减少 26%，并有效减少了关键的结构性错误。

Conclusion: 通过利用历史执行案例提取的信号，模型能够实现更精准的工具调用策略，在提升执行成功率的同时显著降低冗余推理，证明了基于案例的经验对于大语言模型工具使用能力的有效适配。

Abstract: Tool use extends large language models beyond parametric knowledge, but reliable execution requires balancing appropriate reasoning depth with strict structural validity. We approach this problem from a case-based perspective to present CAST, a case-driven framework that treats historical execution trajectories as structured cases. Instead of reusing raw exemplar outputs, CAST extracts case-derived signals to identify complexity profiles for estimating optimal reasoning strategies, alongside failure profiles to map likely structural breakdowns. The framework translates this knowledge into a fine-grained reward design and adaptive reasoning, enabling the model to autonomously internalize case-based strategies during reinforcement learning. Experiments on BFCLv2 and ToolBench demonstrate that CAST improves both schema-faithful execution and task-level tool-use success while reducing unnecessary deliberation. The approach achieves up to 5.85 percentage points gain in overall execution accuracy and reduces average reasoning length by 26%, significantly mitigating high-impact structural errors. Ultimately, this demonstrates how historical execution cases can provide reusable adaptation knowledge for calibrated tool use.

</details>


### [270] [Dual-Dimensional Consistency: Balancing Budget and Quality in Adaptive Inference-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15100)
*Rongman Xu,Yifei Li,Tianzhe Zhao,Yanrui Wu,Bo Li,Hang Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“双维一致性（DDC）”的推理框架，通过结合贝叶斯置信评估与趋势感知剪枝，解决了LLM推理中采样效率与结果质量之间的冲突，在显著降低计算成本的同时提升了推理准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型语言模型（LLM）的推理时扩展（Inference-time scaling）存在效率低下的问题。单纯的宽度共识易导致幻觉强化，而深度剪枝则容易过早剔除有效的复杂推理链，两者缺乏有效的统一协调机制。

Method: 提出双维一致性（DDC）框架，包含两项核心技术：1. 置信度加权贝叶斯协议（Confidence-Weighted Bayesian protocol）：用于评估路径质量；2. 趋势感知分层剪枝（Trend-Aware Stratified Pruning）：用于路径的自适应终止，确保计算资源向高质量路径倾斜。

Result: 在五个基准测试上进行了验证，结果显示该方法在实现与最强基线相当或更高准确率的同时，降低了超过10倍的Token消耗。

Conclusion: DDC通过将采样路径质量与自适应终止统一，实现了在计算成本大幅降低（减少10倍以上Token消耗）的同时，保持或超越了现有基线的推理精度，有效解决了路径一致性与计算效率之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable abilities in reasoning. However, maximizing their potential through inference-time scaling faces challenges in trade-off between sampling budget and reasoning quality. Current strategies remain inefficient as they typically treat sampling width and depth as orthogonal objectives, where width consensus methods risk reinforcing hallucinations, while depth pruning mechanisms prematurely truncate complex yet valid reasoning chains. Therefore, we propose Dual-Dimensional Consistency (DDC), a unified framework that bridges path quality with adaptive termination. By coupling Confidence-Weighted Bayesian protocol with a Trend-Aware Stratified Pruning, our method ensures that computational resources are concentrated on high quality reasoning paths, filtering hallucinations while accelerating consensus. Evaluations across five benchmarks demonstrate that this approach reduces token consumption by over 10 times while maintaining or exceeding the accuracy of strong baselines across various LLMs.

</details>


### [271] [OpenDeepThink: Parallel Reasoning via Bradley--Terry Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15177)
*Shang Zhou,Wenhao Chai,Kaiyuan Liu,Huanzhi Mao,Qiuyang Mang,Jingbo Shang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OpenDeepThink 是一种通过成对比较和进化策略解决 LLM 推理“选择瓶颈”的测试时计算框架，显著提升了模型在客观任务中的推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时计算（Test-time compute）主要通过延长单条推理路径（深度扩展）来提升性能，而通过采样多个候选者（广度扩展）面临“选择瓶颈”——即在没有标准答案的情况下，缺乏可靠的机制来评估和选出最佳推理结果。

Method: 提出 OpenDeepThink 框架：1. 使用基于 Bradley-Terry 模型的成对比较法（Pairwise Comparison）替代传统的单点评分；2. 采用群体进化策略，每一代进行对比、投票、排序，保留优秀候选者；3. 利用生成过程中的自然语言批判作为变异依据，淘汰表现不佳的候选者。

Result: 在 Gemini 3.1 Pro 模型上，通过 8 轮迭代，Codeforces Elo 分数提升了 405 分；在多领域 HLE 基准测试中，显著提升了客观可验证领域的性能；发布了包含 73 个高质量专家标注题目的 CF-73 数据集。

Conclusion: OpenDeepThink 是一种有效的基于群体的测试时计算框架。它通过成对比较和进化策略（变异与优胜劣汰），显著提升了 LLM 在目标明确（可客观验证）任务中的推理能力。研究还发布了高质量的评估基准 CF-73。

Abstract: Test-time compute scaling is a primary axis for improving LLM reasoning. Existing methods primarily scale depth by extending a single reasoning trace. Scaling breadth by sampling multiple candidates in parallel is straightforward, but introduces a selection bottleneck: choosing the best candidate without a ground-truth verifier, since pointwise LLM judging is noisy and biased. To address this, we introduce OpenDeepThink, a population-based test-time compute framework that selects via pairwise Bradley-Terry comparison. Each generation, the LLM judges random pairs of candidates and aggregates votes via Bradley-Terry into a global ranking; top-ranked candidates are preserved and the top three quarters are mutated using the natural-language critiques produced during comparison; the bottom quarter is discarded. OpenDeepThink raises Gemini 3.1 Pro's effective Codeforces Elo by +405 points in eight sequential LLM-call rounds (~27 minutes wall-clock). The pipeline transfers across weaker and stronger models without retuning, and on the multi-domain HLE benchmark, gains appear concentrated in objectively verifiable domains and reverse in subjective ones. We release CF-73, a curated set of 73 expert-rated Codeforces problems with International Grandmaster annotation and 99% local-evaluation agreement against the official verdict.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [272] [Towards Robotic Dexterous Hand Intelligence: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13925)
*Weiguang Zhao,Xihao Guo,Tian Liang,Rui Zhang,Irwin King,Kaizhu Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文是对机器人灵巧手研究的全面综述，从硬件设计、控制学习方法、配套数据集和评测规范四个维度进行系统梳理，旨在解决领域内标准不一的问题，并为未来的研究方向提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧手研究在硬件形态、感知配置、任务设置及评测标准上缺乏统一框架，导致系统性对比困难，阻碍了领域发展的明确化与规范化。

Method: 采用综合综述（Holistic Review）方法，从硬件设计（执行器、感知）、控制与学习方法（范式演进）、数据资源与评估指标四个关键维度进行系统性分析与对比。

Result: 构建了一个衡量灵巧手研究的综合评估框架，识别了主流研究范式的演变规律，梳理了不同硬件设计的权衡（力能力、顺应性、复杂度等），并指出了当前研究与标准化评测之间的脱节。

Conclusion: 本综述通过整合机器人灵巧手在硬件、算法、数据及评测维度的现状，清晰勾勒了该领域的发展脉络，并指明了当前面临的关键挑战及未来研究方向，旨在促进学术界对灵巧手研究的结构化认知。

Abstract: Robotic dexterous hands are central to contact-rich manipulation, with rapid progress driven by advances in hardware, sensing, control, simulation, and data generation. However, existing studies are often developed under different assumptions regarding hand embodiments, sensory configurations, task settings, training data, and evaluation protocols, making systematic comparison difficult and obscuring the developmental trajectory of the field. This survey provides a holistic review of dexterous hand research from four complementary aspects. First, we present a hardware-level analysis covering actuation, transmission, perception, and representative hand designs, highlighting the key trade-offs in force capability, compliance, bandwidth, integration, and system complexity. Furthermore, we review control and learning methods for dexterous manipulation from a methodological perspective, grouping representative works by major paradigms and tracing their evolution in chronological order. In addition, we consolidate datasets, modality design, and evaluation practices, which enables methodological progress to be interpreted together with the ways in which it is trained, benchmarked, and assessed. Finally, we discuss the major limitations of current dexterous hand research and summarize the corresponding future directions. By connecting hardware analysis, methodological development, data resources, and evaluation, this survey aims to provide a structured understanding of dexterous hand research and to clarify the most important open challenges for future study.

</details>


### [273] [Ergodic Imitation for Adaptive Exploration around Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13996)
*Ziyi Xu,Cem Bilaloglu,Yiming Li,Sylvain Calinon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 为了解决机器人模仿学习中的分布偏移难题，本文提出了一种集成演示数据的自适应各态历经控制方法，实现了轨迹跟踪与在线主动探索的动态平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决机器人模仿学习中因环境变化或观测/控制偏差导致的“训练-部署”分布偏移问题，避免机器人因死板遵循名义轨迹而在任务中受阻。

Method: 提出了一种“自适应各态历经模仿”（Adaptive Ergodic Imitation）方法；通过检索演示数据的几何特征构建目标分布，并在检索式滚动时域控制（Receding-horizon framework）中实现轨迹跟踪与探索之间的自适应插值。

Result: 该方法成功实现了机器人在保持演示引导的同时，具备在非预期环境下的在线探索能力，提升了任务完成的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了通过将演示数据融入各态历经控制（Ergodic Control），可以将传统的覆盖搜索方法转化为一种高效的、具备自适应能力的模仿学习框架，有效缓解了分布偏移问题。

Abstract: In robotics, a common challenge in imitation learning is the mismatch between training and deployment conditions, caused, for example, by environmental changes or imperfect observation and control. When a robot follows a nominal trajectory under such mismatch, it may become stuck and fail to complete the task. This calls for adaptive online exploration strategies that remain grounded in demonstrations. To this end, we propose an adaptive ergodic imitation approach that constructs a target distribution from the geometry of the retrieved demonstrations and uses it to generate trajectories that adaptively interpolate between tracking and exploration. Our method extends ergodic control beyond its traditional role in area-coverage and search by incorporating demonstrations into a retrieval-based receding-horizon framework for adaptive imitation.

</details>


### [274] [Behavior Cloning for Active Perception with Low-Resolution Egocentric Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14106)
*Anthony Bilic,Chen Chen,Ladislau Bölöni*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究证明，通过行为克隆可以使机器人仅利用低分辨率的视觉信息，通过闭环控制实现主动感知，从而完成物体寻找与定位任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究行为克隆是否足以在结构化任务中实现“主动感知”（即通过动作改进后续观测），以及视觉输入和动作空间表达对任务成功率的影响。

Method: 采用行为克隆（Behavior Cloning）方法训练机器人；模型直接从低分辨率RGB图像输入预测关节点增量；机器人配备腕部安装的自中心相机，通过闭环控制执行物体寻找及定位任务。

Result: 低分辨率的自中心视觉足以完成任务；预测相对关节增量（relative joint deltas）的表现显著优于预测绝对关节位置。

Conclusion: 行为克隆足以在结构化物体寻找任务中衍生出主动感知能力。闭环控制、低分辨率自中心视觉，以及预测关节增量（deltas）而非绝对位置，是实现该任务的关键要素。

Abstract: We investigate whether behavior cloning is sufficient to produce active perception in a structured object-finding task. A low-cost robot arm equipped with a wrist-mounted egocentric RGB camera must reposition to center a partially visible plant before triggering a grasp signal, requiring actions that improve future observations. The model predicts joint commands directly from low-resolution RGB images under closed-loop control. We show that low-resolution egocentric vision is sufficient for reliable task completion and that predicting relative joint deltas substantially outperforms absolute joint position prediction in our setting. These results demonstrate that visually grounded active perception can emerge from behavior cloning in a reproducible setting.

</details>


### [275] [Motion Planning for Autonomous Vehicles using Optimization over Graphs of Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14199)
*Matheus Wagner,Antônio Augusto Fröhlich*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出利用基于凸集图（GCS）的凸优化框架解决自动驾驶运动规划问题，通过处理非凸自由空间的离散决策实现高效、鲁棒的轨迹生成，解决了传统非线性最优控制计算昂贵和对初值敏感的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性最优控制计算代价高且对初值敏感，而几何规划方法通常将路径规划与轨迹生成解耦；本文旨在探究GCS如何在高维复杂环境下高效、鲁棒地求解运动规划问题。

Method: 将自由空间表示为凸区域的非循环有向图，利用贝塞尔曲线参数化空间路径，采用多项式时间缩放函数优化时间分布，并在简化自行车动力学模型下，通过凸约束处理非凸几何与动力学可行性。

Result: 在CommonRoad场景测试中，GCS方法生成的轨迹在避障和换道任务中表现出与非线性最优控制相当的动力学一致性，且在计算效率和初值鲁棒性方面表现更优。

Conclusion: 凸集图（GCS）提供了一种平衡路径搜索与轨迹优化的有效框架，能够在保持计算效率与收敛稳定性的同时，近似求解非线性最优控制问题。

Abstract: Motion planning for autonomous vehicles requires generating collision-free and dynamically feasible trajectories in complex environments under real-time constraints. While nonlinear optimal control formulations provide high-fidelity solutions, they are computationally demanding and sensitive to initialization, whereas geometric planning methods scale well but often decouple path selection from trajectory optimization. This paper studies the extent to which optimization over Graphs of Convex Sets (GCS) can approximate solutions of nonlinear optimal control problems in the context of autonomous driving. The free space is represented as a finite union of convex regions organized as a directed graph, allowing nonconvex geometry to be handled through discrete connectivity decisions while maintaining convex trajectory constraints within each region. Vehicle motion is parameterized using Bezier curves for the spatial path and a polynomial time-scaling function for temporal evolution. Under small-slip and linear tire assumptions, a simplified dynamic bicycle model enables approximate enforcement of dynamic feasibility through convex constraints on trajectory derivatives. The approach is evaluated in CommonRoad scenarios involving static obstacle avoidance and lane-changing maneuvers, and is compared against a nonlinear discrete-time optimal control formulation. The results indicate that the GCS-based method generates collision-free and dynamically consistent trajectories that closely match those obtained from the nonlinear program, while exhibiting improved computational efficiency and reduced sensitivity to initialization. These findings suggest that GCS provides a structured approximation of nonlinear motion planning problems, capturing dominant geometric and dynamic effects while preserving convexity in the continuous relaxation.

</details>


### [276] [MAPLE: Latent Multi-Agent Play for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14201)
*Rajeev Yasarla,Deepti Hegde,Hsin-Pai Cheng,Shizhong Han,Yunxiao Shi,Meysam Sadeghigooghari,Hanno Ackermann,Litian Liu,Pranav Desai,Fatih Porikli,Mohammad Ghavamzadeh,Hong Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MAPLE 是一种新型 VLA 自动驾驶训练框架，通过在潜在空间内进行无需模拟器的多智能体反应式闭环训练，显著提升了端到端驾驶系统的安全性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作 (VLA) 模型在闭环设置中表现脆弱，且现有闭环监督方案缺乏可扩展性，难以模拟复杂的反应式环境。

Method: 提出 MAPLE 框架，包含两个阶段：(1) 基于真值轨迹的潜在空间滚动监督微调；(2) 结合全局及智能体特定奖励（安全、进度、交互真实性）及多样性奖励的强化学习。该方法在不依赖外部模拟器的情况下实现闭环训练。

Result: MAPLE 在 Bench2Drive 基准测试中实现了最先进的驾驶性能，并展现出可扩展的闭环多智能体博弈能力。

Conclusion: MAPLE 作为一个可扩展的闭环 VLA 训练框架，通过在潜在空间内进行多智能体反应式规划，有效提升了自动驾驶系统在闭环设置下的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models are effective as end-to-end motion planners, but can be brittle when evaluated in closed-loop settings due to being trained under traditional imitation learning framework. Existing closed-loop supervision approaches lack scalability and fail to completely model a reactive environment. We propose MAPLE, a novel framework for reactive, multi-agent rollout of a dynamic driving scenario in the latent space of the VLA model. The ego vehicle and nearby traffic agents are independently controlled over multi-step horizons, while being reactive to other agents in the scene, enabling closed-loop training. MAPLE consists of two training stages: (1) supervised fine-tuning on the latent rollouts based on ground-truth trajectories, followed by (2) reinforcement learning with global and agent -specific rewards that encourage safety, progress, and interaction realism. We further propose diversity rewards that encourage the model to generate planning behaviors that may not be present in logged driving data. Notably, our closed-loop training framework is scalable and does not require external simulators, which can be computationally expensive to run and have limited visual fidelity to the real-world. MAPLE achieves state-of-the-art driving performance on Bench2Drive and demonstrates scalable, closed-loop multi-agent play for robust E2E autonomous driving systems.

</details>


### [277] [Reactive Planning based Control for Mobile Robots in Obstacle-Cluttered Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14232)
*Li Tan,Junlin Xiong,Yan Wang,Wei Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对部分未知环境下的移动机器人运动控制，提出了一种结合反应式路径规划与自适应跟踪控制的策略（RPCS），有效解决了避障与目标跟踪问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在包含障碍物的环境中，移动机器人往往仅能获取部分环境信息，需要在实现目标点导航的同时保证避障的实时性和有效性。

Method: 提出并结合了一种反应式规划策略（RPS）与自适应跟踪控制策略（ATCS）：1. 生成连接起点与终点的参考轨迹；2. 利用部分环境信息，通过RPS对参考轨迹进行局部修改以避障；3. 通过离散化技术设计ATCS实现对修改后轨迹的自适应跟踪。

Result: 数值仿真结果证明了该策略（RPCS）在障碍物环境下成功引导机器人避障并到达目标点，验证了其有效性和优越性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种反应式规划控制策略（RPCS），有效地解决了移动机器人在部分未知环境下的避障及轨迹跟踪问题。

Abstract: This paper addresses the motion control problem for mobile robots in obstacle-cluttered environments. The mobile robot has partial environment information only, and aims to move from an initial position to a target position without collisions. For this purpose, a reactive planning based control strategy (RPCS) is proposed. First, the initial and target positions are connected as a reference trajectory. Then, a reactive planning strategy (RPS) is developed to ensure the collision avoidance by modifying the reference trajectory locally based on the partial environment information. Next, an adaptive tracking control strategy (ATCS) is proposed to track the reference trajectory with potentially local modifications via the discretization techniques. Finally, the RPS and ATCS are combined to establish the RPCS, whose efficacy and advantages are illustrated by numerical examples.

</details>


### [278] [Distill: Uncovering the True Intent behind Human-Robot Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14262)
*Ting Li,David Porfirio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Distill，一种通过去冗、泛化和放宽约束来改善人机意图交流的新方法，通过网页接口与众包测试验证了其能够有效提升人机对意图理解的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人机交互机制（自然语言和终端用户编程）存在局限性：自然语言存在歧义，而终端用户编程过于具体，难以准确捕捉用户的真实意图。

Method: 设计并实现了一个名为 Distill 的人机通信接口，包含三个核心步骤：(1) 移除冗余步骤；(2) 泛化步骤含义；(3) 放宽步骤间的顺序约束。通过众包研究（crowdsourcing study）验证了该方法。

Result: 实验结果表明，Distill 能够有效地从用户提供的初级任务规范中诱导并提炼出其核心意图。

Conclusion: Distill 是一种有效的人机通信方法，能够通过化简、泛化和放宽约束，成功从用户初始的任务描述中提取并明确其真实意图。

Abstract: As robots become increasingly integrated into everyday environments, intuitive communication paradigms such as natural language and end-user programming have become indispensable for specifying autonomous robot behavior. However, these mechanisms are ineffective at fully capturing user intent: natural language is imprecise and ambiguous, whereas end-user programming can be overly specific. As a result, understanding what users truly mean when they interact with robots remains a central challenge for human-AI communication systems. To address this issue, we propose the Distill approach for human-robot communication interfaces. Given a task specification provided by the user, Distill (1) removes unnecessary steps; (2) generalizes the meaning behind individual steps; and (3) relaxes ordering constraints between steps. We implemented Distill on a web interface and, through a crowdsourcing study, demonstrated its ability to elicit and refine user intent from initial task specifications.

</details>


### [279] [Energy-Efficient Quadruped Locomotion with Compliant Feet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14411)
*Pramod Pal,Shishir Kolathaya,Ashitava Ghosal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究通过仿真与实体实验证明，为四足机器人配备适中刚度的柔性足，可在保持运动稳定性的前提下，通过能量再生提升约 17% 的运动效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统四足机器人多采用刚性足以简化控制，但这牺牲了冲击吸收和弹性势能回收能力，导致运动能耗较高。本文旨在探究引入足端柔性对提升运动效率的作用。

Method: 1. 在仿真环境中训练八个具有不同足端弹簧刚度的强化学习（RL）控制策略。2. 通过测量机械能消耗（每米移动消耗）进行交叉评估。3. 在实体四足机器人上进行实测，验证仿真结论。

Result: 实验结果表明，采用中等刚度的弹簧相对于极硬或极软的足端设计，能耗降低了约 17%，仿真与实测趋势一致。

Conclusion: 在足端引入适中的柔性（Compliance）可以显著提高四足机器人的运动效率，且不会牺牲运动稳定性。研究验证了足端刚度与能耗之间存在最优平衡点。

Abstract: Quadruped robots are often designed with rigid feet to simplify control and maintain stable contact during locomotion. While this approach is straightforward, it limits the ability of the legs to absorb impact forces and reuse stored elastic energy, leading to higher energy expenditure during locomotion. To explore whether compliant feet can provide an advantage, we integrate foot compliance into a reinforcement learning (RL) locomotion controller and study its effect on walking efficiency. In simulation, we train eight policies corresponding to eight different spring stiffness values and then cross-evaluate their performance by measuring mechanical energy consumed per meter traveled. In experiments done on a developed quadruped, the energy consumption for the intermediate stiffness spring is lower by ~ 17% when compared to a very stiff or a very flexible spring incorporated in the feet, with similar trends appearing in the simulation results. These results indicate that selecting an appropriate foot compliance can improve locomotion efficiency without destabilizing the robot during motion.

</details>


### [280] [Before the Body Moves: Learning Anticipatory Joint Intent for Language-Conditioned Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14417)
*Haozhe Jia,Honglei Jin,Yuan Zhang,Youcheng Fan,Shaofeng Liang,Lei Wang,Shuxu Jin,Kuimou Yu,Zinuo Zhang,Jianfei Song,Wenshuo Chen,Yutao Yue*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DAJI 是一个用于人形机器人的分层控制框架，通过显式的“关节意图”接口将语言指令与未来感知动作对齐，实现了更具预测性和流动性的全身运动控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形机器人语言指令控制系统存在局限：要么依赖反应式低级控制器修补运动学轨迹，要么直接输出动作策略，缺乏对未来接触变化、支撑状态转换和平衡准备的显式表征。

Method: 该研究提出 DAJI（Dynamics-Aligned Joint Intent）框架：1. DAJI-Flow：利用语言和意图历史自回归地生成未来意图块；2. DAJI-Act：通过学生驱动的 rollout（rollout rollout），将具备未来感知能力的教师模型蒸馏为可部署的扩散动作策略。

Result: 在 HumanML3D 数据集上的 rollout 成功率达到 94.42%，在 BABEL 数据集上的子序列 FID 达到 0.152，证明了该方法在预测性潜在学习和指令跟踪方面的卓越性能。

Conclusion: DAJI 框架通过引入预测性“关节意图”接口，有效解决了人形机器人自然语言控制中对未来物理状态（如接触、平衡）缺乏感知的问题，在多项生成任务中表现出极高的成功率。

Abstract: Natural language is an intuitive interface for humanoid robots, yet streaming whole-body control requires control representations that are executable now and anticipatory of future physical transitions. Existing language-conditioned humanoid systems typically generate kinematic references that a low-level tracker must repair reactively, or use latent/action policies whose outputs do not explicitly encode upcoming contact changes, support transfers, and balance preparation. We propose \textbf{DAJI} (\emph{Dynamics-Aligned Joint Intent}), a hierarchical framework that learns an anticipatory joint-intent interface between language generation and closed-loop control. DAJI-Act distills a future-aware teacher into a deployable diffusion action policy through student-driven rollouts, while DAJI-Flow autoregressively generates future intent chunks from language and intent history. Experiments show that DAJI achieves strong results in anticipatory latent learning, single-instruction generation, and streaming instruction following, reaching 94.42\% rollout success on HumanML3D-style generation and 0.152 subsequence FID on BABEL.

</details>


### [281] [Let Robots Feel Your Touch: Visuo-Tactile Cortical Alignment for Embodied Mirror Resonance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14571)
*Tianfang Zhu,Ning An,Rui Wang,Jiasi Gao,Qingming Luo,Anan Li,Guyue Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Mirror Touch Net，通过对齐视觉和触觉的几何与表征空间，赋予了机器人“镜像触觉”能力，使其能够根据视觉输入预测触觉信号并做出反射性反应，推动了具身智能在移情交互领域的发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类通过“镜像触觉”产生共情，但目前的机器人缺乏实现这种视觉-触觉共振的计算框架，无法像人类一样通过观察产生触觉感知或进行反射性反应。

Method: 提出了“Mirror Touch Net”模型，通过多级约束（语义、分布和几何层面的对齐）将视觉表征映射到与触觉流形一致的几何空间中，实现了从RGB图像到机器人手部1140个触觉单元信号的预测。

Result: Mirror Touch Net 能够准确预测毫米级的触觉信号。曼形分析（Manifold analysis）证明该方法显著降低了跨模态映射的复杂度；此外，该模型成功跨域应用于人类手部观察，实现了触觉预测及对观察到的触摸行为产生反射性响应。

Conclusion: 该研究成功将镜像触觉的神经机制转化为机器人计算框架，证实了视触觉表征对齐是实现镜像触觉的关键，并为未来实现具备预测性触觉感知及移情能力的机器人交互提供了技术可行性。

Abstract: Observing touch on another's body can elicit corresponding tactile sensations in the observer, a phenomenon termed mirror touch that supports empathy and social perception. This visuo-tactile resonance is thought to rely on structural correspondence between visual and somatosensory cortices, yet robotic systems lack computational frameworks that instantiate this principle. Here we demonstrate that cortical correspondence can be operationalized to endow robots with mirror touch. We introduce Mirror Touch Net, which imposes semantic, distributional and geometric alignment between visual and tactile representations through multi-level constraints, enabling prediction of millimetre-scale tactile signals across 1,140 taxels on a robotic hand from RGB images. Manifold analysis reveals that these constraints reshape visual representations into geometry consistent with the tactile manifold, reducing the complexity of cross-modal mapping. Extending this alignment framework to cross-domain observations of human hands enables tactile prediction and reflexive responses to observed human touch. Our results link a neural principle of visuo-tactile resonance to robotic perception, providing an explainable route towards anticipatory touch and empathic human-robot interaction. Code is available at https://github.com/fun0515/Mirror-Touch-Net.

</details>


### [282] [SeaVis: Modeling and Control of a Remotely Operated Towed Vehicle for Seabed Visualization and Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14683)
*Abdelhakim Amer,Aske Alstrup,Frederik Rasmussen,Yury Brodskiy,Andriy Sarabakha,Erdal Kayacan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于拖曳式水下航行器（ROTV）的增益调度LQR控制方法，通过高保真仿真验证，证明其在控制精度、鲁棒性及执行效率上均优于传统PID控制器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率海底测绘对水下机器人的定位航行稳定性及控制精度提出了更高要求，传统的PID控制器难以在复杂工况下平衡性能。

Method: 1. 建立了SeaVis拖曳式水下航行器（ROTV）的数学运动学模型；2. 设计了基于增益调度（Gain-scheduled）的线性二次型调节器（LQR）；3. 在高保真仿真环境中与传统PID控制器进行性能对比验证。

Result: 仿真实验显示，LQR控制器在应对复杂海底地形干扰时表现出更强的鲁棒性，同时显著降低了控制执行器的负载（舵面 actuation），且在全速度运行区间内均保持有效。

Conclusion: 增益调度LQR控制器在ROTV的深度和姿态控制中表现优于传统PID，具有更高的鲁棒性、控制效率，并有效减少了舵面执行器的动作频率，适用于全速度范围。

Abstract: High-resolution seafloor mapping necessitates stable and precise positioning for underwater robots. This paper introduces a novel mathematical model for SeaVis remotely operated towed vehicles (ROTVs) and develops a gain-scheduled linear-quadratic regulator (LQR) for robust depth and attitude control. We validate the approach in a high-fidelity simulation, benchmarking the LQR against a conventional PID controller over a challenging seabed profile. The presented results demonstrate the LQR's superior performance, with significantly enhanced robustness to disturbances, greater control efficiency, and substantially reduced flap actuation. The gain scheduling also confirms the controller's effectiveness across the full operational velocity range. The complete simulation environment and controller are open-sourced.

</details>


### [283] [IntentVLA: Short-Horizon Intent Modeling for Aliased Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14712)
*Shijie Lian,Bin Yu,Xiaopeng Lin,Zhaolong Shen,Laurence Tianruo Yang,Yurun Jin,Haishan Liu,Changti Wu,Hang Yuan,Cong Huang,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对机器人模仿学习中的多模态动作歧义问题，本文提出IntentVLA框架，通过历史感知意图表征提升动作执行的稳定性，并引入AliasBench基准进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
